# ClipTroop (formerly VPlanner) — Project Handoff

Internal content-production dashboard for a YouTube team. Stack: **Next.js 15 (App Router) + Supabase (Postgres + Auth + Storage + Realtime) + Vercel**. Repo on GitHub (**eduardneague/cliptroop**, the owner's personal account, since Oct 5 2026; was viverrodev/vplanner; internal names, cookies, storage keys and the repo keep "vplanner"). Production URL: **`https://app.cliptroop.com`** since Oct 7 2026 (staging `https://staging.cliptroop.com`; vivplanner.vercel.app was removed from Vercel and from every platform's lists; the root cliptroop.com is for a website later). **Domain**: cliptroop.com bought Oct 5 2026 at **Hostico** (hostico.ro) with their **Start** cPanel hosting (1 year): DNS lives in cPanel → Zone Editor (Hostico nameservers), mailboxes in cPanel → Email Accounts; the app stays on Vercel (env vars only in Vercel). Name: **ClipTroop** since 1.9.2 (`APP_NAME`, `APP_SHORT_NAME`, `APP_DOMAIN` in lib/brand.ts), mascot still Clip.

## Infrastructure
- **Vercel**: one project, auto-deploys from `main`. Env vars scoped separately per environment (Production / Preview / Development) for: `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY`, `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` (secret, server-only), `GIPHY_API_KEY` (secret), `NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL`. Analytics (1.4): `SOCIAL_STATS_PLATFORMS` (comma list of youtube,instagram,tiktok that ALSO ask for stats permissions on connect; default `youtube`; add instagram/tiktok only once those permissions are on in their developer apps, or their sign-in refuses to start), optional `INSTAGRAM_GRAPH_VERSION` (default v23.0), `ANALYTICS_CURRENCY` (default USD). 1.19.0: optional `LIVE_MINUTES` (20, 30 or 60; unset = every 10 minutes): how often the live job reads every video's counts (a lighter load on Vercel Hobby). 1.23.0: optional `LANGUAGETOOL_URL` (your own LanguageTool server) or `LANGUAGETOOL_USERNAME` + `LANGUAGETOOL_API_KEY` (a LanguageTool account): writing suggestions then go through the app's /api/grammar instead of each browser asking LanguageTool's free service. 1.25.0: optional `NEXT_PUBLIC_MAX_LONG_VIDEO_MB` (a long video's edit, at most 3072; unset = `NEXT_PUBLIC_MAX_VIDEO_MB`; Supabase's global file size limit must allow it, so Pro for 3 GB). `CRON_SECRET` also protects `/api/cron/analytics`. `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL` (optional) pins the address used for platform return links and email links in production / on your computer; Vercel PREVIEWS (staging) ignore it and use their own address (`VERCEL_BRANCH_URL` for emails), since 1.5.0. Since 1.9.1: `STAGING_URL` (Preview scope, server-only) = staging's own domain, used by email links from the `staging` branch; and `canonicalRedirect()` (lib/supabase/middleware.ts) sends page loads (GET/HEAD, never /api) on a `*.vercel.app` host to NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL in production, or to STAGING_URL on the `staging` branch (VERCEL_GIT_COMMIT_REF), only when the target is https and not itself *.vercel.app (308, path + query kept). `/setup` (Settings → Account → App setup, developers only) lists every env var for the copy it runs on (set / missing, never values) with suggested values. Facebook Page analytics (1.6): `FACEBOOK_APP_ID` / `FACEBOOK_APP_SECRET` (the Meta app's own ID, not the Instagram app ID), optional `FACEBOOK_LOGIN_CONFIG_ID` (Facebook Login for Business configuration) and `FACEBOOK_GRAPH_VERSION` (default v23.0).
- **Outliers (1.27.0)**: optional `YOUTUBE_API_KEY` (a YouTube Data API v3 key in Google Cloud → APIs & Services → Credentials, restricted to that API; without it Outliers reads YouTube through the team's connected channel), `OUTLIERS_DAILY_UNITS` (default 4000 of YouTube's 10,000 daily units), `OUTLIERS_SEARCHES_PER_DAY` (90), `OUTLIERS_CRAWL_SEARCHES` (45), `OUTLIERS_PERSON_SEARCHES` (15), `OUTLIERS_TEAM_SEARCHES` (40), `OUTLIERS_STATS_PER_DAY` (5000), `OUTLIERS_STATS_DAYS` (30; up to 1095 only after accepting YouTube's derived metrics amendment), `OUTLIERS_EXPLORE` (0 = no Explore niches). 1.29.0: `OUTLIERS_EXPLORE_UNITS` (1200, at most half of the units) and `OUTLIERS_EXPLORE_SEARCHES` (6, at most half of the collector's searches): the Explore niches' own share, which a team's collecting can't use up. All listed on /setup.
- **Supabase**: two *separate* projects — `vplanner-staging` and `vplanner-production`. Every migration must be run on **both**, independently (since 1.8.0 the **Database** GitHub Action does it: push to `staging` → staging, push to `main` → production; see `scripts/db/README.md`). This has been a recurring source of bugs (schema drift between them) — always double-check which project you're looking at before running SQL or trusting a query result.
- **GitHub Actions (1.8.0)**: `.github/workflows/ci.yml` on every push / PR: `npm ci`, typecheck, lint, `npm test` (tests/*.test.ts via tsx), `next build` with stand-in env, and "migrations apply from scratch" (postgres:15 service + `supabase/tests/supabase-stub.sql` + `scripts/db/test-from-scratch.sh`, newest migration run twice). `.github/workflows/database.yml`: Supabase CLI `db push --db-url` (dry run first) on every push to `staging` / `main` (no paths filter: a push that only added the workflow never ran it), or by hand (target staging|production, action push|status|baseline). Secrets `STAGING_DB_URL`, `PRODUCTION_DB_URL` = the **Session pooler** connection strings (port 5432; runners have no IPv6, so not `db.<ref>.supabase.co`). Uses repository secrets only (no GitHub environments, so it works on a free private repo). Refuses to push unless 0001 is marked as run (baseline once per database: paste `scripts/db/baseline.sql` into the SQL editor, which creates `supabase_migrations.schema_migrations` and marks 0001–0062, or run the workflow with action `baseline` = `migration repair --status applied`). psql is installed on the runner if missing.
- **Alerts / status (1.8.0, reworked 1.9.8)**: env `DEVELOPER_EMAILS` (comma list; `ALERT_EMAILS` is the old name and still works; if neither is set: the owner of the FIRST team ever made). Developers open `/developer` and `/setup` and are the ONLY ones who get app-wide alerts (team owners don't any more), as notifications (in the app and push). Since 1.19.0 the EMAILS (alerts, "back to normal", test alerts, reports) go ONLY to `ALERTS_EMAIL` (optional env; else `NEXT_PUBLIC_CONTACT_EMAIL`, else hello@cliptroop.com: `alertsEmail()` in lib/errors.ts), never to the developer accounts' own addresses (owner's request, Oct 14 2026). Team emails (a failed post to the masters / schedulers, meeting reminders) are unchanged. `ALERT_ON_PREVIEW=1` to also alert from staging (alerts are otherwise production-only; errors are recorded everywhere). Public `/status` (levels only, hourly bars for 3 days, uptime, 7 days of incidents) + `/api/health` (200/503 JSON, for an uptime monitor such as UptimeRobot every 5 min). The bars come from pg_cron `vplanner-status` every 10 minutes (0067), which calls the app through the same Vault `posting_url` / `cron_secret` as the posting timer, so nothing new to set up in Supabase.
- **Phone app + push (1.9.0)**: env `NEXT_PUBLIC_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY` (65-byte P-256 public key, base64url) + `VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY` (32-byte d, base64url, secret) in all environments (same pair everywhere is fine; NEXT_PUBLIC_ is baked in at build, so redeploy after setting), optional `VAPID_SUBJECT` (mailto:…; else `mailto:` + NEXT_PUBLIC_CONTACT_EMAIL, else the app address). Settings → Notifications & app shows a "Make the keys" helper to the alert people while they're missing. Legal pages read `NEXT_PUBLIC_CONTACT_EMAIL` (must be set before the approvals) and `NEXT_PUBLIC_COMPANY_NAME` (optional, once there's a company).
- **TikTok in production** needs `TIKTOK_CLIENT_KEY` + `TIKTOK_CLIENT_SECRET` with Production ticked in Vercel (Oct 3 2026 production showed "Keys not added yet"), the production return address in the TikTok app's Login Kit redirect URIs, and while the TikTok app isn't approved: its Sandbox keys with the account added as a sandbox target user.
- **Email**: custom SMTP via Resend (`smtp.resend.com`, port 465, user `resend`, password = a Resend API key: CLIPTROOP_SUPABASE_PRODUCTION / _STAGING) configured in Supabase Auth settings, on the verified domain **cliptroop.com** (the owner's personal Resend account since Oct 5 2026; viverro.com is no longer used). Sender: `hello@cliptroop.com` / ClipTroop. The app's own emails: Vercel `RESEND_API_KEY` (CLIPTROOP_APP_PRODUCTION on Production, CLIPTROOP_APP_STAGING on Preview + Development) and `EMAIL_FROM` = `ClipTroop <alerts@cliptroop.com>` (no inbox needed to send). This replaced Supabase's default email sender, which has a strict rate limit unsuitable for real use. Since 1.9.1 the Auth email templates (Invite user, Reset Password, Magic Link, Confirm signup, Change Email Address) are generated by `lib/auth-emails.ts` (brand name/colour, table layout, no gradients) and pasted by the owner from `/setup` into each Supabase project (Authentication → Emails); every link is `{{ .SiteURL }}/welcome?token_hash={{ .TokenHash }}&type=…`, so each project's **Site URL must be that copy's address** (no trailing slash). Until they're pasted, Supabase's default templates keep working through the old `#access_token` flow.
- **Account creation model**: accounts are created **only** via the Supabase dashboard directly (Authentication → Users → Invite user), done personally by the project owner — there is no in-app UI that can create a new account. 1.9.1 flow: invite email → `/welcome` (public; Clip, what happens next, a button that calls `verifyOtp({ token_hash, type })` only when pressed, so mail scanners can't use the link up and it works on any device) → `/set-password` (4 steps: password with show/hide + checklist, saved first; name / username / photo / bio, prefilled; install + notifications via `InstallApp` / `PushSettings`, skippable; all set with pending team invites counted). Expired or used links (`?error=expired`, or Supabase's `#error_code=otp_expired` caught by AuthHashHandler) get a plain explanation; recovery → "Get a new reset link" opens `/login?reset=1`. This was a deliberate architecture decision: account creation and team membership are fully decoupled. Team membership is a separate, lighter-weight in-app request/accept flow (see below).

## Migration history (run in order, on both projects)
- **0001**: full initial schema — profiles, teams, team_members, member_roles, tags, member_tags, long_video_projects, project_titles, project_thumbnails, project_assignees, project_comments, video_review_pins, package_entries, notifications. Enums: `role_type`, `pipeline_stage`. RLS on everything. Helper functions: `is_team_member()`, `is_team_master()`, `member_has_stage_access()`, `role_allows_stage()`.
- 0002: profiles.email, thumbnail storage bucket
- 0003: long_video_projects.updated_by
- 0004: role_colors table
- 0005: comment deletion RLS
- 0006: teams.logo_url, notifications.stage, connected_accounts table, team-logos bucket
- 0007: connected_accounts unique index
- 0008: comment_attachments table + bucket
- 0009: realtime enabled on project_comments
- 0010: project deletion (RLS + app-level, with storage cleanup)
- 0011: user settings — username column, avatars bucket
- 0012: public profiles — teams_visible privacy flag, `get_visible_teams()` security-definer function
- 0013: team_invites table (decoupled from account creation) — **note: the original RLS policy here for accepting an invite had a real, hard-to-diagnose bug**, see 0018
- 0014: allow user deletion — several `ON DELETE` FKs to profiles had no cascade behavior specified, blocking account deletion entirely. Fixed to `SET NULL` (content survives; only the "who did this" link clears). Deliberately **not** applied to `teams.owner_id` — a team must always have a real owner.
- 0015: **critical fix** — `notifications` table had SELECT/UPDATE RLS policies since day one but was *missing an INSERT policy entirely*. Every notification-creating feature in the app (mentions, stage changes, invites, everything) had been silently failing at the database level. Fixed with an open `with check (true)` insert policy — the app's own server actions control who actually gets notified, not RLS.
- 0016: invite expiry (1 hour) + resend support
- 0017: realtime enabled on notifications
- 0018: **fixes the team_invites accept-flow RLS bug from 0013.** The original policy used an inline `EXISTS` subquery against `team_invites` (itself RLS-protected) inside another table's `WITH CHECK` clause — this produced "new row violates row-level security policy" on a genuinely valid accept, confirmed via extensive direct-SQL diagnosis (the policy logic, the data, and `auth.uid()` all checked out correct in isolation; the real request still failed). Switched to a `SECURITY DEFINER` helper function (`has_pending_invite()`), the same proven pattern already used by `is_team_master`.
- 0019: temporary debug function (`get_auth_uid_debug`) used during the above diagnosis — harmless, still in schema, never cleaned up
- 0020: ownership transfer as a request/accept flow (mirrors team_invites exactly) — `ownership_transfer_requests` table
- 0021: structured notifications — added `kind` (discriminator) and `metadata` (jsonb) columns to notifications, so the bell can render real avatars, bold names, and colored role pills instead of plain text. `body` stays as a fallback.

- 0022: **security hardening** (post-audit). Removed user UPDATE access on team_invites / ownership_transfer_requests (invitees could previously rewrite team_id/proposed_roles and join any team as Master); dropped the 0018 self-join policies + `has_pending_invite()`; users can no longer INSERT notifications (server-only now); column-level grants on profiles (no email edits), teams (name/logo/color only; any Master may edit them), notifications (is_read only); triggers: only a Master changes a project's stage, projects can't change team/creator, owner can't be kicked and is always Master, only the OWNER grants/removes Master; assignees must be active same-team members with a role covering the stage; comment attachments must be in the project's folder or a Giphy URL; fixed FKs that blocked account deletion (updated_by, ownership request users); `team_invites_no_master` check; dropped `get_auth_uid_debug()`.

- 0023: **performance** — indexes on every filtered/joined/sorted column (FKs weren't indexed); SELECT policies rewritten to set-based helpers (`my_team_ids()`, `my_project_ids()`, `my_master_team_ids()`, `my_teammate_member_ids()`, all SECURITY DEFINER) evaluated once per query instead of per row; `FOR ALL` master policies split into insert/update/delete. Permissions unchanged (verified with the local test suite).

- 0024: **global search** — `pg_trgm` (in the `extensions` schema) + trigram GIN indexes on profile usernames/names, project titles, alternate titles and team names; `global_search(q, max_results)` RPC is SECURITY INVOKER (RLS decides what's findable), returns `{people, projects, teams, master_teams}` in one call. People carry `member_of`/`invited_to` (only for teams the searcher masters) for the Invite flow. Email only matches when the query contains "@" and is never returned.

- 0025: Ideate notes open to every team member (other stages unchanged).
- 0026: **short videos module** — drops `connected_accounts` (posting stays manual, no social APIs). `team_counters` + `next_team_number()` give race-safe per-team entry numbers (`long_video_projects.entry_number` backfilled by creation order; shorts numbered on insert). Tables `short_videos` (stage enum `script → editing → review → ready → posted`, planned_date, editor_member_id, platforms[], file_link, caption, review_note), `short_video_posts` (one row per platform posted, poster/time forced server-side), `short_video_events` (history written only by triggers). `short_guard_update` trigger enforces field-level rules per role; `short_recompute_posted()` flips ready⇄posted from the posts (uses the `vp.short_system` transaction flag). `notifications.short_id`. `global_search()` returns `shorts` and supports `#12` number lookups. Realtime on shorts + posts.

- 0027: **shorts scheduling queue** — team settings (`shorts_per_day` 1–10, `shorts_weekends`, `timezone`, default editor/reviewer/scheduler member ids, column-granted to masters). `short_videos.schedule_mode` ('auto' = date calculated by the queue; 'pinned' = chosen date, queue flows around it), `queue_position`, `reviewer_member_id`, `scheduler_member_id`. `short_queue_plan()` / `recalc_short_queue()` (row-locked per team) re-date auto shorts on create/delete/pin/unpin/stage change/settings change; posted or partly-posted shorts and past-dated ones never move. `next_short_slot(team)` = preview for the create form. `move_short(id, ±1)` (master) swaps queue neighbours. Guard: posted shorts are stage-locked for everyone (un-mark a platform to reopen); reviewer may approve / request changes; setting planned_date pins, clearing it (or schedule_mode='auto') returns to the queue.

- 0028: **continuous numbering** — `entry_number` now = position in schedule order, always 1…N with no gaps. Shorts: planned_date, queue_position (`renumber_shorts`, called at the end of every `recalc_short_queue`). Long videos: expected_date (undated last), created_at (`renumber_long_videos`, trigger on insert/delete/expected_date change). Two-pass (negative → positive) update keeps the unique (team, number) constraint happy. Numbers are therefore NOT permanent IDs — link by `id`, never by number.

- 0029: **smart scheduling** — `pin_kind` on fixed dates ('anchor' = Auto shorts after it continue from its date [default]; 'oneoff' = holds its own slot, queue ignores it). `team_day_limits` (0–10 per day, masters; any day incl. today) override the per-day default/weekend rule. `teams.shorts_roll_forward` (default on): unposted Auto shorts from past days re-enter the queue; `refresh_short_queue(team)` is called on shorts page loads and only recalculates once per team-day (`teams.shorts_queue_day`). `move_short` works for every unlocked short: swaps list places; a fixed short takes the date of the slot it moves into. `queue_position` is now double precision (fixed shorts are slotted in by date). Posting/un-posting re-plans. Deleting returns the vacated day so the toast can offer "Keep <day> at N".

- 0030: **scheduling rules** — `set_day_limit_keeping(team, day, limit, keep[])` (master): lowering a day lets you choose which shorts stay; dropped Auto shorts flow on, dropped fixed shorts stay fixed on the next day with room, posted ones can't be dropped. **One queue start per team**: only one active (upcoming, unposted) "Start queue from here" short; a second one is refused until the first is released (trigger `zz_short_one_queue_start`); new fixed dates default to "Just this one" while one exists; `current_queue_start(team)` for the UI; `recalc_short_queue` also tidies any system-made duplicates.
- UI copy rule: no dashes in user-facing text; short, plain sentences. DB error messages pass through `plain()` in shorts actions.

- 0031: **short types + Frame.io gate** — `short_type` ('filler' | 'sponsorship' | 'big'; new shorts take `teams.default_short_type`), `caption_enabled` (off by default; normal shorts use the automatic caption). `is_frameio_link()`: Editing → In review is refused unless `file_link` is a Frame.io (frame.io / f.io, https) link. UI: type colors (sponsorship = blue, big = gold) as a left edge + faint tint in the table; right column follows the stage (Review box → Changes requested box → Post to → Posted); Final file saves only on Confirm.

- 0032: **scheduler permissions** — creating shorts AND long videos: master or scheduler (role id `publisher`); scripters write scripts only. Title/type/platforms/caption and dates of Auto shorts: master or scheduler at any stage. Fixed dates (change, release, kind) and starting the queue: master only; a scheduler fixing an Auto short's date gets "Just this one". UI mirrors via `canEditSchedule` / `canStartQueue` in shortPermissions.

- 0033: **schedulers assign people** (editor/reviewer/scheduler) like masters. Short page UI: only stage-relevant cards remain (stage box, Final file on Editing/Ready/Posted, Posted, Activity); everything editable lives in the Settings window (`ShortSettingsDialog`: people, post date, type, post to, final file, caption), opened from the header Settings button or ⋯ → Edit in the table. `components/ui/dialog.tsx` is the shared popup (Esc / X / outside click, focus trap + return). Type shows as an S/B badge at the end of table rows.

- 0034: **scripts** — `scripts` table (one per short, `long_video_id` ready for long videos): TipTap JSON `content` + `content_text` + `word_count`, `version` bumped by trigger; team always derived from the video. Read: teammates. Write: master or scripter (`can_edit_script`), any stage. Saves are compare-and-swap on `version` (`saveScript` action) so nobody overwrites a newer save. Images in the public `script-images` bucket under `<team>/<script>/…`, upload/delete by masters and scripters of that team only.
- Script editor: `modules/scripts/components/script-editor.tsx` (TipTap 3: StarterKit, Highlight multicolor, TextAlign, TaskList/Item, custom `ScriptImage` node with align/width/resize/view/download, CharacterCount, Placeholder). Full-page route `/shorts/[id]/script`; `ScriptCard` on the short page. Autosave ~1s after typing, Ctrl/⌘+S, unsaved-changes warning, conflict banner; only real changes count (trailing empty paragraphs ignored). Export: DOCX via `docx` (lazy-loaded, images converted to PNG, highlights kept) and PDF via print CSS. Spoken length at 150 wpm.

- 0035: **schedulers reorder** — `move_short` allows masters and schedulers.
- Word export: the `docx` library is NOT bundled (Turbopack dev fails on it: "'super' keyword unexpected here"). `scripts/copy-vendor.mjs` (postinstall) copies `node_modules/docx/dist/index.iife.js` to `public/vendor/docx.iife.js` (gitignored); `export-docx.ts` loads it at runtime on first export and uses only type imports. Middleware skips `/vendor/` so it caches.
- Editor: images go in at a block boundary (`blockRange`), never inside text; image toolbar has a drag grip + Move up/down; toolbar popovers render in a portal. Pages view = A4-width paper with page-break guides (page height follows width, √2 ratio).
- Shorts table columns step down by width (sidebar-aware): md = #/title/status/count/⋯, lg + editor, xl + platform icons, 2xl + planned.

- 0036: **writers + scheduler day limits + animation setting** — `short_scripters` (short_id, team_member_id): only a short's writers and masters can edit its script (`can_edit_script_row` / `can_edit_script_id`, also for script images by folder `<team>/<script>/…`). `teams.default_short_scripter_member_id` is added to every new short (trigger). Masters and schedulers add/remove writers (`setShortWriter`, Settings window → Writers). Day exceptions (`team_day_limits`, `set_day_limit_keeping`) now masters AND schedulers. `profiles.animations_enabled`.
- Motion: `app/(dashboard)/template.tsx` (.motion-page rise-in per navigation), `.motion-stagger` on lists, softer modal/toast keyframes, button press-in. Animations use fill-mode "backwards" so no identity transform is left behind (it would trap position:fixed popups). Off via `html[data-motion="off"]` (set pre-paint from localStorage `vp-motion`, synced from the profile by `MotionSync`) or prefers-reduced-motion. Settings → Preferences → Animations.

- Naming: the people who may edit a short's script are **scripters** everywhere (code: `scripterIds`, `setShortScripter`, `ScripterPicker`, `ScriptersButton`; DB: `short_scripters`, `teams.default_short_scripter_member_id`). Picked when creating a short (pre-filled with the team default), in the Settings window, and from the script page top bar.
- Short page below lg: the two columns use `display: contents` so each card is ordered on its own (action box, final file, script, activity last; script first in the Script stage).
- 0037: **animations = Match device / On / Off** (`profiles.animations_enabled` null / true / false). `html[data-motion="on"]` overrides the device's prefers-reduced-motion; "off" forces none. Page entrance animates `.motion-page > *` so content replacing the loading skeleton animates too.

- 0038: **in-app video review** (replaces Frame.io). Private bucket `review-videos` (`<team>/<short>/<uuid>.<ext>`, storage RLS: read = teammates, upload = master/scheduler/the short's editor via `can_upload_short_video`, delete = master). `short_video_versions` (numbered by trigger; file must exist in storage and be in the short's folder), `short_video_comments` (time-pinned notes, replies inherit time, author-only edit, anyone resolves). `short_videos.approved_version_id` set on Review → Ready (never by hand), `keep_media` (master/scheduler). Editing → In review needs a version (Frame.io link = legacy fallback) via `short_review_gate`.
- Upload: browser → Supabase resumable (tus-js-client, 6 MB chunks) → `registerVersion`. Playback: 3-hour signed URLs (`getPlaybackUrl`). Size limit `NEXT_PUBLIC_MAX_VIDEO_MB` (staging 50 on the free plan). Long videos (1.25.0, 0089) have the same review (`kind="long"`, bucket `long-review-videos`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_MAX_LONG_VIDEO_MB` up to 3072). Review page `/shorts/[id]/review` (`modules/review/*`), `VideoCard` on the short page.
- Cleanup (since 1.11.0): the nightly job `{"job": "cleanup"}` (pg_cron `vplanner-cleanup`, 0070; `lib/media-cleanup.ts`) deletes every version of a posted short after the team's `media_keep_days` (7/14/21/30, default 14), unless keep_media; `/api/cron/cleanup-media` runs it by hand. (Before: Vercel Cron, 2 days after posting.) NOTE: shorts ↔ versions now have TWO FK paths; embeds must use `short_video_versions!short_video_versions_short_id_fkey`.

- 0039: **connected social accounts** (Phase A of auto-posting). `social_accounts` (one per team+platform): tokens AES-256-GCM encrypted server-side (`lib/social/crypto.ts`, key `SOCIAL_TOKEN_KEY`), token columns not grantable to `authenticated` (column privileges) and NOT in Realtime; writes only via service role after `requireSocialManager` (master/scheduler). `oauth_states` (single-use, 15 min, PKCE for Google). `social_audit_log` (masters/schedulers read). OAuth: `/api/social/[platform]/connect` + `/callback` (`lib/social/providers.ts`: YouTube via Google, Instagram API with Instagram Login, TikTok v2). Refresh on use (`getAccessToken`) + daily upkeep in the cleanup cron (`refreshExpiring`); failures → needs_reconnect + master notification. Public `/privacy` and `/terms` (`app/(legal)`). Cleanup now deletes ALL versions 2 days after posting (unless Keep).
- Decision: all posting jobs run inside Supabase (pg_cron → secret-protected endpoint, step-by-step state machine), no third-party job service, with status/history shown in-app.

- 0040: **automatic posting** (Phase B). `social_posts` (one active per short+platform; `state` = upload sessions, NOT client-readable; writes server-only), `social_post_events` (in-app history), `teams.post_time_*`. `claim_social_posts()` (skip-locked, 3-min lease). Timer: pg_cron every minute → `run_posting_tick()` → pg_net POST to Vault `posting_url` with Vault `cron_secret` (only when something is due). Endpoint `/api/cron/posting` → `runDuePosts()` (`lib/social/worker.ts`): per-step state machine, saves after each step, retries 1/2/5/15/30 min, then failed + notify. Publishers in `lib/social/publishers/`: YouTube (resumable 8 MB, native publishAt, final status check), Instagram (REELS container from 2 h signed URL → poll → media_publish), TikTok (FILE_UPLOAD, ≤64 MB single else 10 MB pieces, creator_info re-check, status poll), Facebook since 1.12.0 (Page Reels, see 0071). Published → `short_video_posts` upsert (auto Posted). UI: `app/(dashboard)/shorts/[id]/schedule-panel.tsx` (TikTok UX guideline fields), actions in `schedule-actions.ts`; "Run due posts now" hidden on production. Cleanup never deletes while a post is open.

- 0041: `teams.default_youtube_description` (Team → Short videos), auto-filled into every YouTube schedule. Schedule panel moved to the main column; the old manual Posted card was removed (manual "Mark as posted" lives in the panel for unconnected platforms; Facebook is marked automatically when Instagram publishes). Captions default to the short's title; times in 15-min steps; "made for kids" defaults to No with an explanatory Change dialog; scheduling confirmation is a per-platform summary dialog.
- YouTube monetization: not settable through the standard Data API (needs CMS/MCN access). Shorts uploaded via the API are treated like Studio uploads and are eligible for Shorts revenue sharing on YPP channels. Long-video ad settings (mid-rolls) to be verified with a test upload when long-video posting is built.

- 0042: **posting health**. `posting_runs` (every worker run: timer/manual, claimed, ms, error). `posting_health(team)` (masters/schedulers) returns Vault secret presence, pg_cron's last run (cron.job_run_details), pg_net's last responses (net._http_response), last runs. `lib/social/health.ts` turns it into plain findings (passwords don't match = 401 JSON, Vercel protection = 401/403 HTML, wrong address = 404, stopped timer, missing secrets). `/posting` page (sidebar): Health + Needs attention / In progress / Upcoming / Published with links. Schedule confirmation runs live pre-flight checks (`checkBeforeScheduling`) and only enables Schedule when all pass. Per-platform step timeline on the short page (uses the recorded `step`), with late alarm (>3 min past start), links (YouTube Studio / Watch / Instagram post or profile / TikTok Studio) and the full log.

- 0043: `posting_call()` (single place the timer/ping calls the app; adds Vault `vercel_bypass` as `x-vercel-protection-bypass` if present), `posting_ping(team)` → "Test the timer" on /posting. Health shows result age; stale failures are warnings with a retest hint.
- YouTube scopes now upload + force-ssl (replaces readonly): `changePostTime` edits publishAt on YouTube after upload; Cancel after upload deletes the video on YouTube. Accounts connected before need one reconnect (checked via `scopes`).
- Short page: `?view=<stage>` looks back at an earlier step (banner + Back to current; stage controls hidden). Script card only in script/editing; uploads only while editing (short page + review page). Posting data fetched in parallel; panel live-updates via `getPostingState` (4 s while moving, 30 s otherwise, paused when hidden) instead of full-page refresh. Facebook merged into the Instagram card; manual IG mark also marks Facebook.
- Version: package.json 1.0.0; next.config bakes NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_VERSION / _CHANNEL (P prod, E preview/staging, D local) / _COMMIT; `lib/version.ts`. EXPERIMENTAL badge in the top bar only on E. Version line at the bottom of Team settings only.

- Posting rules/alerts: every post must be >= 15 min ahead (`MIN_LEAD_MINUTES` server-side in schedulePosts/changePostTime; picker only offers allowed times). Failures notify ALL masters + schedulers in-app, and by email via Resend (`lib/email.ts`, RESEND_API_KEY secret + EMAIL_FROM; skipped if unset; free plan 3,000/mo, 100/day). Activity shows "VPlanner" (app icon) for actor-less events; automatic posts read "posted it on". Posting page sections are <details> dropdowns (Needs attention red when non-empty); Connected accounts History is a dropdown. Notification rows show a short (vertical) / long (horizontal) type icon.

- Step bar is `app/(dashboard)/shorts/[id]/stage-tracker.tsx` (no box: dot under the step you're on, halo when looking back). Uploads: `VersionUploader` stays "Updating…" until its router.refresh transition ends; `VideoCard` (client) shows "vN uploaded · updating…" and re-refreshes up to 5× until the new version is in the props; the review workspace does the same. YouTube missing the force-ssl scope → actions return `reconnect: true`; Change time shows an inline Reconnect notice; Team cards show a notice + Reconnect (any connected account can be reconnected). Toasts sit at the top on phones.

- **Calendar** (`app/(dashboard)/calendar/`): month (6-week grid) / week / agenda (phones default; current month = rolling 5 weeks from today). Default month: current if it has items, else next upcoming item's month, else latest. Shorts (planned_date) + long videos (expected_date), per-platform post status dots, Sponsor/Big edge, overdue in red. Drag-and-drop (masters/schedulers) → confirm dialog → `updateShortDetails` (planned_date + pin_kind: master keeps anchor, else oneoff) or `updateExpectedDate`; blocked for posted items, past days, and shorts with scheduled posts. Phones: "Move" (date picker) in the agenda.

- Kind colors everywhere: `--short` / `--long` tokens, defaults orange / light blue; teams override them (Team → Colors, `teams.short_color/long_color`, applied by `kindColorCss()` in the dashboard layout, lighter shade in dark mode) (light + dark), Tailwind `short`/`long`, `components/ui/kind-icon.tsx`; used in calendar, notifications, search, sidebar + bottom bar. Never red for kinds.
- Calendar v2: data from `listShorts` (same as the Shorts table) + `getShortSettings` + `listDayLimits` + social_posts. Month (full width, TODAY label, capacity dots: orange planned, red over), week/agenda as full-width day rows with `DayLimitControl`, day panel (click the date), quick view (click an item: where it posts, stage, editor, Move to…, Earlier/Later, Open). Drag onto a day = move; onto a same-day short = reorder (repeated `moveShortInQueue`); onto another day's item = move. Full-day moves offer "Allow N shorts that day" (`setShortDayLimit`). All changes confirmed. `revalidateShort()` now also refreshes /calendar + /posting; long-video actions refresh /videos + /calendar.

- Icons: `ShortsIcon` / `VideoIcon` are solid portrait / landscape cards with an evenodd cut-out triangle whose centroid sits at (12,12). Search thumbnails use them. Calendar: Sponsor/Big cards get a full gradient + edge; same-day drag shows a live reorder preview (list is position:relative; landing slot from cursor vs offsetTop, so no flicker; dragged card slides + fades, others shift one slot); phones get a compact month (markers) with the tapped day's list below.

- 0046: `move_short_auto(short, day)` keeps a moved AUTO short automatic (queue_position = short_position_for_date, then recalc; returns where it landed). Calendar: auto shorts get "Keep it automatic" (default) vs "Pin it to this date"; posted = teal check (flow "done" colour); "This month only" toggle; month/year picker (click the title); skeleton while navigating; long-video dot; capacity dots in the shorts colour.

- 0044/0045: **long-video flow**. Stages: Ideate → Research → Script → Film → Edit → Review (new enum value) → Package → Post (`publish`) → Posted (`done`). Step RPCs (role + current-step checked, each writes a line into the step's chat, stage change allowed via `vp.long_step` flag in `guard_project_update`): `long_mark_filmed(nas_path)`, `long_mark_edited(note)`, `long_review(approve, note)`, `long_set_posted(platform, posted, url)` (all ticked → done; untick → publish). `long_video_posts`, `long_video_scripters` (+ team default via trigger; `can_edit_script_row` long branch), project fields (nas_path, filmed/edited/reviewed at/by, edit_note, review_note, platforms, posted_at, in_app_review), team defaults (default_long_description, long_in_app_review, default_long_scripter_member_id).
- Long-video page: steps are the tabs (`step-bar.tsx`, any step openable, opens on the current step), panels in `step-panels.tsx` (Film, Edit, Review, Post, DescriptionEditor), script page `/videos/[id]/script` (same editor; `ScriptersButton` takes an `action`). Team → Long videos (`long-settings.tsx`).

- 0047: **Thumbnail Studio** (`/videos/[id]/studio`, `modules/studio/`). Variations = `package_entries` (title ≤100, image in private bucket `package-thumbs` at <team>/<project>/<file>, width/height/size, position, up to 3 winners via `toggle_package_winner` (0048, A/B testing)). Placeholder library = `mockup_videos` + private bucket `mockup-library` (server writes only), filled by `importLibrary(team, region)`: YouTube mostPopular (all + Science&Tech 28 + Gaming 20 + Entertainment 24), ~200 videos >60s, thumbnails + channel avatars copied into storage 10 at a time, swapped in only after everything is stored. Mockups (`mockups.tsx`) are drawn at true size (1280 / 834 / 390 / 1920) and scaled by `ScaleFrame`: Home, Search, Up next, Mobile, Tablet, TV; light/dark/both; compare; seeded randomize; fullscreen; shortcuts ←/→ R F D C; warnings (16:9, ≥1280 wide, ≤2 MB, JPG/PNG). Package step shows a summary card (winner + count) and the description editor.

- 0048: up to 3 winners (`toggle_package_winner`, cap enforced in the DB; `set_package_winner` dropped). Studio: ★ toggles, "Winners n/3", Compare starts with the winners; Package card shows all winners. Long page: banner + "Go to <current step>" when viewing another step. Post switches confirm (last one warns it moves to Posted). DatePicker dots use the team shorts colour (soft = planned, solid = full); calendar Move picker shows them. Connected-account buttons: two equal outlined buttons.

- 0049: `swap_shorts(a, b)`: calendar drop of a short onto a short on ANOTHER day swaps them (both auto → trade queue_position; otherwise trade schedule_mode/pin_kind/planned_date/queue_position; anchor only by master; not posted / not with social posts; recalc). Same-day drop = reorder; drop on a day's empty area = move (with capacity options).
- Frame.io removed from the app (Settings "Final file", review card link, video card legacy link, `isFrameioLink`); `hasFrameio` renamed `hasVideo` (= at least one uploaded version). DB column `file_link` left untouched.
- DatePicker dots: hollow ring = planned, solid = full (team shorts colour). Studio on phones: opens on Mobile, icon tools, Details/Library as bottom sheets, grid `minmax(0,1fr)` so the strip can't widen the column. Team settings: tabs via `?tab=` (members / defaults / accounts / appearance / team), max-w-6xl with 2-column groups; OAuth returns to `?tab=accounts`.

- 0050: **documents**. `scripts` now holds several docs per video (`kind` script|research, `name`, `position`; research = long videos only). Edit rule `can_edit_doc` (script: scripters + masters; research: + researchers). Defaults created on first open by `ensureDefaultDocs` (Script · Review · Staging, + Research). `getShortScript/getLongScript` = the main (first) Script version. `script_comments`: anchored by quote + occurrence (never stored in the doc), anyone on the team comments/resolves, author or master deletes; highlights via a ProseMirror decoration plugin (`lib/anchors.ts`). Workspace (`components/workspace.tsx`): DocRail (versions + research, rename by double-click, delete added ones, + Add), right panel (Side by side read-only doc with "Open here", Comments list with jump/resolve/delete/"text changed"), "Copy from…" on empty docs. Script editor views: Strip / Pages / Spread (CSS columns, A4 pages side by side, zoom-to-fit ≥2 pages, desktop only). URLs: `?doc=` `?side=` `?kind=research`.

- 0051: `script_comments.kind` ('comment' | 'edit_idea'); `ensure_script_docs(short, long)` creates the default docs in one locked step (fixes the random "No script has been written"). Workspace v2: rail dots in role colours (Script=scripter, Review=master, Staging=editor, Research=researcher); side by side = an equal page beside the main one (desktop; Spread disabled while open); comments = floating chat (filters All / Comments / Editing ideas, bubbles + highlights in role colours: editing ideas = editor colour, comments = author's main role); selection offers Comment / Editing idea; phones get a ⋯ menu (views, paper, Word, PDF) and the chat as a bottom sheet. Spread shows ≥2 pages. "Empty" = no text and no images (Copy from… returns after Backspace). Script pages are force-dynamic, doc links don't prefetch. Long videos: advancing (buttons or step actions) opens the new step's tab.

- Workspace v3: Side by side = dropdown on the button (no picker bar), right page editable with its own autosave (saveScript CAS), title rows keep both pages aligned (side column not sticky). Clicking a highlighted comment opens a popover at the text (Resolve / All comments). Spread = 2-column grid of A4 page slices (pages = ceil(height / 979px)), click a page → Pages view at that page. Add version/research → name dialog. Heading shortcuts Ctrl+Alt+0..3 shown in the style menu. Phones/tablets: documents dropdown sheet in the top bar (rail is desktop only), top bar sticky only from sm, formatting bar sticky (essentials + More sheet, no sideways scroll).
- Calendar long quick view: winning thumbnail, platforms (posted ✓), assigned people per step. Team → Members: card grid (role pills in role colours, Edit roles / Remove, role editor in-card) + "Invite people" card. Profiles: "Invite to team" (teams where the viewer is master and the person isn't in).

- Mobile menus: `components/ui/anchored-menu.tsx` (portal, always opens DOWN, 12px side margins on phones, closes on outside tap / Esc / scroll) used by the script ⋯ / More / documents menus, Studio Details / Library, calendar month picker, profile invite. Never put fixed menus inside blurred sticky bars (backdrop-filter traps fixed children). Dialogs float with 8px margins on phones.
- Spread = Word "Side to Side": editable CSS-column pages, two at a time (zoom to fit), paged with ‹ › ("Pages 3–4 of 5"), caret-follow; pages only as needed (true row-wrapping needs CSS `column-wrap`, not shipped in browsers yet). Side page follows dark paper (`vp-paper` event; key `vp:script-paper`); the doc open on the right is shown "On the right" and can't be opened on the left. Calendar quick view: thumbnail beside details, Date + Posts to on one row. Team → Members: list rows with Invite people in the header (`invite-toggle.tsx`).

- Spread removed (Strip / Pages only; a saved "spread" falls back to Pages). ScriptersButton always shows a label.
- 0052: team default people for long videos (`default_long_{researcher,filmer,editor,packager,publisher}_id`, + `default_long_scripter_member_id`), set in Team → Defaults → Long videos. New long video form (`videos/new/new-video-form.tsx`): titles (2–5 + main), type, theme, hook, expected date (DatePicker), platforms, people per step (pre-filled), notes, budget; the action assigns `project_assignees` per step, sets the scripter (replacing the trigger's default if different) and platforms. `long_mark_filmed`: only the assigned filmer (any filmer if none assigned) or a master; FilmPanel mirrors it. PersonSelect kinds: + researcher, filmer, packager.
- Stale files from older zips (unzip never deletes): details-card, final-file-card, post-to-card, posting-card (shorts/[id]), date-picker-field, editor-select, final-file-field (short-videos/components). Next: optional in-app video review for long videos (own versions/notes tables + review page reusing the player).

- One people picker everywhere: `ScripterPicker` (chips with × + "+ Add …", props kind / max / readOnly / emptyHint); `PersonSelect` is now that picker with max=1 (all single-role pickers: shorts editor/reviewer/scheduler, team defaults, creation forms). Long video steps use `AssigneeRow` (picker per step, notifies on assign) and Script uses `ScriptersRow` (scripters = the Script step's assignees; `setLongScripter` mirrors into project_assignees). Long video settings (gear, `settings-dialog.tsx`): type/theme, date, platforms, people per step. Ideate redesigned (checklist + section cards). DatePicker treats empty/invalid values as "no date" (opens on today).

- Comments: colour by TYPE everywhere (`TYPE_COLOR`: comment = amber, editing idea = blue) in composer, bubbles, highlights, filters; avatars; calmer tints; Enter adds (Shift+Enter = newline); big view; "Export ideas" (Word via vendored docx, PDF via print window) in script order: Title / Editing Ideas / #n "quote" (bold) / (idea) (italic) — `exportIdeasDocx`, `exportIdeasPdf` in lib/export-docx.ts.
- Shorts Ready to post: default time = now + 30 min (next quarter hour, device clock); "Post all at the same time" switch.
- DatePicker shows shorts + long videos on EVERY picker: `getPlannedDays(from,to)` (calendar/planned-days.ts, cached per range, refreshed on open); orange squares = shorts (hollow/solid with capacity when dayInfo given), blue = long, "+" when more; hover (or press-and-hold on phones) shows a box at the top listing the day's items.
- Design language: rounded squares for platform logos, colour marks (team / kind / role dots, calendar markers), assign chips and the "+ Add" pill. Profile pictures stay round.

- Shorts posting panel: "More options" at the very bottom → "Manually post this video" (confirm) marks every not-yet-posted platform (+ Facebook) posted → Posted. Account settings in tabs (`components/ui/tab-nav.tsx`: Profile / Teams / Preferences / Account). Comments big view: dark backdrop + animation (click outside to shrink). Server-created elements passed into client slots get explicit keys (React key warning).

- 0053 DASHBOARD. `tasks` (derived, never hand-made): `sync_item_tasks(kind, id)` keeps each video's tasks in sync from its step, date and people (triggers on short_videos, long_video_projects, short_scripters, project_assignees, long_video_scripters). States: active (your turn) / waiting (later step) / done (completed_at, credited to the assignee). Own rows only (RLS). `todos` (personal), `profiles.dashboard_layout` (jsonb).
- Dashboard Studio (`modules/dashboard`): layout model (`layout.ts`: CATALOG, sizes s/m/l/w → 12-col grid, readLayout), widgets: tasks, contributions (GitHub grid, colour + scope in its own settings, click a day → quick view), todo (dnd-kit reorder, priority, due, notes), teams (avatar stack max 5 + "+N", click to switch), clock, mini calendar (getPlannedDays). Customize mode: dnd-kit sortable grid (pointer, touch, keyboard), sizes, settings, library, reset, save (`saveLayout`). Deps: @dnd-kit/core, @dnd-kit/sortable, @dnd-kit/utilities.
- Dashboard part 2 next: /tasks page (full history by day with filters). (Upcoming shorts / long videos, pipeline, posting today widgets are done.)

- 0054: `sync_item_tasks` without a temporary table (jsonb instead): 0053's temp table could make stage changes fail. Unexpected short DB errors now show code + message (and are logged).
- 0055: `profiles.sounds_enabled` (boolean, default true, column-granted): Settings → Preferences → Sounds.
- 0057: meetings. Tables `meetings` (team, title, starts_at timestamptz, duration_min, location default 'Discord', link, agenda, notes, status scheduled/cancelled, reminded_3d/1d/1h_at), `meeting_attendees` (meeting, user, team, rsvp pending/yes/maybe/no, responded_at), `meeting_actions` (text, owner, due_date, done_at). `can_organize_meetings(team)` = master or scheduler (publisher). RLS: the team sees everything; organizers add/edit/cancel/delete meetings and invite/uninvite; you change only your own answer (column grant rsvp), anyone on the team can join themselves; anyone on the team adds/ticks action items, author or organizer removes them. Triggers keep server fields honest and reset reminders when the time moves (reminders whose window already started are marked sent). Realtime on all three. `meeting_reminders_due()` + `run_posting_tick()` now also wakes the app when a reminder is due (same Vault secrets as posting).
- 0056: script sketches. `script_comments.sketch_path / sketch_w / sketch_h` (size-checked); the `script_comments_write()` trigger now lets only the author change body, kind or sketch fields and requires the sketch path to be `<team>/<script>/<file>.png`; public bucket `script-sketches` (10 MB, png + json) with insert policy "teammates upload sketches" (folder = a script of a team in `my_team_ids()`). Deleting a comment removes its png + json (admin client, after the delete is allowed). The app reads comments without the sketch columns if 0056 hasn't run yet.
- Dashboard round 2: full width; one of each widget (library + readLayout dedupe); new widgets: upcomingShorts, upcomingLongs, pipeline, posting (today + failures), weather (Open-Meteo, city search in its settings, °C/°F, device location fallback Bucharest); analog clock (second hand setting); My tasks redesign (summary tiles, grouped sections, cards with step progress). Drag: reorder on dragOver (grid reflows = live preview) + DragOverlay floating card, settle time 180ms (prevents ping-pong loops between different-sized widgets). Contribution squares scale 12–28px to fill the card.

- Dashboard round 4 (positioned board, replaces the bento/S-M-L system): every widget has x/y/w/h on a 12 column grid (`modules/dashboard/grid.ts`, pure + unit-tested: `compact` slides up, `preview(base, {move|resize})` = push aside / swap with a widget it mostly covers / top edge past a widget's middle = go below it, `fill` grows widgets into empty space right, left, then down within `CATALOG[type].limits`, display only; `fixed` widgets (sized by hand) don't grow; `firstFit` for new widgets). Layout v2 `{v:2, widgets:[{id,type,x,y,w,h,fixed?,settings}], fill, sounds}`; `readLayout` migrates v1 to the default and cleans/limits/de-overlaps; `saveLayout` stores `readLayout(raw)`. DEFAULT_LAYOUT = the team's favourite screenshot (6 cols there, x2 here). CSS `.dash-board` positions with vars (`--col`/`--rowh` px from JS once measured, container units only for first paint; never animate container units, Chrome restarts those transitions from half-measured values); transitions only while customizing (`.dash-anim`). Below 960px board width: CSS stacks in reading order (`--o`, 2 cols then 1), drag off. Drag = pointer capture + rAF writing `--dx/--dy/--tilt` directly (React state only when the target cell changes), lifted card shrinks to its saved size, dashed ghost shows where it lands, grid cells fade in, auto-scroll near window edges, spring landing; resize from right/bottom edges and corner (double-click corner = normal size); arrow keys move, Shift+arrows resize. Widgets read their box via `useBox()` (`widget-box.tsx`) and adapt (clock face beside/above/hidden, weather days by width, posting two-line, teams/todo compact). Library = live previews of every widget with real data (scaled real components, `inert`). Sounds: `lib/sounds.ts` (Web Audio, no files; check/uncheck/lift/drop/pop; dashboard "Sounds" toggle). To-do: All is default (remembered per device), checking = pop + drawn check + sparks + green flash + chime, title struck through, then slides to the finished group (FLIP); in Active it folds away after 0.75s; progress bar. Every `DndContext` gets `id={useId()}`.

- v1.1.0 polish (round 5): **sounds app-wide** (`lib/sounds.ts`: check, uncheck, lift, drop, pop, success, error, notify, send, tick, ask, advance, celebrate; throttled 40ms; `SoundSync` in the dashboard layout applies `profiles.sounds_enabled` (0055) and plays `data-sound="<name>"` on click, `data-sound="none"` opts out, switches/checkboxes/radios tick; toasts play success/error or `opts.sound` (`useAction({ sound })`), confirm = ask, realtime notification = notify, comments = send, stage moves = advance, Posted = celebrate, calendar drag = lift/drop). Settings → Preferences → Sounds (On/Off). The dashboard's own Sounds toggle was removed (layout.sounds kept for old data, ignored). **Clip the clapperboard** mascot (`components/ui/mascot.tsx`, moods celebrate/idle, CSS in globals "Mascot"; fixed colours + cream halo so it works on dark; sin/cos values rounded or SSR hydration differs). My tasks: today clear = Clip celebrates "All done for today!"; only overdue left = calm Clip + red link; Next up = quiet one-line list with "See all". Drag: card eases after the pointer (55ms time constant), sticky target cell (0.62 hysteresis), softer tilt, `scale` property for lift, longer glide curve, glowing ghost (the cell grid came back in 1.2.0: faint while customizing, brighter while dragging; `.dash-cells`). Widgets: rise-in cascade only on page open (`.dash-intro`, removed after 1.5s so nothing replays), list cascades (`motion-stagger`), pipeline bars grow + `CountUp` numbers (`components/ui/count-up.tsx`), contribution columns ripple in, animated weather icon, live dots for uploading/failed posts, today pulses on the mini calendar, hover lift; to-do: last one checked = fanfare + Clip. Popups: `overflow-x-hidden` on dialog/menus/pickers (no sideways scrollbar).

- v1.2.0 Scripting (round 6): **@mentions** in script comments + editing ideas (`modules/scripts/lib/mention-people.ts`: people with their jobs on THIS video; `rankPeople`/`rankRoles` put the likely person first: editing idea → this video's editor, other editors, schedulers, master; comment → this video's scripters, master, the rest), autocomplete in `comment-composer.tsx` (arrows/Enter/Tab/Esc, "Notifies" chips), server resolves with `lib/mentions.ts` and sends kind `script_mention` (metadata `href` = script page `?doc=&comment=`, the bell follows `metadata.href`; the workspace opens that comment). **Draft safety**: the quoted text stays highlighted while writing (pending decoration + "Writing a comment…" tag in `anchors.ts`), selection changes are ignored while a draft is open, Esc/close asks before discarding, `beforeunload`, debounced local backup with a Restore/Discard banner. **Sketch Studio** (`modules/scripts/sketch/engine.ts` pure scene model + `sketch-studio.tsx` UI, no new packages): select/hand/pen/marker/eraser/shapes/line/arrow/text/sticky/stamps/images (file + paste), properties bar, handles + rotate + marquee, undo/redo (120), zoom/pinch/fit, grid toggle, palm rejection, keyboard shortcuts, phone layout; "Use sketch" exports a PNG (white, padded, max 1600px) + the scene JSON (re-editable), uploaded to `script-sketches`. Sketch thumbnails under their idea → `components/ui/lightbox.tsx` (Esc/X/backdrop/swipe down, tap to zoom). Word + PDF exports put each sketch under its idea. **Skeletons**: every route has a loading.tsx that mirrors its page (fixed words such as titles, tabs and column names are real text, only data shimmers; helpers in `components/ui/skeleton.tsx`: SkeletonPage/Back/Tabs/Steps/Lines/Section/Button); the dashboard skeleton is the default board in the real `.dash-board` CSS; `shorts/new`, `videos/new`, `videos/[id]/script`, `posting`, `dashboard`, `teams/new` got their own (child routes otherwise show the parent's skeleton). One shimmer wave for the whole screen (`.skeleton` uses a viewport-fixed gradient). **What's new** (not a page): `lib/changelog.ts` is the record of every release; `components/ui/whats-new.tsx` = `WhatsNewHost` (in the dashboard layout: the window + a one-time "VPlanner 1.x is here" card with Clip after feature updates) and `WhatsNewButton` (sidebar row with an amber dot until seen, Settings header, Team footer); seen/told state per device in localStorage. Clip in My tasks is sized from the free space (ResizeObserver; up to 230px) and Next up shows 2 items when today's list is done.

- v1.2.1 (round 7): **THE calendar**: `components/ui/month-calendar.tsx` (`MonthCalendar`) is the only month calendar in the app; `DatePicker` (popover) and `DateChip` (compact chip with the calendar icon, optional ×) in `components/ui/date-picker.tsx` wrap it, and the dashboard Calendar widget is `<MonthCalendar fit hrefFor=…>`. It fetches `getPlannedDays(from, to)` (now returns `{days, perDay, weekends, limits}`) so every calendar shows shorts hollow = planned / solid = full (team shorts-per-day + day limits) and long videos, with the hover / press-and-hold peek, Today, Clear and the legend. `dayInfo` only overrides the shorts count (e.g. leave out the short being moved). The big /calendar page is a separate planning view. **Rule: never build another month calendar; change MonthCalendar and it changes everywhere.** **Sketch uploads** go through `prepareSketchUpload(scriptId)` (server action: you must be able to read the script; admin client creates the `script-sketches` bucket if it's missing and returns one-time signed upload links for the PNG + JSON; client `uploadToSignedUrl`), so storage policies can't block them; errors show their real reason. `deleteComment` works before 0056 too. **Black paper** in Sketch Studio: `Scene.paper` ("light" | "dark"), `PAPER` colours, `INK` adapts (`shownOn`), marker uses screen on dark, the studio root gets the `dark` class, export keeps the paper; remembered in localStorage `vp-sketch-paper`. Deleting a script comment / editing idea asks first.

- 0058: analytics. Tables (all keyed by team): `analytics_daily` (team, platform, day, content `all|shorts|long` → views, watch_minutes, avg_view_seconds, likes, comments, shares, saves, reach, followers_gained/lost, followers (snapshot), total_views/total_likes (snapshots; TikTok only gives totals, so its daily views = the difference between days)), `analytics_countries` (team, platform, metric `views|followers`, day, country alpha-2 → value, watch_minutes), `analytics_content` (one row per video/post: platform + external_id, kind short/long/post, title, url, thumbnail, published_at, duration, latest views/likes/comments/shares/saves/reach, linked `short_id` / `project_id`), `analytics_syncs` (per team+platform: last_run_at, last_ok_at, last_error, backfilled, revenue_note), `analytics_revenue_daily` (YouTube estimated revenue per day, currency) and `revenue_access` (team, user, granted_by, granted_at). `can_view_revenue(team)` = master, or granted AND still an active member; `is_master_of(team)`. RLS: the team reads the four non-revenue tables (no browser writes at all; the sync writes with the service role); revenue rows only via `can_view_revenue`; `revenue_access`: you see your own row, masters see all, only masters grant (target must be an active member) or revoke; a trigger stamps granted_by/granted_at.

- 0059: Facebook for analytics. The "platform in (youtube, instagram, tiktok)" checks on `social_accounts`, `oauth_states`, `analytics_daily`, `analytics_countries`, `analytics_content`, `analytics_syncs` are replaced by ones that also allow `facebook` (found by definition, so the old auto-generated names don't matter); `analytics_daily.engagements` (Facebook gives one "post engagements" number). Revenue stays YouTube-only.
- 0060: `profiles.palette` (colour theme id, null = the original), `grant update (palette)` to authenticated.
- 0062: script hand-offs + revenue currency. `profiles.currency` (ISO 4217, `grant update (currency)`). `scripts.step` ('write' | 'review' | 'staging', script docs only; backfilled by name for existing videos with the edit trigger paused so nothing counts as an edit; `scripts_one_step_per_video` unique index; `scripts_kind_fixed` also keeps a step from changing; `ensure_script_docs` sets it on new docs). `team_script_people` (team, step review|staging, member) = Team → Defaults → Scripts; `script_doc_people` (script, member, team, added_by) = per video on its Review / Staging doc. `script_step_member_ids(script)` = the doc's own people, else the team's defaults (active members). `is_script_step_person(script)`; `can_edit_script_id` and the "writers edit scripts" UPDATE policy now also allow those people (so their image uploads work too). `set_script_people(script, team, step, members[])` (per doc: masters, schedulers, the video's scripters; team defaults: masters; everyone must be an active member). `script_handoffs` log + `script_hand_off(script, copy)`: Script (scripters/masters) → Review, Review (reviewers/masters) → Staging; refuses when the next step has nobody; copies the text into the next doc only when it's empty (no text, no images); returns the recipients' user ids. RLS: team reads all three tables; writes only through the functions. Tested on a stub database (permissions, copy, defaults, outsiders, immutability, re-run).
- 0063: tasks for everything, one account = one team, error log. Stops with a list if any (platform, external_id) is in more than one team, then unique index `social_accounts_one_team (platform, external_id)`; `social_audit_log` action check gains `refused_taken`. `tasks.kind` now 'short' | 'long' | 'script' | 'meeting'. `sync_action_task(action)` + trigger `meeting_actions_tasks`: the owner has an active task (due = the item's due date), done when ticked, moved when reassigned, deleted with the item. `sync_script_tasks(script)` + triggers on `script_handoffs`, `script_doc_people`, `team_script_people`, and `short_videos` / `long_video_projects` (stage/date changes): a Review / Staging doc that was handed to gives its step people a task (stage review|staging, item = the doc), done when it's handed on or the video leaves its writing steps (short: script; long: ideate/research/script). Backfills open action items and handed-off docs. `app_errors` (fingerprint unique; source server|browser|job; message, route, digest, stack, last_user_id, count, first/last_seen, alerted_at, resolved_at; RLS on, no policies) + `record_app_error(...)` → {id, count, alert} (alert = first time, after it was marked fixed, or ≥ 1 h since the last alert); `status_checks()` → timer scheduled/last run (pg_cron), analytics last copy / failing, posts failed / published in 24 h, errors in 24 h (+ kinds), accounts needing reconnect. Both service_role only. Tested on a stub database incl. a re-run.
- 0064: performance (changes no permissions). Indexes every foreign key in `public` that had no ordinary index starting with its columns (found from the catalog: 11 on a full database), `tasks (user_id, state)`, `app_errors (last_seen desc)`. Rewrites every RLS rule that still asked per row: `auth.uid()` → `(select auth.uid())`, `is_team_member(col)` → `col in (select my_team_ids())`, `is_team_master(col)` → `col in (select my_master_team_ids())` (plain column arguments only, never under NOT) via `alter policy` from `pg_policies` (33 rules). Idempotent (the second run changes nothing). Tested: all 64 migrations from scratch on a Supabase stub, rules diffed before/after, access checks as master / member / outsider.
- 0066: "Staging done" (optional last tick of the script flow). `script_handoffs` checks re-made with names (old auto-named ones found by definition and dropped): from_step write|review|staging, to_step review|staging|done, plus `script_handoffs_done_shape` (to_step done ⇔ from_step staging). A done row: script_id = the video's Staging doc, to_script_id null. `sync_script_tasks` also closes a Review / Staging doc's open tasks when the video has a done row at or after the doc's last hand-in (undo reopens them: the done row is deleted and a new active task is inserted for current step people). `script_finish(p_script any doc of the video, p_done default true)` security definer: masters, the video's scripters (`can_edit_doc`), its Review or Staging people; inserts / deletes the done row, syncs both docs, returns {done, changed, staging_id, team, short, long, recipients} (recipients only on a new done: active members on Review / Staging, scripters, masters, minus the actor). Tested on a stub chain: from scratch + twice, outsider refused, idempotent, tasks close / reopen, team can read, direct insert refused.
- 0067 (1.9.8): status history. `status_samples(at, component, level ok|warn|down, detail ≤300)` RLS on, no policies (service role only), indexes (at) + (component, at desc); `status_state` (one row: the last status call's pg_net request id + time). `status_tick()` security definer: writes "app" for the PREVIOUS call from `net._http_response` (missing row = didn't answer → down; 5xx / timed out / no status → down; 2xx–4xx → ok, and 4xx also writes timer = warn "the app refused the timer's call: check CRON_SECRET / posting_url"), deletes samples older than 30 days, then `posting_call('{"status": true}')` and remembers the request id. `status_history(p_hours 1–720)` = worst level per component per hour with sample / warn / down counts and the worst sample's detail; `status_incidents(p_days 1–30)` = runs of non-ok samples per component (a gap over 25 min splits them; `ended_at` = the next check after the last bad one, null = ongoing; max 50); `status_current()` = latest level per component in the last 30 min. All three execute for service_role only. `cron.schedule('vplanner-status', '*/10 * * * *', 'select status_tick();')` (same name again = update). Tested on the stub: from scratch + twice, no Vault → nothing, no answer / 200 / 401 / timeout cases, history + incidents + current on synthetic data, 30-day cleanup, authenticated refused.
- 0068 (1.9.9): bug reports / suggestions. `feedback_reports(user_id → profiles set null, team_id → teams set null, kind bug|idea, message 1–500, files jsonb array ≤3 of {path, name, type, size}, context jsonb ≤4000 bytes, status new|done, created_at, done_at)`, indexes (created_at desc), (user_id, created_at desc), (team_id). RLS: select own only; no insert / update / delete for anon / authenticated (developers read and change through the admin client). Private bucket `feedback` (26214400 bytes = 25 MB, `image/*`, `video/*`); storage policies: insert / select / delete only in your own folder `<user id>/…`. `submit_feedback(p_kind, p_message, p_files, p_context, p_team)` security definer, authenticated only: checks kind, trimmed length 1–500, ≤3 files, each path in the caller's folder AND present in storage.objects (bucket feedback), at most 10 per user per hour (P0001), keeps the team only if it's one of theirs, cleans the file entries, context dropped if over 3000 bytes; raises plain-English messages (22023 / P0001 / 42501) the app shows as they are. Tested on the stub: from scratch + twice, ok case, every refusal, anon refused, direct insert / update refused, rate limit at the 11th, another user sees nothing.
- 0069 (1.10.0): Clip's tour + why the status check failed. `profiles.tutorial_done_at timestamptz` (null = show the tour once) + `grant update (tutorial_done_at) on profiles to authenticated` (own row only, through the profiles update policy). `status_state.last_answer_at / last_answer_code / last_answer` (the last answer to the 10-minute status call). `status_tick()` replaced: reads the previous call's `net._http_response` (status_code, timed_out, error_msg, first 2000 chars of the body) and names the reason: no answer / timed out / not reachable / answered; 401–403 with "vercel" in the body = Vercel's protection (fix: Vault `vercel_bypass` = Vercel → Settings → Deployment Protection → Protection Bypass for Automation); 401 = passwords differ (Vercel `CRON_SECRET` vs Vault `cron_secret`); 404 = wrong address (Vault `posting_url`); 5xx = the app errored; other 4xx = refused. Samples logic unchanged. `status_checks()` redefined: `timer_last` also has `message` (the job's last `return_message`, 200 chars). Tested from scratch + run twice, each reason checked on the stub.
- 0070 (1.11.0): daily jobs on Supabase's timer + how long video files stay. `teams.media_keep_days smallint not null default 14` + check (7, 14, 21, 30) + `grant update (media_keep_days) on teams to authenticated` (masters only through the "masters can update team details" policy). pg_cron: `vplanner-cleanup` '30 3 * * *' → `posting_call('{"job": "cleanup"}')`, `vplanner-analytics` '10 5 * * *' → `posting_call('{"job": "analytics"}')` (scheduling the same name again updates it). Vercel Cron is gone (vercel.json has no crons): it only ran on the production deployment, so staging never copied analytics (the "Analytics updates: last copy 41 h ago" warning) or cleaned up. Tested from scratch + run twice; default 14, 30 accepted, 10 refused, grant present.
- 0071 (1.12.0): posting to Facebook. Drops `social_posts`' platform check (found by definition: `platform` + `tiktok`) and adds `social_posts_platform_check` = youtube, instagram, tiktok, facebook; `teams.post_time_facebook time not null default '18:00'` + `grant update (post_time_facebook) on teams to authenticated`. Tested from scratch + run twice (constraint has facebook, default 18:00, grant present).
- 0072 (1.12.1): who can comment on a script. `can_comment_script(p_script)` (security definer) = `can_edit_doc(team, short, long, kind)` (masters, the video's scripters; researchers on research docs) OR `is_script_step_person()` on any Review / Staging doc of the same video. `script_comments` insert policy "script people comment" (was "teammates comment": anyone on the team) and update policy "script people resolve comments" (can_comment_script OR the author). Reading unchanged (team). Tested: master/scripter/reviewer/staging can, outsider and a scheduler not on the script can't; outsider resolve = 0 rows; author edits own body; run twice.
- 0073 (1.12.1): `guard_project_update()` (same as 0045) + a long video's `expected_date` changes only for masters and schedulers (`has_team_role(team, 'publisher')`); no user (server after its own check) passes. Tested: filmer refused (42501), filmer's other fields fine, master ok, server ok; run twice.
- 0074 (1.12.2): Facebook earnings in Revenue. `analytics_revenue_daily` platform check becomes `platform in ('youtube', 'facebook')` (the old check is found by definition: contains platform + youtube, dropped, re-added as `analytics_revenue_daily_platform_check`). Read rules unchanged (`can_view_revenue`: masters + people a master picks); only the server writes. Tested: facebook + youtube rows accepted, tiktok refused (check), master reads both, a member without access reads 0, `authenticated` can't insert; from scratch + run twice.
- 0075 (1.13.0): `developer_usage()` (security definer, plpgsql; EXECUTE revoked from public/anon/authenticated, granted to service_role only): one JSON with database size, top 80 tables (bytes; exact row counts for public tables, reltuples estimates for others), storage per bucket, uploads per day (30 d), counts (people / 7 d / 30 d sign-ins, new, teams, shorts, longs, live and cleaned video files, scripts, posts, tasks done), per team (members, shorts, longs, video files/bytes, files/storage bytes attributed by path's first folder: team id for review-videos/package-thumbs/mockup-library/script-sketches/team-logos, long video id for thumbnails/comment-attachments, script id for script-images; rows over every public table with team_id and an estimated dbBytes share), per person (auth.users + profiles: teams, last sign-in, live videos uploaded, other files by storage owner, tasks done). Tested: numbers right on sample data, authenticated/anon refused, service_role ok; run twice.
- 0076 (1.13.0): `teams.tasks_visibility` ('own' default | 'masters' | 'team', check `teams_tasks_visibility_check`, column grant); `shared_task_team_ids()` (team for members, masters for masters) and `can_see_team_tasks(p_team)`; tasks policy "team tasks when shared" (beside "your own tasks"). Tested: own → 1 each; masters → master 3, others own only; team → everyone in the team 3, other team 1; bad value refused; non-master update 0 rows; run twice.
- 0077 (1.13.0): `daily_word_plays` (user_id, day, puzzle, guesses text[] ≤ 6, solved, finished_at, created/updated; PK user+day; check solved ⇒ finished); RLS select own only, no client writes (server writes after checking the guess); `daily_word_team(p_team, p_day)` (security definer: members' tries/solved/finished for a team you're in). Tested: 7 tries and solved-unfinished refused; member reads only own, can't insert/update; team results only for members, outsider excluded, other team empty, anon refused; run twice.
- 0078 (1.14.0): objectives. `objectives` (team_id, title 1–80 (trimmed), metric matching `^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{1,39}$` (the app knows the ids, so a new metric needs no migration), period day|week|month|quarter|year, target 1..1e9, filters jsonb object ≤ 2000 bytes, color `^[a-z]{2,16}$` default blue, position double, paused_at, created_by/created_at/updated_at); trigger `objectives_write`: at most 50 per team (23514 "A team can have up to 50 objectives."), stamps created_by/created_at, keeps team_id/created_by on update, trims the title; column grants (insert: id, team_id, title, metric, period, target, filters, color, position, paused_at; update: title, metric, period, target, filters, color, position, paused_at); RLS: teammates select (`my_team_ids()`), masters insert/update/delete (`my_master_team_ids()`). `objective_targets` (PK objective_id + period_start, team_id stamped from the objective, target 0..1e9 where 0 = that period off, updated_by/updated_at; trigger `objective_targets_write`: period_start must be a start of the objective's period (23514), keys immutable; grants insert (objective_id, period_start, team_id, target) / update (target); masters write, teammates read). `objective_periods` (PK objective_id + period_start, team_id, period_end, target, value numeric(16,2), reached_at, reached_target, winner jsonb ≤ 2000 bytes, celebrated_at, updated_at): teammates select only, written by the server. Trigger `objectives_redefined`: changing metric / filters / period clears that objective's periods (and a new period also clears its targets). `objective_sync` (team_id PK, synced_at; no client access). Functions (security definer, EXECUTE for service_role only): `objective_claim_sync(p_team, p_seconds default 30)` → true when it's this call's turn (the throttle), `objective_record(p_rows jsonb)` (per row: upsert period_end/target/value when changed; then claim the win once: reached_at/reached_target/winner set when target > 0 and value ≥ target and not yet won at this or a higher target; `quiet` rows leave celebrated_at empty; returns the new, non-quiet wins; a non-array → 22023). Realtime publication: objectives, objective_targets, objective_periods.
- 0079 (1.15.0): only the connected accounts' numbers, more numbers, the daily word in one step. `analytics_syncs.account_ref` (text 1–300: the platform's own account id, or `name:<lowercased name>` for numbers kept while nothing is connected) + `account_since` (when that account became the team's; null = from the start). `analytics_claim_legacy()` (security definer, revoked from public/anon/authenticated; the migration runs it once): for each connected account without a claimed row, walks `social_audit_log` (connected / reconnected / disconnected) newest first; an event naming another account (another lowercased username / display name) before this account's own run means a switch: that platform's analytics_daily / analytics_countries / analytics_revenue_daily rows up to the switch day (UTC) and analytics_content rows updated before it are deleted, and the row is claimed with account_since = the switch and backfilled = false (the next copy fetches the history again); platforms with nothing connected keep `name:<last account>`. New columns: analytics_daily engaged_views, avg_view_pct numeric(6,2), dislikes, playlist_adds; analytics_content engaged_views, watch_minutes numeric(16,1), avg_view_seconds numeric(10,1), avg_view_pct numeric(6,2), subscribers_gained, skip_rate numeric(6,2) (Instagram's reels_skip_rate), reposts. `analytics_content_days` (PK team_id + platform + external_id + day; platform check the 4; source 'daily' = the platform's own number for the day (YouTube) | 'total' = the running total that morning's copy saw (the day = next copy − this one); views, likes, comments, shares, watch_minutes, updated_at; index (team_id, day); RLS teammates select only, the server writes). `daily_word_guess(p_user, p_day, p_puzzle, p_word, p_solved)` → (result ok|over|again, guesses, solved, finished): plpgsql security definer with `#variable_conflict use_column`, `^[a-z]{5}$` else 22023, insert on conflict do nothing then `for update`, appends unless finished or already tried, finishes on a solve or the 6th guess; EXECUTE for service_role only. Checks: `supabase/tests/connected-numbers.test.sql`.
- 0080 (1.15.0): projections. `projections` (id, team_id, title, metric `^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{1,39}$` (the ids live in the app: a new metric needs no migration), scope jsonb ≤ 1000 bytes, target numeric(20,4), direction up|down, start_day, deadline (> start_day, ≤ start_day + 3660), start_value, baseline jsonb ≤ 20000 bytes (Compare's "then"), color `^[a-z]{2,16}$`, note ≤ 300, position, created_by/created_at/updated_at, achieved_at/achieved_value, ended_at/ended_value, archived_at, `money` generated always as (metric ~ '^(revenue|rpm)') stored); trigger `projections_write`: at most 50 running per team, stamps created_by/created_at, people can't set achieved/ended, metric/scope/start_day/start_value/baseline/team_id never change, a new target or direction clears achieved, a later deadline (today or after) clears ended; column grants (insert: id, team_id, title, metric, scope, target, direction, start_day, deadline, start_value, baseline, color, note, position; update: title, target, direction, deadline, color, note, position, archived_at); RLS select `team_id in my_team_ids() and (not money or can_view_revenue(team_id))`, masters insert/update/delete (`my_master_team_ids()`). `projection_points` (PK projection_id + day, team_id, value, created_at): select through its projection (same money rule), written by the service role only. Realtime publication: projections. Checks: `supabase/tests/projections.test.sql`.
- 0081 (1.16.0): advanced analytics and Download for Claude. `analytics_daily.metrics` + `analytics_content.metrics` (jsonb object ≤ 8000 bytes, default '{}': every other number a platform shares, per day / per video; supabase-js upserts fill missing keys with null, so the sync merges the stored ones first: `keepStoredMetrics`), `analytics_revenue_daily` gains cpm / playback_cpm numeric(12,4) and ad_impressions / monetized_playbacks bigint. `analytics_breakdowns` (PK team_id + platform + external_id ('' = the account, else one video, ≤ 200) + dimension `^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{1,39}$` + span (1 = that day; 7 / 28 / 90 / 365 = the window ending on `day`; 0 = everything up to `day`, a video's since it was published) + day + key (1–300: the platform's own name: YT_SEARCH, MOBILE, age25-34|female…); views, watch_minutes numeric(16,1), value numeric(18,4), updated_at; index (team_id, dimension, day)). `analytics_retention` (PK team_id + platform + external_id; points jsonb array ≤ 32000 bytes of [where 0…1, share still watching (> 1 with rewatches), vs similar videos 0…1 or null]). `analytics_content_revenue` (PK team_id + platform (youtube | facebook) + external_id; revenue, ad / premium / gross revenue numeric(14,4), cpm, playback_cpm, ad_impressions, monetized_playbacks, currency `^[A-Z]{3}$`). RLS: breakdowns and retention teammates select (`my_team_ids()`), content revenue `can_view_revenue(team_id)`; insert/update/delete revoked (the server writes). `analytics_breakdown_sums(p_team, p_platform, p_from, p_to)` → (dimension, key, views, watch_minutes, value, days): security invoker, EXECUTE for authenticated; the per-day rows (external_id '', span 1) of a range added up, `value` weighted by views. Bucket `video-frames` (private, 3 MB, image/jpeg only; `<team>/<short>/<version>.jpg`): teammates read (`review_path_ok` + `my_team_ids()`), `can_upload_short_video(short)` insert, masters delete. `short_video_versions.frames_path` (≤ 300) is set only by `set_version_frames(p_version, p_path)` (security definer, authenticated: the caller may upload to that short, the path must be that version's own file name, the object must exist); trigger `short_video_versions_frames_guard` nulls it on inserts made as anon / authenticated. Checks: `supabase/tests/advanced-analytics.test.sql`.
