- 0083 (1.19.0): every video, numbers within minutes, impressions and CTR. Columns: `analytics_content.impressions` (bigint), `ctr` (numeric(6,2), %), `live_at`, `details_at`; `analytics_content_days.impressions/ctr`; `analytics_daily.impressions/ctr`; `analytics_syncs.live_at`, `live_full_at`, `live_error` (≤ 500), `live_cursor` (≤ 2000: where a long list stopped), `live_totals` (jsonb object ≤ 2000 bytes: followers, total_views, total_likes, videos, at), `reach_job` (≤ 200), `reach_after`, `reach_checked_at`, `reach_note` (≤ 500). Table `analytics_live` (team_id, platform, external_id, at, views, likes, comments, shares; PK (team, platform, external_id, at); indexes (team_id, at), (at); RLS: teammates read (`my_team_ids()`), no writes for anon/authenticated). `analytics_live_hours(p_team, p_hours default 48)` and `analytics_live_gains(p_team, p_minutes default 2880)` (security invoker: RLS applies; a change between two looks spread evenly over the time between them, never negative; ≤ 8 days). `analytics_live_fanout()` (security definer, revoked from public/anon/authenticated): one `posting_call({job: 'live', team, platform})` per distinct active (team, platform), deletes counts older than 8 days, thins counts between 48 and 60 hours old to the last one of each hour per video (hourly sums unchanged). Timer `vplanner-live` '5-59/10 * * * *'. `analytics_reach_totals(p_team)` (security definer, service role only): each YouTube video's impressions and impressions-weighted CTR over the days kept. `analytics_syncs` joins the `supabase_realtime` publication. Checks: `supabase/tests/live-numbers.test.sql` (fan-out, pruning, thinning, RLS, sums, no writes / calls for signed-in people, reach totals).
- 0084 (1.20.0): the new Analytics pages, read-only functions (security invoker: RLS applies, nothing written). `analytics_top_content(p_team, p_from date, p_to date, p_limit int default 10, p_platforms text[] default null)` → (platform, external_id, views, from_totals): what each video got in a range of days, biggest first, ≤ 100: YouTube's `analytics_content_days` 'daily' rows summed; the others' 'total' rows: the last copy up to the morning after the range minus the first copy of the range (a video published in the range starts from 0; a drop is no gain), only the platforms asked. `analytics_live_buckets(p_team, p_minutes default 60, p_step default 10)` → (at, platform, views, likes, comments): the 10-minute counts per `p_step`-minute slot over the last `p_minutes` (step 1–60, at most two days of slots), the slots ending NOW (so they add up to `analytics_live_gains(p_minutes)`), a change spread over the time it happened in. Both granted to authenticated + service_role, revoked from public/anon. Checks: `supabase/tests/analytics-pages.test.sql` (top content: YouTube days + running totals, a video posted in the range, first seen in it, a drop, the limit, platforms, dates either way round, RLS; slots: the hour's 60 views spread over 3 slots, slots ending now, likes, RLS, at most two days) and the e2e (`scripts/e2e/live.e2e.ts`: both through PostgREST as a teammate, `p_platforms` as a JSON array).
- 0085 (1.21.0): Sync now in the background, and YouTube's whole history. Columns: `analytics_syncs.history_from` (date: how far back YouTube's per-day numbers go), `history_done` (boolean not null default false: they reach the channel's first day), `sync_requested_at` (timestamptz); `analytics_content_days.subscribers_gained` (bigint) and `avg_view_seconds` (numeric(10,1)): a video's subscribers and average view per day, filled when its page asks YouTube. `analytics_sync_now(p_team)` → int (security definer; granted to authenticated, revoked from public/anon): masters and schedulers (`has_team_role(team, 'publisher')`) only, else 42501; -1 when any platform of the team was asked in the last 90 seconds; else one `posting_call({job: 'analytics', team, platform, live: true})` per distinct platform with an `active` account (an account to reconnect is skipped), upserting that platform's `sync_requested_at` for each call sent (a database without the timer's secrets answers null: nothing sent, nothing marked); returns how many were sent. Safe to run more than once. Checks: `supabase/tests/sync-now.test.sql` (the columns and the default, an editor refused, the master gets 2 then -1, two calls queued with `live`, `sync_requested_at` per platform) and the e2e (`scripts/e2e/youtube-history.e2e.ts`: the RPC through PostgREST as editor and master, the history backfill down to the channel's first day).
- 0086 (1.22.0): analytics you can trust. Live streams are a kind: `analytics_content.kind` check adds 'live', `analytics_daily.content` and `analytics_revenue_daily.content` add 'live'; `analytics_content.content_type` (YouTube's creatorContentType: SHORTS, VIDEO_ON_DEMAND, LIVE_STREAM…; check `^[A-Z_]{1,40}$`) and trigger `analytics_content_kind` (before insert or update; YouTube rows with a content_type get their kind from it, others untouched). `analytics_live_totals` (team, platform, at, followers, total_views, videos; PK team+platform+at; RLS: teammates read, no client writes; kept 8 days by `analytics_live_fanout()`): each account's totals every time the 10-minute job reads them. `analytics_syncs.live_since` (since when every video is counted: the first complete full pass; backfilled from `analytics_live` as the moment 80% of the videos seen had first been seen) and `money_currency` (`^[A-Z]{3}$`: the currency YouTube's revenue is asked in, the team owner's). `analytics_content.days_filled_at` (a video's whole history was asked from YouTube: its page asks once) and `analytics_content_days.subscribers_lost` (net subscribers per day). YouTube's stored days are fetched again (`history_from` = a week ago, `history_done` false where a copy has run) so live streams come apart on every day. Functions (security invoker, granted to authenticated + service_role): `analytics_period_videos(team, from, to, limit ≤ 500, platforms, metric)` (per video for a stretch of days: YouTube's own per-day rows added up, subscribers gained − lost, impressions and clicks from the reach rows; the others' running totals: the change between the first copy in the stretch and the morning after, from 0 for a video posted in it; sorted by the number picked), `analytics_live_window(team, from, to)` (per video between two moments, a change spread over the time between reads, ≤ 8 days), `analytics_live_series(team, hours ≤ 192)` (per hour and platform with shares). `analytics_live_fanout()` also drops totals older than 8 days. `analytics_catch_up(team)` (security definer, authenticated): members only (42501), -1 without the timer's Vault secrets, else claims each active platform not copied for 30 hours (`insert … on conflict do update … where last_run_at older than 30 h`) and sends one `posting_call({job: 'analytics', team, platform, live: true})` per claim; returns how many. Ends with `notify pgrst, 'reload schema'`. Checks: `supabase/tests/analytics-accuracy.test.sql` (kinds and the trigger, the checks, the stretch sums for both sources and from 0, sorting / platforms / limit, another team sees nothing, the window and the hourly shares, totals RLS and the 8-day drop, catch-up: outsider refused, -1, one call then 0).
- 0087 (1.23.0): the team's dictionary for writing suggestions. `team_dictionary` (id, team_id, word: 1–48 characters, trimmed, no spaces; added_by default `auth.uid()`; created_at; unique (team_id, lower(word)); trigger `team_dictionary_limit` refuses more than 5,000 words per team with check_violation). RLS: teammates read and add (insert grant on id, team_id, word only, `added_by = auth.uid()`); whoever added a word or a master removes it; no updates. Additive only. Checks: `supabase/tests/writing-suggestions.test.sql` (stamped, no chosen added_by or update, one of each word whatever the capitals, single words only, outsiders see / add / remove nothing, who may remove, the limit).
- 0088 (1.23.1): live streams told apart from premieres; realtime matched to the platform's own count. `analytics_content.live_tab boolean` (null: not known): whether a YouTube video is on the channel's Live tab; the kind trigger (`analytics_content_kind`, replaced) now goes: live_tab true → 'live'; live_tab false and the kind was 'live' or content_type LIVE_STREAM → 'long' (or 'short' when content_type SHORTS: a premiere); else content_type as in 0086. `analytics_syncs.live_ratio jsonb` ({ ratio, from, to, counted, own, at } or { ratio: null, at, why }). Additive only. Checks: `supabase/tests/live-tab.test.sql` (a premiere off the Live tab is long whatever its guess or LIVE_STREAM says, on the tab is live, without it LIVE_STREAM decides, Shorts stay Shorts, other platforms keep their kind, a later guess doesn't undo it, live_ratio exists).
- 0089 (1.25.0): long videos reviewed in ClipTroop, more titles in Thumbnail Studio, how long files stay. Private bucket `long-review-videos` (3 GB a file, mp4 / quicktime / webm; `<team>/<project>/<file>`; `long_review_path_ok(name)`; read: teammates; upload and resumable update: `can_upload_long_video(project)` = master, scheduler or editor; delete: masters). `long_video_versions` (mirror of short_video_versions keyed by project_id; trigger `long_video_versions_before_insert` numbers it, sets team and uploader, checks the folder and that the file is in storage; RLS: teammates read, uploaders insert, no update / delete; realtime). `long_video_comments` (mirror of short_video_comments: time-pinned notes, replies inherit the time, author-only edits, anyone on the video resolves (resolved_by stamped), author or master deletes; guard trigger; realtime). `long_video_projects.approved_version_id` (fk, on delete set null) and `keep_media`: never set directly (`long_review_fields_guard` puts them back unless `vp.long_step`), and `in_app_review` (0045) only by masters (the guard raises 42501). `long_set_keep_media(p_project, p_keep)` (masters, schedulers). `long_mark_edited` replaced (reviewed in ClipTroop = the video's `in_app_review`, else the team's `long_in_app_review`: no live version → 23514 "Upload the edit first"), `long_review` replaced (approving records the latest live version). `teams.long_media_keep_days` (3, 7, 14, 21, 30; default 7) and `studio_keep_days` (7, 14, 30, 90 or null = keep; default 30), column grants. `package_titles` (id, project_id, title 1–100 tidied, position, created_by, created_at; teammates read, masters and packagers write). `global_search` (0026 + `winner_id`: the first winning package entry with a picture; since 1.25.1 a DO block first calls pg_trgm's `similarity()`, because Supabase's `postgres` isn't a superuser and refuses a function's `set pg_trgm.word_similarity_threshold` until pg_trgm is loaded in the session: "permission denied to set parameter" (42501) at statement 82 on the first push). `developer_usage` (0075 + the new bucket in a team's storage, long versions in the video counts). Additive only. Checks: `supabase/tests/long-review.test.sql` (bucket, numbering / folder / file / no update, notes and their rules, other teams, the gate on / off / per video, approve, the guard, the keep switch, the review mode, the defaults and their checks, package_titles, search's winner, developer_usage).
- 0090 (1.26.0): the search bar finds everything. `search_snippet(txt, pos, width default 160)` (immutable: the words around a match, from a word to a word, on one line, "…" where cut; null for nothing). `short_videos_caption_trgm_idx` (gin `lower(caption)`). `global_search(q, max_results)` replaced, still SECURITY INVOKER (RLS decides; projections' money rule from 0080 applies), after the same pg_trgm loader DO block as 0089 (Supabase's `postgres` isn't a superuser; the CI lint checks it): keeps `people`, `projects`, `shorts`, `teams`, `master_teams` and now keeps each row's `score` (4 #number, 3 exact, 2 start, 1 inside, + word_similarity; 0.8 another title, 0.5 a field or caption); `projects` also match theme / subtheme / hook / description / notes (`matched_field`, `matched_text`), `shorts` their caption; new lists, for 3+ characters and not a #number (`deep`): `scripts` (from `search_docs(q, max_results)`, SECURITY DEFINER: under RLS a filter can only use an index when it's leakproof and LIKE isn't, so every search read every script in full (seq scan, ~80 ms for 3,600 scripts here); it applies the table's rule itself (`team_id = any(my_team_ids())`) so `scripts_text_trgm_idx` finds them: the words in a row first (at most 200 candidates sorted: the video's title has them, then the newest), then, if fewer than asked, documents with every word (the three longest through the index, `like all` for the rest); `kind`, `name`, `video_kind`, `video_id`, `entry_number`, `video_title`, `snippet`, team, score 2.3 / 2 / 1; authenticated only; timed on 800 long videos, 2,000 shorts, 3,600 scripts, 200 meetings: 25–50 ms a search, about 120 ms at worst when every document has the words but not in a row), `meetings` (title, agenda or notes; `matched_field`, `matched_text`; nearest in time first among equals), `actions` (meeting action items: `text`, `meeting_id`, `meeting_title`, `meeting_at`, `due_date`, `done`, `mine`, `owner_name`), `objectives` (`title`, `period`, `color`, `paused`), `projections` (`title`, `deadline`, `color`, `achieved`, `ended`, `archived`). Same grants (authenticated only). Additive. Checks: `supabase/tests/search-everything.test.sql` (the snippet, scripts in a row / every word / research / not for 2 letters, search_docs with nobody signed in, as anon, and for another team, meetings by title, agenda and notes, action items marked mine, objectives and projections of your teams only and money only with revenue access, long videos by description, shorts by caption, a #number looks nowhere else, every list in the answer, another team finds only its own). Applied as a role that isn't a superuser: refused without the loader, accepted with it.
- 0091 (1.27.0): Outliers. YouTube's public data, shared by every team (server writes, anyone on a team reads through `on_a_team()`): `yt_channels` (title, handle, avatar, country, subscribers, views, video count, long / Shorts medians, uploads a week, last upload, fetched_at), `yt_videos` (title, description, tags, published_at, duration, kind long|short|live, views / likes / comments, score, projected, vph, formats[], format version, lang, thumb_colors / thumb_hash / lum / sat, chapters, `outlier_doc()` tsvector index, fetched_at / stats_at), `yt_video_stats` (views over time), `yt_video_changes` (title / thumbnail changes). Each team's (RLS: teammates read; masters and researchers through `my_outlier_team_ids()` write niches, niche channels and tracking; masters write alerts; everyone saves): `outlier_niches` (≤ 12 a team, name / keywords / exclude words tidied by a trigger, language, region, kinds, insights jsonb (server), phase, counts; `team_id` null = the 24 Explore niches), `outlier_niche_channels` (source, relevance, hidden), `outlier_niche_hits`, `outlier_tracked`, `outlier_folders`, `outlier_saves` (video | idea | channel, ≤ 5,000 a team, one per folder; `project_id` / `short_id` set only by `outliers_link_idea(save, project, short)`, security definer: all in the caller's team), `outlier_dismissed` (own), `outlier_settings` (alert_min, alert_to managers|everyone|off, watch words, digest; `digest_sent_on` server-only). Server only: `outlier_quota` + `outlier_take(kind, n, cap)` (all or nothing), `outlier_search_cache`, `outlier_search_log`, `outlier_alerts`, `outlier_global`, `outlier_locks` (`outlier_lock` / `outlier_unlock`). Reads: `outliers_overview`, `outliers_videos(team, niche, filters jsonb, sort, limit, offset)` (every filter in one query, peers for the spike chart, total), `outliers_channels`, `outliers_video`, `outliers_channel` (security definer, `is_team_member` first). Collector helpers (service role): `outliers_corpus`, `outliers_due_channels`, `outliers_due_videos` (details again after 22 days), `outliers_due_thumbs`, `outliers_alert_candidates`, `outliers_set_scores / stats / thumbs / formats / relevance`, `outliers_purge(stats_days)` (YouTube's 30 days: unrefreshed videos and channels deleted, saves stay and are read again). Timer `vplanner-outliers` '17,47 * * * *' → `outliers_fanout()` (one `posting_call({job: 'outliers', team})` per team with a niche or a tracked channel, and `{job: 'outliers', explore: true}`). Checks: `supabase/tests/outliers.test.sql` (12 sections) and `scripts/e2e/outliers.e2e.ts`.
- 0092 (1.27.5): comments copied with their script, how long files stay, glass and dashboard backgrounds. (1) `script_comments_write()` (the 0056 insert trigger) keeps the author, `created_at` and resolved state only when the transaction-local setting `vp.copying_comments` is 'on' (anything else is still the writer's, now). `script_copy_comments(p_from, p_to, p_items jsonb default '[]')` SECURITY DEFINER: both documents of the same team and the same video (P0002 for not found / another team / another video / itself), the caller may comment on the target (`can_comment_script`, else 42501); copies every comment and editing idea of the source (kind, body, author, dates, resolved), skips ones the target already has (same quote, occurrence, kind, body, author) and new ids that are taken; `p_items` [{id, new_id, sketch}] gives each copy its id and says which drawings the server already copied to `<team>/<target>/<new_id>.png` (else no drawing). Returns the new ids; execute for authenticated only. (2) `teams.media_keep_days` and `long_media_keep_days` lose NOT NULL; all three keep-days checks (with `studio_keep_days`) become `null or in (1, 3, 7, 14, 21, 30, 60, 90, 180, 365)` (null = never deleted); defaults and today's values unchanged. (3) `profiles.glass_ui` boolean default false, `dashboard_bg_path` text (check `profiles_dashboard_bg_path_check`: in your own folder `<id>/<name>.(jpe?g|png|webp|avif|gif|mp4|webm)`), `dashboard_bg_on` boolean default false, `dashboard_bg_dim` smallint default 30 (0–80); column grants for update. Public bucket `dashboard-backgrounds` (25 MB, those seven types), insert / update / delete only in your own folder. Tested: `supabase/tests/comment-copies.test.sql`, `supabase/tests/keep-and-backgrounds.test.sql`, from scratch + twice.
- 0093 (1.28.0): glass choices, saved wallpapers, profiles, leaving a team. (1) `profiles.glass_style` ('simple' | 'liquid', default simple), `glass_scope` ('cards' | 'everywhere'), `glass_sidebar` boolean, `glass_level` smallint 0–100 (default 50); checks and column grants. (2) `profile_wallpapers` (id, user_id default auth.uid() → profiles on delete cascade, path unique (check: own folder, picture / video extension), luma smallint 0–100 or null, created_at); RLS: see / add / replace / remove your own; grants select, delete, insert (path, luma), update (path, luma). `profile_wallpapers_write()` BEFORE insert / update: user_id = auth.uid(), created_at = now(), 3 at most per person (counted with the profile row locked; P0001 "You can keep 3 wallpapers…"), user_id / created_at never change (42501). `profile_wallpapers_follow()` AFTER update of path, luma or delete: replaced → `profiles.dashboard_bg_path` and `profile_pages.bg_path` follow; removed → the one in use becomes the newest other one (or null and `dashboard_bg_on` false), a profile background using it goes. Today's `dashboard_bg_path` becomes everyone's first wallpaper. (3) Profiles: `vp_task_cat(kind, stage)`, `vp_task_xp(kind, stage)` (long edit 90, film 70, script 60, research 40, short editing 40, package 35, short script 30, long review 25, publish 20, short / script review 15, else 10), `vp_level_floor(level)` (500·a·(a+1) + 100·b·(a+1), a = level / 10, b = level % 10), `vp_level_for(xp)`, `vp_frame_level(frame)` (ring 1, dashed 3, glow 5, duo 8, orbit 12, sunburst 16, laurel 20, prism 30, crown 50), all immutable; `profile_xp(user)` SECURITY DEFINER, not executable by clients: done tasks with `activated_at` (it was their turn), the first completion per (kind, item, stage), +25 % (rounded) when completed (UTC date) by `due_date`. `profile_pages` (user_id pk default auth.uid() → profiles on delete cascade, public default true, bio ≤ 300, layout jsonb ≤ 32 KB, frame (the ten ids), bg_path (own folder), bg_luma (copied, not writable), updated_at); RLS: select own or public (to authenticated), insert / update own; grants insert / update (public, bio, layout, frame, bg_path). `profile_pages_write()` BEFORE insert / update: user_id = auth.uid(), a frame only at its level (P0001 "Reach level N to use this frame."), bg_path only one of your wallpapers (P0001; copies its luma). Bios move from `profiles.bio` (then nulled, since profiles are readable by everyone signed in). `profile_stats(user)` SECURITY DEFINER, execute for authenticated: null when signed out, not there, or private and not yours; else jsonb {xp, tasks, on_time, dated, videos, teams, first_at, last_at, by {cat: n}, ranks {all|cat|ontime: {"1"|"5"|"10"|"25"|"50"|"100"|"250"|"500"|"1000": completed_at}}, team_dates {"2","3"}, levels {"5","10","25","50"}, streak {best, current, dates {"3","7","30"}}, days {UTC date: n, last 372 days}}. (4) `leave_team(team)` SECURITY DEFINER: signed in, not the owner (P0001), an active member (P0002); deletes their ownership transfer requests for it and their member row (task triggers clean their open steps). Tested: `supabase/tests/glass-wallpapers.test.sql`, `supabase/tests/profiles.test.sql`, from scratch + twice.
- 0082 (1.17.0): the morning copy can't get stuck. `job_runs` (id bigserial, job 'analytics' | 'cleanup', team_id → teams on delete cascade, platform (the 4 or null), started_at, finished_at, ok, detail ≤ 2000; index (job, started_at desc); RLS on, no policies: service role only; rows older than 30 days pruned by the 03:30 clean-up). `analytics_morning()` (security definer, revoked from public/anon/authenticated): one `posting_call({job: 'analytics', team, platform})` per distinct active (team, platform) in social_accounts; returns how many. Timers: `vplanner-analytics` at 05:10 UTC now runs `select analytics_morning()`; `vplanner-analytics-retry` at 05:40 and 06:40 UTC sends `{job: 'analytics', retry: true}`. `record_app_error()` (same signature, replaced): source 'job' alerts at most once per 24 h (others once per hour), keeps the newest message and stack, returns `first`. `status_checks()` (replaced, everything from 0069 kept) adds `analytics_last_ok`, `analytics_accounts` (active accounts) and `analytics_job` {started, finished, unfinished (started > 10 min ago, no finish, last 30 h), failed (last 30 h), problem (the latest unfinished/failed run's detail)}. - 0061: revenue streams. `analytics_revenue_daily` gains `ad_revenue`, `premium_revenue` (YouTube Premium = estimatedRedPartnerRevenue), `gross_revenue` (numeric(14,4), nullable). New `revenue_entries` (money YouTube doesn't report): team, day, source (`sponsorship|brand_deal|affiliate|youtube_other|facebook|instagram|tiktok|merch|other`), amount numeric(14,2) > 0, currency, note ≤ 300, optional `short_id` / `project_id`, created_by (default auth.uid()), created_at. RLS: read via `can_view_revenue(team)`, insert/update/delete via `is_master_of(team)`; trigger `revenue_entries_stamp` fixes created_by / created_at and keeps team_id from changing. The app works before it runs (no other income; YouTube streams are saved once it has).
- v1.3.0 (round 8): **Meetings** (`modules/meetings`: `lib/types.ts` (Meeting, RSVP labels, durations, Google Calendar link, `.ics` builder), `lib/queries.ts` (listMeetings / getMeeting / listMyActions; return [] before 0057), `lib/reminders.ts` (`runMeetingReminders()`: claims the tier in the DB, then notifications `meeting_reminder` + one email per person via Resend, times in the team's time zone), components `meetings-home`, `meeting-detail`, `meeting-dialog`, `rsvp`, `local-time` (times always formatted in the viewer's browser), `meetings-realtime`). Pages `/meetings`, `/meetings/[id]`, `/api/meetings/[id]/ics`. Actions `app/(dashboard)/meetings/actions.ts` (create/update/cancel/delete, setRsvp, setInvitees, saveMeetingText (autosave agenda/notes), add/update/deleteAction); notification kinds `meeting_scheduled|meeting_changed|meeting_cancelled|meeting_reminder|meeting_action` (bell follows `metadata.href`). The timer route `/api/cron/posting` runs reminders first, then posts. Dashboard widget `meetings` ("Next meeting", in DEFAULT_LAYOUT at x8 y7 w4 h2). Meetings on the big calendar (`CalendarView meetings` prop, own filter, violet chips; put on the viewer's local day in the browser) and in THE calendar (`getPlannedDays` returns `meetings`; violet dot top-right, peek lists them, legend "meeting"). **Navigation**: `lib/nav-items.ts` `NAV_GROUPS` (–, Content, Schedule, Team), `BOTTOM_ITEMS` (Home, Shorts, Long, Calendar) + More sheet (`MORE_ITEMS` + Settings, What's new, Log out) in `sidebar-nav.tsx`; active item has an amber bar. **Logo**: `components/ui/clip-logo.tsx` (`ClipLogo`, `Brand`) = Clip cropped to the board, static, claps on hover (`.brand-row:hover .clip-logo-arm`); `app/icon.svg` is the same drawing (browser tab). **Tailwind**: `opacity.12` added (22 `/12` tints were silently missing). Confirm dialog takes `cancelLabel`. `sendAlertEmail` takes `footer`.

- v1.4.0 (round 9): **Analytics** at `/analytics` (nav group "Insights"; also in the phone More sheet, which is now a 2×2 grid). Page `app/(dashboard)/analytics/page.tsx` reads `?tab=production|audience|content|revenue&range=7d|28d|90d|12m&compare=0` and loads ONLY the open tab's data; production windows end today, platform windows end yesterday (platforms report a day late); team time zone from `teams.timezone`. `modules/analytics/lib/ranges.ts` (RANGES, windows + the same-length previous window, buckets: days ≤31, Monday weeks ≤120, else months labelled "Oct ’25"). `lib/queries.ts` (server-only): `getProduction` (from our own tables: shorts posted / long videos published, on-time % vs planned day, median idea→posted days, overdue now, average days per step from stage events (shorts) and tasks (long videos), people (steps done, on time, on their plate), automatic-posting success per platform, the posted list), `getAudience`, `getContent`, `getRevenue` (+ the access list for masters), `getPlatformStatus`. Components: `analytics-view.tsx` (tabs, range + compare controls, Export CSV per tab with a BOM so Excel reads UTF-8, Sync now for masters/schedulers), `charts.tsx` (hand-made SVG: `LineChart` with crosshair tooltip + arrow keys and a dashed previous-period line, `StackedColumns` (≤24px columns, 4px rounded top, 2px gaps), `BarList`, `StatTile` (delta with arrow + sign, `good` direction, % or points), `ChartCard`, `Legend`), `world-map.tsx` (Natural Earth 110m TopoJSON from jsDelivr `world-atlas@2`, loaded in the browser once, decoded by hand, Equal Earth projection, Antarctica dropped, 5 quantile steps of amber, hover tooltip with share; if the CDN fails, the list beside it still has every number). Views chart has "By platform | Together" (Together = total vs the dashed previous period). Chart colours are CSS vars validated for colour-blind separation in light and dark: `--chart-yt/ig/tt/short/long` in globals.css. **Sync**: `modules/analytics/lib/sync.ts` `syncTeamAnalytics(team)` / `syncAllTeams(budgetMs)`: YouTube Analytics API (daily totals, Shorts vs long via `creatorContentType`, countries per day, channel subscribers/total views, latest 50 uploads with stats, estimated revenue when the monetary scope was granted), Instagram API with Instagram Login (`/insights` total_value per day with a metric fallback, followers, follower_demographics by country, latest 25 posts + their insights), TikTok (`user.info.stats` totals + `video.list` up to 10 pages; daily deltas come from the snapshots). First successful sync backfills 90 days (countries 28), later runs refresh the last few days. Videos are matched to our shorts/long videos through `social_posts.external_id/permalink`, `short_video_posts.post_url`, `long_video_projects.youtube_video_id`, `long_video_posts.url`. Daily Vercel cron `/api/cron/analytics` ("10 5 * * *", `maxDuration` 60, 50 s budget) + `syncAnalyticsNow(teamId)` (masters/schedulers) and `setRevenueAccess(teamId, userId, on)` (RLS: masters only) in `app/(dashboard)/analytics/actions.ts`. **Stats permissions**: `STATS_SCOPES` in `lib/social/providers.ts` (YouTube `yt-analytics.readonly` + `yt-analytics-monetary.readonly`, Instagram `instagram_business_manage_insights`, TikTok `user.info.stats` + `video.list`), added to the sign-in only for platforms in `SOCIAL_STATS_PLATFORMS`; `hasStatsScopes()` decides "reconnect to allow stats". Existing connections must be reconnected once. **Sidebar icons**: grey at rest, their colour on hover and on the open page (`ACCENT` in `sidebar-nav.tsx`: shorts orange, long blue, meetings violet, the rest amber). `StatTile` takes `className`.

- v1.5.0 (round 10): **Staging reconnect fix**: `redirectUriFor()` ignores `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL` when `VERCEL_ENV === "preview"` (it was sending staging sign-ins back to production, where the one-time state doesn't exist); `appUrl()` (emails) uses `VERCEL_BRANCH_URL` on previews. The callback passes the platform's own refusal text through (`social_error=refused&message=…`; `access_denied` stays "cancelled"). **Setup check** (`lib/social/setup.ts` `getSocialSetup()`, shown in `connected-accounts.tsx` for masters/schedulers on the accounts tab): environment + host, SOCIAL_TOKEN_KEY set?, per platform: keys set?, analytics permission asked?, the exact return address (copy button) and where it's registered in each console. Account cards say "Reconnect once to allow Analytics" when `SOCIAL_STATS_PLATFORMS` includes the platform but the stored scopes don't (`statsMissing`). **World map**: the country shapes ship with the app: `modules/analytics/lib/world-shapes.ts` (generated: Natural Earth 1:110m via github.com/johan/world.geo.json (Unlicense), pre-projected with Equal Earth into 1000×439, `{c: alpha-2, d: path}`, Kosovo = XK, Antarctica dropped; ~88 KB, its own chunk via dynamic import); `iso-countries.ts` and the jsDelivr fetch are gone. `WorldMap` takes `empty` (message over a grey map) and `compact`; the Audience tab always shows the map card ("Where your audience is": YouTube views | watch time | Instagram followers). **Country sync** no longer stops at the first error and no longer sends `maxResults`; each run refreshes the last 4 days and fills any day of the last 28 that has no rows; the first error is reported in the Sync now summary (`SyncResult.note`). `getAudience` reads one day past the range so today's follower snapshots count as "followers now". **Dashboard analytics widgets** (`modules/dashboard/components/analytics-widgets.tsx`): `output` (This week: shorts/long out, on time, overdue; own data), `views`, `followers`, `topVideos`, `audienceMap`. They load their own data through server actions `loadDashAudience` / `loadDashProduction` (in `app/(dashboard)/analytics/actions.ts`; membership checked first) with a shared 5-minute client cache, so the dashboard page doesn't pay for them unless one is on the board or the library is open. Not in DEFAULT_LAYOUT (added from Customize → Add widget). **UI refresh** (no gradients anywhere except the skeleton shimmer and the official Instagram logo): calendar chips, shorts rows, thumbnails placeholders, review player and search results use solid tints; `--violet` is 88 58 200 in light (7.4:1) and 152 130 245 in dark (5.8:1), solid violet buttons use `dark:text-paper`. Long videos list: title "Long videos", summary line, "New long video" button like shorts, neutral thumbnail placeholder, dark stage pill with a state dot, a 8-segment pipeline strip on each card, due date coloured by urgency (amber ≤7 days, red late, green posted). Shorts list: stage tabs with colour dots, second line only when useful (pin for fixed dates, Overdue, the reviewer's "Changes:" note; `ShortListItem.reviewNote`), compact Mark done is an outline. Short page meta: date chip (pin when fixed) + editor. Orange "your turn" boxes are `border-amber/40 bg-amber/[0.06]` everywhere. `/videos/new` action bar is a solid sticky bar. New icon `PinIcon`. **Mascot** is now called **Clippy** (aria labels, logo tooltip "Hi, I'm Clippy!"). **scripts/dev-mock/**: a stand-in Supabase with sample data so the real pages render locally for checks/screenshots (see its README).

- v1.6.0 (round 11): **Connecting**: Connect/Reconnect open `/api/social/[platform]/connect?…&popup=1` in a pop-up window (falls back to the same tab if blocked); the connect route sets the `vp_social_popup` cookie (path /api/social, 15 min; name in `lib/social/popup.ts`) and the callback then answers with a tiny page that sends the result by BroadcastChannel("vp-social") + a localStorage "vp-social" event + `window.opener.postMessage` (Google/Meta cut `opener`, so the first two matter) and closes itself (or goes to the accounts tab). `connected-accounts.tsx` listens, de-duplicates, toasts and `router.refresh()`es; the same-tab path keeps `?tab=accounts`. **Required permissions**: `REQUIRED_SCOPES` / `missingRequired()` in `providers.ts`; the callback refuses a sign-in with unticked required boxes (`social_error=missing_permissions&message=<labels>`) and keeps the old connection; a missing Analytics box connects with `note=stats_missing`. Reconnecting a DIFFERENT channel/account/Page clears that platform's analytics rows. The whole callback is in one try/catch. **Hydration**: client components show relative times with `<Ago iso>` (`components/ui/ago.tsx`: `suppressHydrationWarning`, refreshes every 30 s); `relativeTime()` stays for server components. **Setup check** only when `VERCEL_ENV !== "production"`. **Facebook** (analytics only, never posting): `PLATFORMS` now has `facebook` (`POSTING_PLATFORMS` = the three we post to; the short page's posting panel and /posting filter social_accounts to those). Provider: Facebook Login dialog (`auth_type=rerequest`; `config_id` when FACEBOOK_LOGIN_CONFIG_ID is set, else `scope=pages_show_list,pages_read_engagement,read_insights`) → long-lived user token → `/me/permissions` → `/me/accounts`; the first Page with the ANALYZE task is saved (access token = Page token, refresh_token_enc = the user token, used to list/switch Pages: `listFacebookPages` / `chooseFacebookPage` in team/actions.ts; switching clears the old Page's numbers). Page tokens don't expire (refresh is a no-op). Sync (`syncFacebook`): one insights call per metric so a retired metric can't blank the rest: `page_media_view` (views; replaced impressions Nov 2025), `page_post_engagements` → `engagements`, `page_daily_follows_unique` / `page_daily_unfollows_unique`, `page_video_views` (stands in for views if media views are refused); day = end_time − 12 h; `followers_count` snapshot on today's row; `published_posts` (25) with `insights.metric(post_media_view)` (retried without insights). No country data (Meta retired page_fans_country). **Audience is per platform now**: `Audience.perPlatform[p]` = views / engagement / watch hours (YouTube) per day + prev, followersNet + prev; `totals` stays for the widgets. TikTok day maths fixed: views ON day D = total copied the morning of D+1 − total of D (the query reads one day past the range); `Audience.tiktok` carries lifetime totals + how many copies exist; a note explains the first day. The Audience tab's status chips are the filter (`PlatformFilter`): All together / one / several, remembered per device (localStorage `vp-audience-platforms`); KPIs, the views chart (By platform | Together), Views by platform, the shorts/long card (YouTube) and the CSV follow it. Sync now summary shows TikTok video/follower counts and Facebook metrics/posts. **Chart colours re-picked for 4 platforms** (validated, all pairs, light + dark): YouTube 200 48 46 / 224 86 79, Instagram 163 62 168 / 185 91 192, TikTok 15 156 136 / 31 168 147, Facebook 31 111 224 / 61 133 238 (`--chart-fb` new). `StatTile` shows a change that rounds to 0.0% as no change. **Colour themes** (`components/ui/palette.tsx`): presets Clippy (default, no attribute), Ocean, Forest, Lagoon, Grape, Berry, Sand; CSS in globals.css as `html[data-palette="x"]:not(.dark)` / `html.dark[data-palette="x"]` overriding `--paper`, `--paper-2`, `--surface`, `--surface-2` and `--amber` (the accent; white text ≥ 3.4:1 in every theme). Kind, chart, status and meeting colours never change. Saved on `profiles.palette` (`setPalette` in settings/actions.ts; before 0060 it says "saved on this device only"), cached in localStorage `vp-palette`, applied before paint by the head script in app/layout.tsx and kept in sync by `<PaletteSync>` in the dashboard layout (palette read in its own query so a missing column can't cost the profile). Picker: Settings → Preferences → Colours (light + dark preview per theme).

- v1.7.5 (round 13): **Script flow** (0062): `modules/scripts/lib/flow.ts` (`getScriptFlow(docs, team, scripterIds)` → steps with people / usesDefaults / last hand-off, `isStepPerson`), `components/script-flow.tsx` (`FlowStrip` under the script top bar via the new `ScriptEditor.subBar` prop: Script → Review → Staging chips with avatars and a ✓ when sent, People dialog (scripters with the existing scripter actions; Review / Staging with `setScriptPeople`, "Team default" badge, "Use the team's defaults"), "Ready for review / staging" + confirm dialog (who's notified, copy-when-empty checkbox, "Sent to review · 2h" afterwards). Actions in `scripts/actions.ts`: `setScriptPeople`, `handOffScript` (RPC, then notifications kind `script_handoff` + one email each). Pages pass `flow` to `ScriptWorkspace`; `canEdit` = scripters/masters OR this doc's step people; the old ScriptersButton shows only before 0062. `DocListItem.step` (read with a fallback before 0062). Team → Defaults → Scripts (`team/script-settings.tsx`). Bell renders `script_handoff`. **Currency**: `lib/fx.ts` (`getFxRates()` from open.er-api.com, cached 6 h, ~160 currencies; `convert()`), `getRevenue(…, wanted)` converts every stored amount (YouTube rows in ANALYTICS_CURRENCY, income in its own) into the picked currency at today's rate; `Revenue.currencies/fx/fxNote`, entries keep `original`. `setRevenueCurrency` saves on the profile + a `vp_currency` cookie (works before 0062). Revenue tab: "Amounts in" searchable picker (Common first) + the required "Rates By Exchange Rate API" credit; Add income uses the shown currency. **Globe**: `components/globe.tsx` (canvas, orthographic projection, horizon clipping with rim arcs, far side faintly through the sea, graticule, drag + coasting, slow auto-spin off with reduced motion and when scrolled away, hover / tap card, arrow keys, `focus` turns to a country), data `lib/world-globe.ts` (generated from the same Natural Earth file: lon/lat in 0.1°, delta-encoded, 55 KB, own chunk). `components/audience-map.tsx`: modes All platforms / YouTube views / watch time / Instagram / TikTok / Facebook followers (`layerFor`, All = average share per platform, hover card lists each platform), `AudienceMapView` (map or globe + list). Audience card: mode dropdown + Map | Globe (remembered: `vp-map-mode`, `vp-map-view`); hovering the list turns the globe. Widget `audienceMap` settings `{ view, mode }`. `Audience.followerCountries` (instagram/facebook/tiktok; TikTok's Display API doesn't share countries). Facebook sync reads `page_follows_country` (falls back to `page_fans_country`) into analytics_countries (followers, latest copy). **Themes**: 12 (Default (was Clippy; id "default"), Sand now gold, + Cherry, Orchid, Lime, Mocha, Slate), white text ≥ 3.4:1 on every accent except Default's 3.38. The mascot's visible name is gone (tooltip "Hi there!") until the new name is chosen. **Other**: native selects replaced by `Select` (income source, Copy from…, studio region); `Select` search ranks best matches first and drops group headers while filtering; `AnchoredMenu` ignores clicks/scrolls inside `[data-floating-menu]` (Select's portal); resolving script comments / ideas asks first; sketch Edit drawing / Remove are solid pills; studio rows: inset ring, more padding, bordered star/delete; `ChartCard` controls wrap under the title on phones (title `basis-52`); What's new treats any release with "new" items as a feature update and shows "1.7.5" in full. **dev-mock**: `/fx/latest/USD` sample rates (start the app with `FX_RATES_URL=http://127.0.0.1:54321/fx/latest/USD`), script docs for short #231 (`MOCK_SENT=1` = already sent), Facebook follower countries, `MOCK_GLOBE=1` (widget as globe, all platforms), `MOCK_CURRENCY=EUR`.

- v1.9.0 (round 15): **Brand in one place**: `lib/brand.ts` (APP_NAME, APP_SHORT_NAME, MASCOT_NAME "Clip", APP_TAGLINE, APP_DESCRIPTION, COMPANY_NAME, CONTACT_EMAIL, BRAND_COLOR); every user-facing "VPlanner" (pages, emails incl. the email header, push, ICS PRODID, error alerts, platform messages) reads it, so a rename = edit brand.ts (+ domain env). Comments and old changelog entries still say VPlanner. **Installable app (PWA)**: `app/manifest.ts` (standalone, start_url /dashboard?source=app, amber splash, shortcuts), icons in `public/app-icons/` (icon-192/512, maskable-512, apple-touch-icon 180, badge-96 monochrome; rendered from app/icon.svg on amber #E8630D), root metadata `appleWebApp` + apple icon, `components/pwa.tsx` (`PwaRegister` registers /sw.js; `PushKeeper` in the dashboard layout re-registers this device after logging in again when `localStorage vp-push-user` = this user and the server's hashed cookie doesn't match; `InstallApp` = beforeinstallprompt button / iPhone Share → Add to Home Screen steps / Android ⋮ → Install app; `PushSettings` on/off/test + blocked/needs-install/not-configured states with friendly errors; `PushKeysHelper` makes VAPID keys in the browser with WebCrypto). `public/sw.js` (VERSION constant: bump on change): push → showNotification (icon, badge, data.url, no tag unless sent), notificationclick focuses an open window and navigates or opens one, pushsubscriptionchange re-subscribes and POSTs, navigation fetch falls back to the cached `/offline.html` (a route handler returning self-contained HTML: it must work without Next's scripts). Middleware matcher skips `sw.js`, `manifest.webmanifest`, `app-icons/`; public routes add `/`, `/data-deletion`, `/offline.html`. next.config: security headers on every path (nosniff, Referrer-Policy strict-origin-when-cross-origin, X-Frame-Options DENY, Permissions-Policy camera/mic/payment/usb off + geolocation self (weather widget), HSTS 1 year includeSubDomains) and `/sw.js` no-cache. **Push**: `lib/push/web-push.ts` (RFC 8291 aes128gcm + RFC 8292 VAPID ES256 with node:crypto, no package; `isPushServiceUrl` allows only fcm.googleapis.com, updates.push.services.mozilla.com, web.push.apple.com / *.push.apple.com, *.notify.windows.com over https without ports), `lib/push/send.ts` (`pushToUsers`: batches of 10, ok → last_sent_at + failures 0, 404/410 → delete, else failures+1 and delete at 8; `queuePush` runs it in `after()`; `deviceLabel(ua)`), `lib/push/guard.ts` (`sameOrigin` requires a matching Origin header; cookie `vp_push_ep` = sha256(endpoint) base64url, httpOnly). `sendNotifications()` now also queues a push per row: title = metadata.team.name or APP_NAME, body = the notification text, url = `notificationUrl()` (`lib/notification-url.ts`, shared with the bell: metadata.href (in-app paths only) → short → project + stage → else `/dashboard?notifications=1`, which opens the bell). Routes: `POST/DELETE /api/push/subscribe` (signed in, same origin, 20/min, keys validated: p256dh 65 bytes, auth 16), `POST /api/push/test` (once per 15 s). `signOut` deletes this device's row (cookie hash) before signing out. Settings → **Notifications & app** tab (install card, notifications card, your devices with "This device" and Remove via `removePushDevice`). The bell shows "Get these on your phone too" when this device isn't on. **Public pages**: `/` = home page for signed-out visitors (signed in → /dashboard), `/privacy` (rewritten: everything stored incl. analytics/revenue/push/error logs, YouTube API Services + Google Privacy Policy + Limited Use + revoke link, Instagram/Facebook/TikTok use + revoke paths, processors, cookies, retention, rights), `/terms` (platform terms incl. "agree to be bound by the YouTube ToS"), `/data-deletion` (Meta/TikTok data deletion instructions). Shared `lib/legal.ts` (LEGAL_UPDATED, contact from NEXT_PUBLIC_CONTACT_EMAIL). **CI**: actions/checkout@v5, setup-node@v5, `ubuntu-24.04`, Supabase CLI from its release tarball (no Node 20 action), plain-language error for a wrong DB password (`set -o pipefail` + tee). Tests: `tests/web-push.test.ts` (RFC 8291 Appendix A byte-for-byte, round trip, VAPID verify, push-service allowlist). **dev-mock**: `MOCK_PUSH=1` (two devices), `pub.cjs` (signed-out shots), sweep covers the new pages.

- v1.9.1 (round 16): **Staging done** (0066): `modules/scripts/lib/flow.ts` reads `to_step` and returns `flow.done {at, by}` (done rows don't count as a doc's "sent"); `FlowPermissions.finish` (both script pages: scripters / masters `canEdit`, or any step person); `FlowStrip`: ticks on every step when done (pop animation, staggered), a green "Done" chip at the end of the strip, on the Staging doc a green "Staging done" button → `finishScript(id, true)` (sound celebrate through the toast, `DoneBurst` overlay: ring + tick draw + sparks, 2.1 s, CSS `.done-check` in globals.css) and, once done, "Staging done · ago" + Undo (confirm "Take back done?"). Local `doneNow` state shows it at once and resets when the server's `flow.done.at` changes. `finishScript` (scripts/actions.ts): RPC → refreshDocs → notification kind `script_done` (push follows automatically; no email by owner's wish), bell case with a green "Script ✓ Review ✓ Staging ✓" pill. **Onboarding**: `lib/auth-emails.ts` (templates), `app/welcome/` (page + `welcome-card.tsx`, public in middleware), `app/set-password/` rewritten as 4 steps inside a `ToastProvider` layout, `/login?reset=1`, AuthHashHandler handles `#error…` hashes. **App setup** `app/(dashboard)/setup/` (alert people only, else 404): 1 Supabase URL Configuration (Site URL = origin, Redirect URL `origin/**`), 2 the five email templates (Copy subject / Copy body / sandboxed preview iframe), 3 platform return addresses (from `getSocialSetup()`, which now also returns `origin`), 4 website / privacy / terms / data deletion / authorized domain / JS origin + where each goes in Google, Meta, TikTok (TikTok URL properties: verify the Domain with a DNS TXT in cPanel), 5 every env var with status. Linked from Settings → Account for the alert people. **Domain readiness**: `canonicalRedirect()` + `STAGING_URL` (see Infrastructure), `appUrl()` uses STAGING_URL on the staging branch. **Log out confirmation**: `components/ui/logout-button.tsx` (useConfirm: "Log out?" / Stay signed in) replaces the three `<form action={signOut}>` (header, More sheet, Settings → Account). **Phone fixes**: dashboard Customize header wraps (greeting full width, toolbar `w-full sm:w-auto`, Save pushed right); audience map default mode "all" (analytics view, widget fallback, Studio select, `layout.ts` default settings; saved widgets keep their stored mode). PushSettings status dot no longer squashes. Tests: `tests/canonical-redirect.test.ts`, `tests/auth-emails.test.ts`. dev-mock: `MOCK_DONE=1`, `script_finish` RPC, `team_invites` table.
- v1.8.0 (round 14): **Tasks for everything** (0063): `modules/dashboard/lib/queries.ts` `TaskLook = TaskKind | "meeting"`; `extrasFor()` loads the script docs (step, name, video) and meeting actions (text, meeting title) behind tasks; script tasks read "Review the script" / "Stage the script" → `/shorts|videos/<id>/script?doc=<doc>`, meeting tasks show the item text, "From <meeting>" and link to `/meetings/<id>`; `KindIcon` has "meeting" (violet). Analytics' production numbers only count short/long tasks. **Notifications added**: `meeting_action` on reassign, `meeting_action_done` (to whoever added it), `script_people` (added as reviewer/stager), `revenue_access`, `app_alert`; the bell renders all of them. **One account, one team**: the OAuth callback and `chooseFacebookPage` refuse an account whose (platform, external_id) is already in another team (`error=taken`, audit `refused_taken`; a 23505 from the new unique index means the same); the accounts tab explains how to move it. **Errors**: `lib/error-kinds.ts` (`isControlFlow`, `fingerprint`: ids/numbers/quoted values/query normalised; tested), `lib/errors.ts` (`reportError` → `record_app_error` RPC → notifications + one email per alert person when `alert` and production; `alertRecipients`, `isAlertPerson`), `instrumentation.ts` `onRequestError` (Node runtime only, written as an if-block so the Edge build drops the import; skips redirects/not-found), `app/api/errors/route.ts` (browser reports: same origin, signed in, 10/min/user, 12 KB), `components/error-reporter.tsx` (window errors + unhandled rejections, noise filtered, 5 per page) mounted in the root layout; error screens `components/ui/error-screen.tsx` used by `app/error.tsx`, `app/(dashboard)/error.tsx`, `app/global-error.tsx`. **Status**: `lib/status.ts` (`coreChecks` db/auth/storage with time limits, `jobChecks` from `status_checks()`, `vendorChecks` = Vercel/Supabase/Resend statuspage JSON, cached 2 min), `/api/health`, `/status` (public; recent errors + Mark fixed only for alert people; refreshes every minute), `lib/health-watch.ts` `watchHealth("timer"|"daily")` (the posting timer checks every 15 min, the analytics cron once a day; problems become alerts). Middleware lets `/status` and `/api/health` through without sign-in. Team page footer links Status. **Analytics catch-up**: `claimAnalyticsCatchUp(team)` in `sync.ts` (any stats-enabled account not copied for 30 h → claims by moving `last_run_at` with a conditional update; 10-min in-memory guard) and the analytics page runs `syncTeamAnalytics` in `after()`; `AnalyticsView catchingUp` shows "Getting the latest numbers…" and refreshes at 35 s and 70 s; otherwise "Updates by itself every morning." under Sync now. **Globe zoom**: wheel (native non-passive listener; lets the page scroll at 1× / 6×), + / − / 1× buttons, + and − keys, zooms toward the pointer, slower spin when zoomed, halo only at 1×; the globe itself is loaded with `next/dynamic` (ssr off) only when shown. **Performance**: `createClient` wrapped in `cache()` (one client per request, so the client-keyed caches work); middleware uses `auth.getClaims()` (verifies the JWT locally once Supabase uses asymmetric signing keys; with the legacy shared secret it still asks the server); dashboard layout in one round (palette in the profile select, team colours from `getTeamsAndCurrent`); `listTeamPeople`, `getStudioData`, `getMeeting` cached and parallel inside; analytics paging in parallel batches of 4; one batch on Team (13 reads), long video, short (common reads start before roles), calendar (team-wide reads start while the month is worked out; the 6 month questions in one go), shorts list / new short (queue refresh chained only before the reads that need it), both script pages, posting (health chained on membership), videos list. **Tooling**: `npm test` (`scripts/run-tests.mjs`, tests in `tests/`: dashboard grid, analytics ranges, chart scale, error kinds), `npm run db:test`, `npm run db:baseline`, `supabase/config.toml`, `scripts/db/` (baseline.sh, test-from-scratch.sh, README), lint clean (row menu radio items are `menuitemradio`). **dev-mock**: tasks of every kind, meeting + action items, `status_checks` / `record_app_error`, `app_errors`, `/storage/v1/bucket`, PATCH answers with matched rows, `MOCK_STALE=1`, `MOCK_STATUS_BAD=1`, `sweep.cjs` (every page, status, time, browser errors, error screens).

- v1.7.0 (round 12): **Facebook Page lookup**: `facebookPages()` in `providers.ts` reads `/me/accounts` first; when that's empty (Facebook Login for Business with "current Pages only" often returns nothing there) it asks `debug_token` (app token `APP_ID|APP_SECRET`) for the user token's `granular_scopes`, takes the `target_ids` of the pages_* / read_insights scopes (max 25) and opens each with `/{id}?fields=id,name,access_token,picture{url}` to get its Page token. If ids exist but no token comes back, the error says to also tick `business_management` in the Login for Business configuration. The Setup check lists Facebook as "Analytics permission: always". **Revenue, all streams** (migration 0061): the YouTube sync asks for `estimatedRevenue,estimatedAdRevenue,estimatedRedPartnerRevenue,grossRevenue` per day (falls back to estimatedRevenue alone) plus the Shorts / long split (`creatorContentType`); if the stream columns are missing (before 0061) the upsert retries without them. `getRevenue` returns `total` (YouTube), `other` (manual income), `all` (both), `rpm`, `bestDay`, `streams` (YouTube ads / Premium / "memberships, Supers & Shopping" = the remainder, then each income source; biggest first), `split` {shorts, long}, `entries`, `months` (YouTube / other / views per month, last 12), `perBucket` {revenue, other}. Actions `addRevenueEntry(teamId, {day, source, amount, note})` (currency from ANALYTICS_CURRENCY) and `deleteRevenueEntry(teamId, id)` (masters; RLS enforces it). Revenue tab: KPIs All streams / YouTube (estimated, best day) / Other income / YouTube per 1,000 views, stacked YouTube + other columns with a legend, "Where it comes from" list with %, Shorts vs long, the Other income card (Add income: date chip, source, amount, note; delete asks first), By month table, CSV with streams, months and entries. **Thumbnail Studio** (`modules/studio/components/studio.tsx`): slim header (back button, title, #number · video title; Details and Library on the right); thumbnails panel = a list of compact rows on xl (124px thumb, auto-growing title, ★ and delete; warnings shortened with the full reason as a tooltip) and a swipeable strip of tiles below xl with an editor for the one that's showing; the active tile is scrolled into view inside the strip without moving the page. Preview controls (layout, light/dark/both, compare, shuffle, fullscreen) live in the preview card's own toolbar; a "1/5 · Test A · title" bar with ‹ › arrows sits above the preview; shortcut hint at the bottom (lg+). **A/B test** = 2 or 3 winners: they get letters A/B/C (in list order) on every thumbnail, in the nav bar and in Compare; the panel shows an "A/B test on" banner (letters → numbers, "Compare" opens Compare with exactly the testers, a line about YouTube Studio's Test & compare); 1 winner shows "★ #n is the winner. Star 1 or 2 more to A/B test them."; none shows a hint. The long video Package card shows A/B letters on the thumbnails and an "A/B test on · A vs B" line. **dev-mock**: Studio fixtures (`MOCK_WINNERS=0..3`, 5 variations with a 4:3 and an over-2 MB one, 16 library videos), `createSignedUrls` returns links to generated sample SVG images, `offset` paging works (pages past 1,000 rows used to repeat the first page).

- 0094 (1.29.0): Outliers, which channels count. (1) `outlier_niches`: `langs` text[] (null = the team's own language, worked out from its titles; [] = any; at most 8 two-letter codes), `regions` text[] (two-letter countries, empty = anywhere), `only_group`, `kids` (insert / update granted, 0091's manager policies decide), `profile` / `profile_at` (server). `outlier_niche_channels`: `verdict` (ok | topic | lang | region | kids; null = not checked), `rel_v` (the fit version that judged it), `added_by`; the source check (found by its name) now allows 'audience'. `outlier_niche_hits.ok` (a found video counts only when it fits). `yt_channels`: `main_lang`, `lang_share`, `kids_share`, `top_cat`, `region_guess`, `profile`, `profile_v`, `profile_at`, `scan_depth`. `yt_videos.lang_region`. `outlier_settings.audience_at` / `audience_note` (server). Quota buckets `xunits` / `xsearch` (Explore's own). (2) `outlier_blocked` (team, channel, title, `added_by` stamped by `outlier_blocked_guard`, at most 1,000 per team; teammates see, add and remove). (3) `outlier_audience` (team, channel, views, videos, ours, top[]; the server writes, teammates read). (4) `outlier_members(team, niche, group_only)` / `outlier_member_hits(team, niche)` (service role): the channels and found videos that count (own channels always; found ones with `rel_v >= 2` and verdict ok, unless `only_group`; never hidden or taken out); `outlier_scope` uses them (same signature; scopes ids, channel, tracked, audience, group, niche). (5) Rewritten: `outliers_videos` (same columns; made for kids only where a niche takes them; hidden channels out except on their own page; q also matches the handle), `outliers_channels` (dropped and recreated with verdict, grp, counted, blocked, pending, main_lang, region; `p_f->>'only'` all | group | found | out | hidden | tracked | blocked; channels not read yet listed as pending), `outliers_video` (blocked), `outliers_channel` (blocked, audience, each niche's verdict / group / counted, up to 100 long videos), `outliers_overview` (the niche's choices, fit_v, team_lang, group_n / found_n / out_n; explore keywords / phase / group_n; blocked; audience_n), `outliers_corpus`, `outliers_due_channels` / `_videos` / `_thumbs`, `outliers_alert_candidates` (what counts only); new `outliers_audience(team)`, `outlier_kept_channels`, `outliers_channel_samples`, `outliers_set_profiles`, `outliers_set_hits`; `outliers_set_relevance` takes verdict and v. Tested: `supabase/tests/outliers-fit.test.sql`, from scratch + twice, `scripts/e2e/outliers.e2e.ts`.
- 0095 (1.29.1): Clearing notifications. `notifications.cleared_at` timestamptz (null = still in the bell), `grant update (cleared_at)` to authenticated (since 0022 a reader may change only `is_read`; the update rule `recipient_id = you` decides whose), partial index `notifications_inbox_idx (recipient_id, created_at desc) where cleared_at is null` for the bell. Tested: `supabase/tests/notifications-clear.test.sql` (yours only, read too, the words can't be changed, the bell's list, the index), from scratch + twice, `scripts/e2e/notifications.e2e.ts`.

## Key architectural patterns (apply these consistently going forward)
- **The admin-client pattern**: `createAdminClient()` (service role, bypasses RLS, `server-only`) is used only in server actions, only after verifying the caller, and **only with values the user could not have edited** — e.g. invite/transfer rows (users have no UPDATE on those since 0022), the verified user id, constants. Accept flows claim the row atomically (`update ... where status = 'pending'`). The original 0013 "RLS bug" was most likely the insert-then-`.select()` gotcha (the returned row must pass the SELECT policy, and `is_team_member()` can't see a row inserted in the same statement), not a session problem.
- **Privileged project actions derive the team from the project row** (`requireProjectMaster` in `videos/[id]/actions.ts`), never from a teamId sent by the browser.
- **Notifications are created only via `sendNotifications()`** in `lib/notify.ts` (admin client).
- **Structured notifications**: every notification-creating action populates `kind` + `metadata` (actor name/avatar, team name/logo/color, role names/colors, project/stage info) via shared helpers in `lib/notify.ts` (`actorMeta()`, `teamMeta()`). `NotificationBell` renders each kind distinctly.
- **`displayName(username, fullName, email)`** in `lib/avatar.ts` is the canonical way to get a person's display name — username first, then full name, then email prefix. Always pass all three in that order.
- **Decoupled invites**: `team_invites` (join a team) and `ownership_transfer_requests` (become the owner) are both request/accept-notification flows with 1-hour expiry, cancelable by the sender, and both notify the *sender* of the outcome too (accept or decline), not just the recipient.
- Middleware sets `Cache-Control: private, no-store` on every response — a documented Supabase/Vercel gotcha where a cached auth response can cause "works, then goes stale until re-login." Dashboard layout also has `export const dynamic = "force-dynamic"`.
- Sign-in emails link to `/welcome?token_hash=…&type=…` (1.9.1, `lib/auth-emails.ts`); verifying happens only on the button press. `AuthHashHandler` still handles the old flow and now also turns `#error…&error_code=…` (expired / used links) into `/welcome?error=expired`.
- `AuthHashHandler` (mounted in root layout) catches `#access_token=` hash-fragment redirects (used by dashboard-triggered invites and password-reset links, which can't use the app's own `?code=` callback route since they're not triggered by app code) — explicitly parses the token and calls `setSession()` directly rather than relying on the Supabase client's automatic hash-detection, which didn't reliably fire for `type=invite` links in testing.

- **Performance conventions** are documented in docs/DEVELOPMENT.md ("Performance conventions"; moved out of the README in 1.11.0) — parallel `Promise.all` queries, `cache()`d loaders, `getCachedUser()` reads the identity middleware already verified (sanitized `x-vp-verified-user-*` request headers), `useAction()` + `useOptimistic` for instant UI, realtime patches rows instead of refreshing pages, `loading.tsx` skeletons on every route (they must mirror the page: a new route or a changed page layout updates its loading.tsx in the same zip), `compressImage()` before every upload.
- **Who sees a problem (1.9.8)**: app-wide (the database, sign-in, files, the timers, analytics copying, email, the services) → public `/status` as a level only + full details and alerts for the developers on `/developer`; one team's (a failed post, an account to reconnect, one account's analytics failing) → that team's Posting page, never an alert to anyone. New checks get `publicPart: true` only if they're about the whole app; never pass `detail` to /status's client parts.
- **Breakpoints (1.9.6)**: Tailwind's screens come from `lib/breakpoints.ts`; code that checks a width uses `minWidth()` / `belowWidth()` from there (never a hard-coded 768/1024/1280). The dashboard switches on its own board width (container query + NARROW), keep the two in sync.
- **Outer loading screens show the inner page (1.18.0)**: coming from elsewhere, Next shows the outermost new `loading.tsx` first, so the outer ones (shorts, videos, analytics, meetings and their `[id]`) pick the deeper page's skeleton from `usePathname()`; a new nested page with its own loading.tsx gets a line in its parent's.
- **Instant feedback (1.9.5)**: every click must answer at once. Moves to another route get their `loading.tsx` (mirror the page; if the page has `?tab=` tabs, make loading.tsx a client component that reads the tab and shows that tab's skeleton). Moves that stay on the same route (`?tab=`, `?view=`, `?doc=`) go through `PendingNav` / `PendingLink navKey` / `PendingSwap fallbacks`, with the skeletons in a shared file next to the page. `NavProgress` + `instrumentation-client.ts` cover the bar and the React "lost ping" nudge for every navigation; use `startNavProgress(href)` only for moves that don't go through Next's router. Client parts that need the browser before they know what to show render a skeleton of their final shape, never an empty box.
- The project page takes its team from `project.team_id` (via cached `getProject()`), not the workspace switcher.

## Known loose ends
- All previous loose ends resolved (prod invite accept confirmed working, diagnostic error reverted, debug function dropped, `0000_testing_commands.sql` is the owner's personal scratch file → lives in gitignored `supabase/scratch/`).
- `types/database.ts` is still `any` — generate real types (planned alongside Supabase CLI setup).
- Schema drift from running migrations by hand: solved by the Database Action once each project is baselined (`baseline` through the last migration it has). Until then keep running new ones in the SQL editor on both projects.
- Hydration warning #418: the Calendar's post/meeting times were one cause (fixed in 1.12.2, useLocalFormat). Sweeps now also cover /developer (all tabs), /word and Settings → Preferences (47 pages). The one seen once on the dashboard path (Europe/Athens) hasn't come back; both time-zone sweeps (Kiritimati ro-RO, Los Angeles en-GB, 42 pages, with and without MOCK_POSTS) are clean.
- Components loaded with `next/dynamic` and rendered on the server get other `useId()` values in the browser: the Mascot's clip-path ids warn (hydration, attributes only, it draws fine) on the Projections and Objectives empty states (also in 1.27.5; the sweep lists them). Fixed for the Instagram logo (CSS gradient) and Analytics → Advanced (imported normally) in 1.20.0.
- `/does-not-exist` answers 200 with the 404 screen (the catch-all renders inside the streamed dashboard shell); harmless for a private app.

## Shorts module (Part 1 of the shorts plan)
- Routes: `/shorts` (table/cards, stage chips, "Assigned to me", "Overdue", inline Mark done + per-platform posted toggles), `/shorts/new` (date presets + same-day warning, "Create & add another"), `/shorts/[id]` (workflow actions incl. review loop with note, autosaving details card, posting card with live-post links, activity).
- Code: `modules/short-videos/lib/{constants,permissions,queries,dates}.ts`, `modules/short-videos/components/*`, actions in `app/(dashboard)/shorts/actions.ts`.
- Roles: Master everything; Scripter creates + edits basics while in Script; assigned editor marks done + file link; **Scheduler** (role id `publisher`, renamed in UI) marks posted + caption/file link.
- (Superseded) Social posting was first dropped, then built after all: connected accounts (0039), automatic posting (0040), health (0042/0043). Manual "Mark as posted" remains for unconnected platforms.

## Version history (newest first; package.json follows: fixes 1.x.y, features 1.x.0, milestones x.0.0)
The full, team-facing notes live in `lib/changelog.ts` (shown in the app under "What's new"); every zip adds its entry there AND a line here.
- **1.29.1** (Oct 21 2026): **Clear your notifications, and small fixes.** (0095; no new dependency) The first of the owner's Oct 21 21:20 list, one version each. (1) **Notifications**: the bell lists only the ones not cleared (`loadBell` in `lib/notification-select.ts`, used by the layout; a database without 0095 lists them all, `missingColumn` tells a missing column from other errors: 42703 / PGRST204). The X on a row (`clearNotification` → `lib/notification-clear.ts` `clearOne`; not on an invite or ownership request still waiting for an answer; shown on hover with a mouse, always on touch) and Clear all at the bottom of the bell (`clearAllNotifications` → `clearAllButQuestions`: everything not cleared, older ones too, except questions still pending; asks first with the confirm, which keeps the bell open: `data-confirm` on the confirm's root, ignored by the bell's outside click). Rows fold away (`.notif-item`, 200 ms, one timer per clear) and come back where they were if saving fails. Cleared elsewhere arrives over realtime (UPDATE with `cleared_at`). Empty bell: "You're all caught up." History keeps cleared ones. (2) **Team → Defaults → Short videos**: the first row and the default people use `repeat(auto-fit, minmax(11rem / 11.5rem, 1fr))`, so a half-width card (xl: two columns) wraps instead of spilling; the skeleton matches. (3) **Contributions**: the squares don't re-fit while a popup holds the page (`scrollLocked()`; `onScrollFree()` in `use-scroll-lock.ts` measures again once it's let go); with 1.29.0's scrollbar gap this ends the grid growing under the day's list. (4) **Profile header**: @name / joined and the XP lines `text-ink-soft` (were faint), the bio `text-ink/90`. (5) **Brand**: `select-none` on the logo and the side bar's nav (a double click painted the selection colour behind "ClipTroop"). (6) **Light mode over a wallpaper**: the top bar / phone top bar `paper / 0.62` with the picture lifted (`brightness(1.3)` in the blur), a see-through side bar keeps at least 0.7 of its pane, the phone tab bar 0.72 (`html:not(.dark):has([data-dash-bg])`, globals.css). The side bar's scroll bar (`.side-scroll`): grey, only while pointing at it. Scripts saved before 1.27.5 lost those once (links with other words, pictures, highlights need adding again). (2) **Writing**: `lib/script-keys.ts` (`ScriptKeys`, priority 50): Tab in a list sinks the item, after "1." / "a." starts a list, else inserts a tab character (`.script-doc` `tab-size`); Shift+Tab lifts or removes it. `ScriptOrderedList` (type 1 a A i I, `data-list-type`, inline `list-style-type`; input rule `^([aAiI])\.\s$`), nesting 1. → a. → i. (`lib/list-markers.ts`, pure). Word export: `new Tab()` and one numbering reference per list with its format and start. (4) **PDF**: `lib/print-script.ts`: printing builds a plain copy (`DOMSerializer`) as `#script-print-root` straight under `<body>` with every other child of body hidden (`html.vp-printing`), waits for pictures and fonts, prints; afterprint removes it. The old `visibility: hidden` + absolute print target made Firefox print blank pages for long documents. Clicking: a comment wins over a writing suggestion on the same word (`GrammarLayer skipInside`, the "Suggestion here" button), links have `cursor: pointer`. (6) **Long videos**: `deleteProject(id, _, { stay, confirmTitle })` (the typed name must match; removes the script pictures and sketches folders), `getVideoWork(id)` (what goes with it, who wrote in its scripts lately), `delete-video-dialog.tsx` (two steps: the summary with a warning when a script was edited in the last 15 minutes, then the name), `video-menu.tsx` on every grid card and table row (masters). Grid / table and step chips switch instantly with skeletons (`ViewToggle`, `FilterChip`, PendingNav / PendingSwap; `loading.tsx` picks the skeleton from the view cookie), shorts too. (7) **Dashboard**: resize from every edge and corner (`grid.ts` `resize` with `left` / `top`: the opposite edge stays; with the board's gravity a widget can't sit lower than the space above it allows), the Followers graph (`followersLine`, `AreaGraph` in a `GraphRoom` that draws only when tall enough; in a small box the graph wins over the platform chips, whose numbers move to the tooltip), the digital clock (`skin: "digital"`, seven segments), Top videos platform chips (saved in the widget's settings; up to 8 per platform from `loadDashAudience`), Contributions colours from every theme (`CONTRIB_THEME` = follow your own), Upcoming longs, RSVP on Next meeting (`RsvpButtons size="xs" fill`), the full-width Add a widget. Weather through `GET /api/weather` (`?q=` cities, `?lat=&lon=&units=`: two tries, 6 s timeout, Next's data cache 1 day / 15 min, coordinates rounded to ~1 km; the widget caches 6 h per place and retries once). `MonthCalendar` waits up to 1.5 s for the month's dots behind a skeleton (also before hydration) so days and dots arrive together. - **1.25.1** (Oct 15 2026): **0089 installs on Supabase.** The first push stopped at statement 82 (`create or replace function global_search … set pg_trgm.word_similarity_threshold = 0.45`): "permission denied to set parameter" (42501). Supabase runs migrations as `postgres`, which isn't a superuser; until pg_trgm's library is loaded in the session, Postgres doesn't know that setting and treats it as superuser-only (0024 and 0026 loaded it by building trigram indexes first; CI runs as a superuser, so it passed). Fix: a DO block before the function calls `<pg_trgm schema>.similarity('a', 'a')`. Checked as a role that isn't a superuser in a fresh session: refused without it, accepted with it. `scripts/db/test-from-scratch.sh` now refuses a migration from 0089 on that has `set pg_trgm.` before a `.similarity(` call (docs/DEVELOPMENT.md, Branches, CI and migrations). No app code changed. 0089 never got recorded as run, and it's safe to run twice, so the Database Action simply runs it again.
