- **1.23.3** (Oct 15 2026): **Strip stays a strip.** Going from Grid (zoomed out) or Pages (Wide or Full) back to Strip left text moved to the right or pushed down. `usePages` left its last measuring function in `remeasure` when it was turned off, and the editor calls `remeasure()` when the zoom changes, which it does with the view; the old function laid Strip out on the old pages (TipTap makes the editor `position: relative` in every view, so the blocks still measured). Now turning it off, or any change, empties `remeasure` and stops any late call (fonts arriving too). Checked with the real hook in the harness (`pages-hook` scene, `proto/views.mjs`): it failed before the fix and passes after.
- **1.23.2** (Oct 15 2026): **No more ghost drafts.** The unsent-comment check (`script-editor.tsx`, `DRAFT_KEY` in localStorage, the "Restore" box) ran again on every redraw of the workspace (its `onAddComment` is a new function each render), so it found the copy of the comment being written, or just posted (the copy is removed only once the save succeeds), or the one with a sketch just used, and the box stayed after posting. Now it runs once per document (`backupChecked`), never while a comment is being written, and the box goes when a comment is posted or discarded (`clearDraft`) or a new one is started.
- **1.23.1** (Oct 15 2026): **Pages you can write on.** (0088) (1) **Pages and Grid redone** (`modules/scripts/lib/pages.ts`, pure, tests/pages.test.ts: `placeBlocks(blocks, room)`: each block (top-level node; a list's items one by one) from the natural one-column layout onto a page, whole (Word's "keep lines together": no splitting between lines any more), the natural space between blocks kept except at a page's top, a heading with what follows, `breakAfter` (page break) → next page, a block taller than a page stays where it starts and its page runs longer (`extra`); `pageGeometry` (sheets one under another or 2 / 3 to a row, a row as tall as its tallest page), `shiftsOf` (dx, dy per block), `sameShifts`, `pageAt`). `modules/scripts/components/use-pages.ts`: `PagePlaces` (plugin `pagesKey`: node decorations `transform: translate()` per block, mapped through edits until measured again; `handleDOMEvents.mousedown` on the editor's own empty box → `caretNear`), `measureBlocks` / `measurePages` (offsetTop chains inside the editor (position: relative in paged views), so transforms never change what's measured: no feedback loop, nothing above the edited block moves), `caretNear(view, x, y)` (a click on empty paper or between blocks → the closest text on that page), `usePages(editor, { on, cols, paper, ratio, gap, zoom })` (measured in the frame after each change, before it's drawn; ResizeObserver on the editor and the paper; fonts). The old gap widgets (`PageBreaks`, `.pm-page-gap`) and the grid's copies (`pages-grid.tsx`, deleted: safe to delete on your side too, nothing imports it) are gone. Grid is the editor itself: the paper's box is page 1 (794 wide), other pages are translated to their place, the frame around it `overflow-clip` (the natural column is taller than the grid) and zoomed to fit (`transform: scale`, the paper no longer `mx-auto` when zoomed: Wide / Full used to sit off to the right). Sheets clickable (`data-sheet`, cursor text), page numbers, `--script-head-bottom` scroll margin. Commenters' photos sit in each page's own right margin (`faces[].left`), popovers and the composer are clamped to the whole frame (`frameW`). The suggestions button shows in Grid too. Printing undoes the moves. (2) **Page break** (`modules/scripts/lib/page-break.ts`: node `pageBreak` (block atom, `div[data-page-break].pm-page-break`), `setPageBreak` (`insertPageBreak`: in a top-level line split at the cursor (at its start the break goes before it, at its end a new paragraph follows (a heading's end → text)), in a list / quote / on an image after the whole block with a new paragraph), Mod-Enter (priority 1000, before HardBreak's), a toolbar button and one in the phone sheet; Strip shows a dashed line "Page break", Pages / Grid take no room (label when selected); `.pm-page-break { break-after: page }` in print; Word export `new PageBreak()`; every editor knows it (SidePage, and the comments list's copy, which now also knows highlights, text align and nested checklists: one it didn't know made the copy empty and every comment looked rewritten). (3) **Header** (`script-editor.tsx`): top bar + the flow strip (Script → Review, Ready for review) + the formatting bar in one opaque sticky block (`headRef`, `headStyle`: under the app bar at 56 px; phones: only the formatting bar stays, the rest slides under the app bar (z 15 < the app bar's 20); view-only on phones: not sticky); its real bottom is `--script-head-bottom` on the root (the documents list, the link window, a page scrolled to). (4) **Shortcuts**: `HeadingKeys` Mod-0 (text), Mod-1/2/3 (headings), Mod-Alt-0…3 still; the style menu shows `Ctrl+1` (⌘ on a Mac, `MOD`) and its value just the name. (5) **Suggestions**: `scoreColor` / `scoreInk` (score.ts: < 50 red, < 65 orange, < 75 yellow, < 85 light green, < 95 green, then `--teal`; `--score-*` in both themes) for the ring, the badge on the button and the corner button; `GrammarToolbarButton` has the score ring (30 px) next to it; `ScoreRing` sizes its stroke and number. Accept all (`takesAll`) takes every suggestion with a fix, its first one, spelling too, except sentence-wide ones and `name` ones (`looksLikeName(word, before)`: a capital not at a sentence start, all capitals, a capital inside, a digit; set in refresh); `acceptAll` from the end, skipping one that overlaps a fix already made (`applyOne` returns where its change starts); the panel says what's left and "Add to dictionary" for the names (`addWords`, one toast; `dictionary.add(word, { quiet })` returns whether it was added). (6) **Done ticks**: the tasks widget's Done tab and empty state, and the to-do checkbox, use the calendar's filled `--teal` tick (`PostedTick` look). (7) **Realtime** (`realtime-math.ts`: `countRatio(counted, own, days)`, `usableRatio` (≥ 1.03, ≤ 4, from the last 3 days), `ratioDays(since, now, tz)` (days after the one counting started, 3+ days ago, inside the 8 kept); `fillHours(..., { from, scale })` lifts counted hours and running-total estimates (not the platform's own days) and marks them `scaled`): the live job works the ratio out every 6 h (`ratioDue`: `analytics_live_series` 192 h summed per platform day next to `analytics_daily` content all) into `analytics_syncs.live_ratio`; `liveHours` applies it (`Coverage.ratio / ratioFrom / ratioTo`, `PlatformHours.lifted`); the realtime card and tab draw the extra fainter with `fainterNote` / `liftedWords` ("matched to YouTube's own count"); the Overview draws lifted hours solid and says it (`liftedTo`). Developer → Status: "Realtime next to the platforms' own count" per team and platform. Why: YouTube Studio said 182K for 48 hours where the counts added up to 132K; YouTube's own daily numbers have views the public counts don't. (8) **Live streams vs premieres** (`sync.ts`: `YT_VIDEO_PARTS` + status; `ytLiveTab(token, channelId)` (playlist "UULV" + the channel id's tail, 1 unit per 50, `complete`; a 404 counts as "no streams" only when "UULF" exists); live.ts `settleLiveTab`: videos that went live, ended 45+ min ago, public, live_tab null → read the tab once, set live_tab (true when on it; false only when the whole tab was read)). (9) **Insights** (`insights-math.ts`): no gap nudge for a kind left behind long ago (`since > max(45, usual × 8)` days), the gap's detail "Your last 11 came out about every 6 days.", a slump only from videos 10+ days old; `patterns.ts`: What works compares only the latest 200 settled videos of the last 18 months (`RECENT_MAX`, `RECENT_DAYS`) and drops a finding whose two sides were posted at different times (`sameTime`: middle posting times more than max(60 days, 30% of the span) apart). (10) **Charts**: the "videos posted" chip's fill and number (`--mark-short/long/live/post`, `--on-mark`: white on darker fills in light mode, a dark number on the light fills in dark mode). Tests: pages (rewritten), realtime-math (lift, ratio, days), insights-math (stopped kinds, young slumps), patterns (time balance, the window); SQL `live-tab.test.sql`; the pages checked in a real browser (Working notes).
- **1.23.0** (Oct 15 2026): **Scripting: write like a pro.** (0087) (1) **Writing suggestions, Grammarly's way** (`modules/scripts/grammar/`): four kinds (`types.ts`; correctness red, clarity blue, engagement green, delivery purple; `--gr-*` colours on :root, html.dark and the paper itself). `blocks.ts` (pure): `textBlocks(doc)` (each textblock's text with every character's position; a hard break is "\n", other inline nodes " "), `placeIn`, `batches` (blocks joined by blank lines, ≤ 6,000 characters a request), `splitBack`, `urlSpans`, `signature` (rule + the words + hashes of the 24 characters around them: what Dismiss remembers). `languagetool.ts`: `fromLanguageTool` (a LanguageTool match → a finding: the kind from issueType / category, Grammarly-style titles (`titleOf`: "Correct the spelling", "Add a comma", "Fix the agreement"…), `cleanMessage`, ≤ 3 replacements, a misspelling carries its `word` for the dictionary), `DISABLED_RULES` (quotes, dashes, arrows, ellipsis, paragraph-end punctuation, TOO_LONG_SENTENCE…). `style.ts` (ours, English, instant in the browser): wordy phrases, redundancy, a filler, passive voice with "by", long sentences (> 35 words, `wide`: a dotted line), "very / really + adjective" → a stronger word (the article follows: `articleFor`), clichés, repeated words (3+ in a block, or twice within 6 words) with synonyms, hedging (I think, I believe that, I feel like, I guess, I suppose, kind of, sort of, I just wanted to), slang unless the goal is Informal, casual words for Formal; `removalEdit` (taking words out cleanly: the space, a comma after an opening word, the next word's capital), `mergeFindings` (spelling and grammar first; sentence-wide ones never hide others). `score.ts`: `writingScore` (weighted per 100 words, 30–100), `countByCategory`. `client.ts`: `grammarVia()` (GET /api/grammar once a page), `checkText` (public: the browser POSTs to api.languagetool.org, `language` auto with preferredVariants en-US…, `level=picky` only for Formal; app: POST /api/grammar), staying under the free allowance (18 checks, 68 KB a minute). `plugin.ts` (`GrammarMarks`, `grammarKey`): suggestions with their positions in the plugin's state, drawn as inline decorations (`gr-mark gr-<kind>` + `data-gr`; sentence-wide ones `gr-wide` + `data-gr-wide`, because overlapping decorations share one span), moved through edits and dropped when their words change or a letter is typed against them (`follow`); meta `set` / `drop` / `active`; `version` only goes up when suggestions come or go. `use-grammar.ts` (`useGrammar(editor, {enabled, scriptId, teamId, me})`): refresh 250 ms after typing (ours + LanguageTool's answers cached by block text; a block not checked again keeps LanguageTool's earlier suggestions, moved with the edits; no redraw when nothing changed), LanguageTool 1 s after (changed blocks only, the one with the caret first; 429 → paused a minute; other failures → offline, retried after 30 s), `accept` (one transaction, removals through `removalEdit`, undoable), `acceptAll` (one clear fix each, never spelling (names), from the end), `dismiss` (localStorage `vp:grammar-dismissed:<doc>`), `pick`, `recheck`; settings in localStorage `vp:grammar` (on, language, formality, kinds off); the team's dictionary (read with the browser client, `addDictionaryWord` / `removeDictionaryWord` in `app/(dashboard)/scripts/grammar-actions.ts`, optimistic). `grammar-ui.tsx`: `SuggestionCard` (kind · title, the fixes showing the change word by word (`diffWords`), why, Dismiss, Add to dictionary), `HoverCard` (after 260 ms on a word, pinned by a click or a tap, follows scrolling, Escape), `GrammarPanel` (the score ring, the four kinds as filters with counts, a status line, Accept all, the list opening into cards (the next one opens after a fix), Goals: formality, language, kinds, the dictionary, turn off; credits LanguageTool and links its privacy page, as its free API asks), `GrammarFab` (round button above the Comments one, the badge in the most important kind's colour), `GrammarToolbarButton`, `GrammarLayer`. The browser's own spell check is off while suggestions are on; only people who may edit the document get them. `app/api/grammar/route.ts`: GET `{via}`; POST to `LANGUAGETOOL_URL` (your own server) or a LanguageTool account (`LANGUAGETOOL_USERNAME` + `LANGUAGETOOL_API_KEY` → api.languagetoolplus.com), ≤ 20,000 characters, 90 a minute per person and server. (2) **Links**: `modules/scripts/lib/links.ts` (pure: `webUrl` (http(s) only, no credentials), `hostOf`, `shortUrl`, `embedFor`: YouTube incl. Shorts and the start time (youtube-nocookie), youtu.be, Vimeo, TikTok, Instagram posts and reels, X, Spotify, Google Docs / Sheets / Slides / Drive previews, Loom, Figma); `lib/link-preview.ts` (pure: `isPrivateAddress`, `isLocalHost`, `parsePreview` (og / twitter / title / description / icons from the head), `frameAllowed` (X-Frame-Options, CSP frame-ancestors)); `app/api/link-preview/route.ts` (signed in; Node http(s) with a lookup that refuses private addresses on the address actually used, ports 80 / 443, ≤ 4 redirects each checked, 6 s, 600 KB, pages only; cached an hour, privately). `modules/scripts/components/link-ui.tsx`: `LinkCard` (on hover after 380 ms, or a click: icon, site, title, description, picture with a play badge for videos; Open here, New tab, Copy, Change, Remove), `LinkWindow` (a panel on the right, 46vw up to 760 px, Bigger; the site's player, the page itself when it may be framed (sandboxed iframe), else the preview with "Open in a new tab"; full screen on phones; the editor makes room for it). The editor never follows a link on a click (`openOnClick: false`); Ctrl / ⌘ + click opens a new tab. (3) **Pages, like Word**: `modules/scripts/lib/pages.ts` (pure: `pageBreaks(units, {height, gap, padTop, padBottom})`: a block that doesn't fit goes to the next page, or splits between its lines when its first line fits; a heading moves with the text after it; something taller than a page runs on; `sameBreaks`). `modules/scripts/components/use-pages.ts`: `PageBreaks` (widget decorations `span.pm-page-gap` as tall as the gap, where the page breaks; moved through edits until measured again), `measurePages(view, doc, paper, content, zoom, ratio, gap)` (units = top-level nodes and list items; positions as if there were no gaps (`natTop` / `natBottom`); lines from the characters themselves (`getClientRects` and a binary search for each line's first character, line boxes from line-height, `posAtDOM`), so lines out of view count too), `usePages` (only in Pages and Grid; after edits (120 ms), resizes, fonts; gives up after 6 redraws without an edit or a width change). Sheets are drawn behind the text (`data-sheet`, a number on each); the paper itself is transparent in Pages. (4) **Grid** (`components/pages-grid.tsx`): copies of the pages (the editor's own HTML, `inert`) scaled into 2 or 3 columns (2 under 520 px); a page opens in Pages at that page (`openPage`); the editor stays mounted out of sight (`grid-hidden-editor`) to lay them out. (5) **Widths** (`WidthMenu`, `vp:script-width`): one long page `max-w-3xl` / 1080 px / the whole width; Pages zoomed 1 / up to 1.25 / to fit (up to 2) with `transform: scale` (sizes from `offsetWidth`, `scaleRef`); the side page follows (`vp-width`). Views `vp:script-view` = strip | pages | grid (the old "spread" opens Grid), `vp:script-grid` = 2 | 3. Printing resets the zoom and the grid's hidden editor. (6) **Comments**: Delete next to Resolve in the popover from the text (`confirmDelete`; author or master, from the page's `me`); the commenter's photo in the paper's right margin on the line each open comment starts (`faces`: comments within 16 px share a row, +N after 3, ringed in the kind's colour, opens the popover); posting at once (`onPosting` / `posting`: the composer closes, the words get a "Posting…" tag (`PendingMark.label`), `CommentsChat` shows a loading card with the poster's photo until the saved one arrives (`posting.id`); on failure the text, kind and drawing come back). (7) **Headings** (`lib/headings.ts` pure: `headingLevel`, `fixHeadings`, `smallHeadingsToH3`; `lib/heading-keys.ts` `HeadingKeys`): the style menu sets the level (`setHeading`; picking the current one does nothing, it used to switch back to text), Ctrl+Alt+0–3 set too, levels are whole numbers 1–3 wherever they come from (load, Copy from, the side page), pasted h4–h6 become h3. (8) **Loading**: skeleton lines on the paper in the paper's own ink (`.script-paper .skeleton`); `PaperSheet` (the paper and shape the person picked); `(dashboard)/loading.tsx` follows the path (shorts → the shorts loading, videos → the videos loading), so "Write the script" from the dashboard shows the script's skeleton from the first moment; `notificationUrl` sends a scripter's notification to /shorts/<id>/script and a long video entering Script or Research to its script. (9) The right-hand panels take turns (`modules/scripts/lib/dock.ts`: comments, suggestions, a link). Tests: grammar, headings, links, pages; SQL `writing-suggestions.test.sql`; screens and the page breaks checked in the harness (Working notes).
- **1.22.0** (Oct 15 2026): **Analytics you can trust.** (0086) (1) **Live streams** (`lib/kinds.ts`: `VideoKind` short | long | live | post, `KIND_LABEL` / `KIND_SHORT_LABEL` / `KIND_PLURAL` / `KIND_TITLE`, `isWide`, `holdSeconds`; every screen and export uses them; `LIVE_ICON_PATH` / `LiveIcon`; `--chart-live`, `LIVE_COLOR`): `ytContentTypes()` saves YouTube's creatorContentType per video (`saveContentTypes`), the daily copy and history split 'live' (`SPLIT_CONTENTS`, zero-filled), `youtubeTypeAverages` maps LIVE_STREAM to live; Overview's YouTube card (Shorts, long videos and live streams), Content's Live streams tab, Advanced's "Live streams", Insights' live groups, reach totals per kind. (2) **Realtime** (`lib/realtime-math.ts` pure + tests: `totalAt`, `hourlyFromTotals`, `hourlyFromDays` (the day's views ÷ 24 in the platform's day: Pacific for YouTube and Facebook), `estimateHours`, `fillHours`; `lib/live-hours.ts` `liveHours(supabase, team, shown, count)` → per hour per platform views (counted, or estimated before `live_since` from `analytics_live_totals` / `live_totals` / the morning copy's `total_views`, else daily views), `estimated` flags, likes / comments / shares, coverage): Realtime's 48 hours add up (the realtime card and tab draw the estimated part fainter, `soft()`, with a note "every one is from …"); the live job keeps totals on every read, sets `live_since` on its first complete pass, reads YouTube's whole list every 3 h (was 6) and up to 150 hot videos, refreshes 50 videos' other numbers a call with the unnamed live streams first, and continues YouTube's history whenever a call has 22 s left. Hours read as stretches (`hourRange`: "Today, 5 PM – 6 PM"). YouTube's subscribers show as YouTube shares them (`fmt3`: "153K", `followersRounded`, a title explaining). (3) **Overview** (`lib/overview.ts`, `tabs/overview-tab.tsx`): the 48 hours from `liveHours(96)` (views with `est`, engagement, likes, comments, shares for TikTok / Facebook) plus the per-day-only numbers (watch time, followers, impressions, CTR, average view, revenue) for the last two days copied (`OvMetric.days`; cards captioned "Oct 10–11", chart as two columns); the same cards in every range; markers carry thumbnail and time; Lifetime says how far YouTube's history is copied (`history`), with the channel's all-time views. **Click any point** (any number, days / weeks / months, hours): `TrendChart` builds `PeriodPoint`s (stretch, title, the chart's totals per platform, estimated) → `components/period-videos.tsx` (`PeriodVideos` dialog: platform chips with shares, kind filter, sort by the number / views / newest, ← →, what other videos made, notes) → `GET /api/analytics/period` → `getPeriodVideos` (`lib/period.ts`; `lib/period-types.ts` shared with the browser): YouTube asked per video for exactly that stretch (`fetchYouTubePeriodVideos`, YouTube's top 100 by the number, money in the person's currency; kept 10 min per server), impressions / CTR and the other platforms from `analytics_period_videos`, hours from `analytics_live_window`; an old upload YouTube names that we don't have is added (`addMissingYouTubeVideos`). `DayVideos` / `loadViewsDay` are no longer used by the Overview. (4) **Charts** (`charts.tsx`): `PostedMark` (one icon per point: the kind's icon or the count; hover / focus / tap opens a list in a portal: "N videos posted", kind counts (`kindCounts`), picture, title, kind, platform, time (`markTime`), link to its analytics); points closer than the icon merge (phones); `gaps: "bridge"` joins missing days with a faint dashed line; `Series.est` draws estimated stretches dashed and says "estimated" in the tooltip. (5) **A video's page** (`video-analytics.tsx`): bigger picture (Shorts 168 × 299 at xl, long 420 wide), exact views with the score badge and rank, `usualWords()` ("This Short got 39,228 views. That's similar to the 23,900–39,700 you usually get by day 12": `VideoPageData.usual` = the 25th–75th percentile of the ones before it at the same age (their curves, first 60 days) else their totals), stats watch time / average % viewed / average view / likes / comments / shares / subscribers (gained − lost) / revenue (`.stat-grid` takes 7 and 8); the "Right now" box is gone (the realtime card has it); details asked again after 1 h (was 12); `canFill` only while the start is missing and `days_filled_at` is empty (a week-old video may ask again after 12 h; 3-year reach); subscribers net per day (`subscribers_lost`); Reach's CTR line and the per-day bars only cover their stretch (`DayLine` weighted weekly over 90 days, `DayBars` trimmed); a day with 0 impressions has no CTR (`reach-parse.ts`). The skeleton mirrors the new header. (6) **Revenue**: stored YouTube money is asked in the team owner's currency (`teamMoneyCurrency`; `moneyCurrencyChanged` refetches when it changes); `loadRevenueVideos` / `loadVideoRevenueDays` ask YouTube in the viewer's currency (YouTube converts per day, like Studio). (7) **Fewer calls** (`lib/api-access.ts`: `teamForRequest`, `personCurrency`, `json`): reads moved from server actions (which run one after another per tab) to GET routes: `/api/analytics/realtime`, `/api/analytics/period`, `/api/analytics/videos`, `/api/calendar/planned` (≤ 400 days); `useRealtime` asks only while visible, at most once a minute on changes (catching up on return), every 5 min otherwise; Posting's `AutoRefresh` only while visible; the missed-morning catch-up is `analytics_catch_up` (claimed in the database; in the page's `after()` only without the timer's secrets: "inline"); `MonthCalendar` caches ranges, prefetches the months around and is warmed by date pickers on hover / focus and by the Custom range panel; `topInRange` says once per server when 0084 hasn't run (PGRST202), instead of an error per page. (8) **Search**: `lib/search-words.ts` `searchWords()` (tested) → `findVideos` needs every word in the title (each an `ilike`, also on ClipTroop's titles), ranks the whole phrase first; the search bar reads `/api/analytics/videos` (an older search is aborted; a failure isn't remembered as "nothing"). (9) **Insights**: `BestSlots` (podium of the best 3 slots, hours as 16:00 – 20:00) and rank badges on the map. (10) **Reach**: `cursorAfter()` (the next run never skips a report made at the same moment as the last one saved), live streams' reach rows. (11) Another account connected: `analytics_live_totals` purged, `live_since` / `money_currency` reset. Tests: realtime-math, video-search, reach (cursorAfter, no impressions), live-math (hourRange), history-math (zero-filled kinds); SQL `analytics-accuracy.test.sql`; screens checked with the visual harness (Working notes).
- **1.21.0** (Oct 14 2026): **Analytics, finished.** (0085) (1) **Sync now in the background**: `syncAnalyticsNow` → `analytics_sync_now(team)` (one `posting_call` per platform with `live: true`, -1 within 90 s); `runAnalyticsJob` copies that platform, then its live counts when more than 8 s of its 52 s are left; before 0085 or without the timer's secrets, every platform in `after()`. Copying every platform inside the request ran past the request's limit and crashed the page; the button's call is also wrapped in try/catch now. (2) **YouTube's whole history** (`youtubeHistory()` in `sync.ts`; pure `lib/history-math.ts` `historyChunk` / `historyRows`, tests/history-math.test.ts): every copy goes back a year per report, newest first, to the channel's `snippet.publishedAt` (daily totals, Shorts / long, per-day splits, revenue), up to 8 s past the extras' deadline (`HISTORY_EXTRA_MS`), saving `history_from` / `history_done`; days before the first views aren't saved, days YouTube leaves out after it are 0; `adoptAccount()` resets both. Lifetime starts on the channel's first day with views (it started 90 days before connecting: "6.6M since Jul 14"). (3) **Whole-life windows**: `youtubeWindows` also fetches span 0 (external_id '', from 2005-04-23) and the new `country` dimension for 7 / 28 / 90 / 365 / 0; `lib/windows.ts` (pure, tests/windows.test.ts): `spanFor` (Lifetime or > 365 days → 0), `pickSpan` (nearest by ratio; 0 counts as 730 days), `windowWords`, `coversRange` (90% of the days), `youtubeCountries` (the days' own countries when they cover the range, else the nearest window, saying what it covers); `Window.lifetime` in `ranges.ts`. (4) **Overview** in two columns (`overview-tab.tsx`): chart, Shorts vs long, Top content, platforms | `RealtimePanel` (replaces `RealtimeMini`; bigger: subscribers now, 48 h and 60 min, videos getting views; on phones under the chart), the latest videos one at a time, hints. KPI cards in `CardStrip` (sideways, with arrows). Marks under the chart = the sidebar's Shorts / long icons in the team's colours (`MarkIcon`, `MARK_COLOR`, a count when several). Lifetime's Top content ranks `analytics_content.views`; `topInRange` logs RPC errors. The `inAccountTime` filter on the platforms' own videos is gone from Overview and Insights (it hid videos posted before the account was connected: "Nothing got views"). (5) **Realtime tab**: 48 hours / 60 minutes switch (`active48` / `active60`, `top60`, `reactions60` in `realtime.ts`; `perSlot()` in `live-math.ts`), each account's totals on top. (6) **Revenue**: click a day / week / month (`StackedColumns onPick`, `pickHint`) → `RevenueVideosDialog` (`components/revenue-videos.tsx`, loaded on demand) → `loadRevenueVideos` → `fetchYouTubeRevenueVideos` (YouTube's top 25 by estimatedRevenue for that stretch, titles via `ytVideos`, in the person's currency, kept 10 min in the server's memory, not stored; skipped when that stretch's YouTube revenue is 0); ← → step. (7) **Insights**: `Finding.sides` (`typicalOf` / `sidesOf` in `patterns.ts`, `EXAMPLES` = 5: the videos nearest each side's median views) for split findings, momentum and subscribers; Examples button + `FindingExamples` dialog (`advanced/findings.tsx`: 5 pairs, the finding's subject highlighted); `getInsightsPreview` drops `sides`; one Download for Claude button. (8) **A video's tabs** (`video/video-tabs.tsx`, `video/video-parts.tsx`, `video-analytics.tsx` rewritten; `?tab=overview|reach|engagement|audience|revenue|advanced`, `tabsFor()` per platform): header numbers with icons (views, watch time, likes, comments, shares, revenue; `ThumbUpIcon`, `CommentIcon`, `ShareIcon`, `MoneyIcon` in `components/ui/icons.tsx`); Overview (`TabbedChart`: views / watch time / subscribers / estimated revenue cards switch the chart, See more → Advanced; `LiveCard`: +views in 48 h in big type, hour by hour, the last 60 minutes; milestones; At a glance), Reach (impressions, CTR, views from impressions, traffic sources, searches, sites, suggesting videos, playlists), Engagement (watch time and average view per day, retention, likes / dislikes, comments, shares), Audience (subscribed, ages, countries, devices, OS, where it played, sharing), Revenue (per day, RPM, CPM: `loadVideoRevenueDays` → `fetchYouTubeVideoRevenueDays`, ≤ 3 years, 10-min memory cache, not stored), Advanced (as before). On open, a YouTube video whose `device` split is missing or over 12 h old calls `loadVideoDetails` → `fetchYouTubeVideoDetails` (`youtubeVideoSplits`: 12 dimensions as span 0 with its id; `youtubeVideoDays` with `subscribers_gained` / `avg_view_seconds`; at most every 10 min per video and server), then refreshes. `getVideoBreakdowns()` in `advanced.ts`; `video.ts`: `breakdowns`, more per-day series, `live.minutes`, `contentDayCols()` probe. (9) **Speed**: explicit column lists (`dailyCols()`, `INSIGHT_COLS`, `contentDayCols()`; `metrics` is up to 8 KB a row), Compare switched in the browser (`replaceState`), the clicked tab / range marked at once (`pending` + spinner), `claimAnalyticsCatchUp` looks at most every 5 min per team and server. (10) **Even grids** on Audience and Advanced (`byCardHeight`, cards `h-full`, long lists start short with Show all). (11) **A video's header at every width** (a bug since 1.17.0: from 640px the picture, the title and the verdict sat side by side, so on tablets and small laptops the title broke letter by letter): the verdict (and realtime) sit beside only from xl for long videos (lg for shorts), else under it in a grid; a long video's picture is 240px wide from sm, 300px from lg, and never stretches (`self-start`). Its numbers: `.stat-cq` / `.stat-grid[data-n]` in globals.css (a container query: all in one row when there's about 100px per number, else even rows 3 + 3 / 3 + 2 / 2 + 2, else pairs); a currency written as its code shows the code small (`MoneyValue`). The live card shows only with counts. Its `loading.tsx` mirrors the new page (client component: the platform's tab names from the path). Mock: country windows for every span and 0, lifetime windows, per-video splits for the 12 newest, content days with subscribers / average view / impressions / CTR. E2E: `scripts/e2e/youtube-history.e2e.ts` (history to the first day, gaps as zeros, a year per report, a video's details, revenue videos, the Sync now RPC, the reset on another account). Tests: history-math, windows, ranges (lifetime), live-math (`perSlot`), patterns (sides).
- **1.20.0** (Oct 14 2026): **The new Analytics.** (0084) (1) **Tabs** (`components/analytics-view.tsx` shell + `components/tabs/*`): Overview (default) · Content · Audience · Realtime (green dot) · Revenue (lock when not allowed) · Insights · Advanced · Production; the page loads only the open tab's data (`AnalyticsData` union). (2) **Ranges** (`lib/ranges.ts`, tests/analytics-ranges.test.ts): 48h (Overview only; elsewhere it reads as 7d), 7d, 28d, 90d, 365d (`12m`), Lifetime (from the team's first `analytics_daily` day; Production: the team's first day), Custom (`?from=&to=`, `readCustom`: real days, from ≤ to, the end cut to the last day with numbers, ≤ 10 years; else 28d); `components/range-bar.tsx`: chips (a dropdown on phones), Custom opens a panel (two date chips, This month / Last month / this year / last year; the date popover carries `data-floating-menu` so picking a day doesn't close it). Realtime and Insights have no range. Compare hides on Content and Lifetime. (3) **Overview** (`lib/overview.ts` `getOverview`, `components/tabs/overview-tab.tsx`): KPI cards that switch the chart (views, watch time, followers = gained − lost or the change in the count, engagement, impressions, CTR weighted by impressions, revenue in the person's currency; more in "More numbers": likes, comments, shares, average view), a sparkline and change each; the headline ("Your videos got N views in the last 28 days", change, shorts / long videos posted); the trend chart (`LineChart` + `tips`, `area`, `marks`: Together / By platform / Shorts · long, Per day / Running total, Days / Weeks / Months by range (`gransFor`), the stretch before dashed, a mark per video posted (titles in the tooltip), click a day → DayVideos); 48h = hour by hour from `analytics_live_hours(team, 96)`, compared only when the 96 hours were all counted; **Shorts vs long videos** (YouTube: total, Shorts, long with shares and changes, the channel's all-time views from `live_totals` / the morning copy's `total_views`; stacked columns per day/week/month, Side by side, Share); Top content (streamed promise: `analytics_top_content`, before 0084 the most viewed posted in the range; 48h: `analytics_live_gains`); Realtime mini (`RealtimeMini`); Latest videos (the newest 6, × typical from the 300 newest); Insights preview (streamed `getInsightsPreview`); Views by platform. Changes next to "before" only when that stretch has numbers on 80%+ of the days (`fairBefore`). CSV: every number per day, all platforms and each. Pure math in `lib/trend-math.ts` (tests/trend-math.test.ts: `dailySeries` (TikTok views from the copy or the running total's change, likes from `total_likes`, followers gained − lost or the count's change, Facebook engagements), `bucketDays`, `rollup`, `runningTotal`, `weightedAvg`, `combineRates`, `change`, `gransFor`, `hourSlots`). (4) **Content**: a tab per kind (All · Long videos · Shorts · Posts, counts), totals (videos, views with shorts / long apart, likes, comments, impressions, CTR weighted), no own search box and no "Every video" switch: the range decides (Lifetime = `every`; old `?every=1` links open Lifetime). (5) **App search** (`components/search/use-video-search.ts`): a "Video analytics" section (`searchVideos`, 5 results: thumbnail, #number, views, × typical; Enter opens `/analytics/video/…?from=search`); "/" always opens the palette now; when `global_search` fails the videos still show. (6) **Audience** (who watches): followers now / net / gained / lost / watch time, "Followers gained" (Growth: one platform's own count when copied on most days, else what each gained since the first day; Per day), the country map, YouTube's and Instagram's audience splits (`getAudienceBreakdowns`, `buildBreakdowns` / `readBreakdowns` taken out of `getAdvanced`: age & gender, subscribed, devices, OS, where they watch, YouTube apps; followers vs not, follows, ages, genders, cities, engaged audience). `getAudience` adds `followersDaily` / `followersFlow`. (7) **Realtime tab** (`tabs/realtime-tab.tsx`, `useRealtime()` hook in `realtime-card.tsx`): views 48 h / 60 min, likes and comments 48 h (`reactions48`), the 48 hours per platform, the last 60 minutes in 10-minute bars (`getRealtime(team, {full})` + `analytics_live_buckets`), the 25 videos getting views (60 min, 48 h, posted), each account's totals, a platform filter. (8) **Insights** (`lib/insights.ts` + pure `lib/insights-math.ts`, tests/insights-math.test.ts): alerts (views ±20% this week on 300+ views, a video ≥ 2.5× its usual in its first 14 days, the last 5 settled ones all < 0.7×, a break ≥ 2× the usual gap (median of the last 10, regular posters only), YouTube CTR ±1 point on 1,000+ impressions, followers lost this week), What works for every platform and kind (`findPatterns`), the best time to post (weekday × 4-hour bands, median × typical, 12+ settled videos; cells with 1–2 videos stay pale), the cross-platform finding, a tip per finding (`TIPS`, `tipFor`); `FindingCard` / `PatternsBlock` take a tip. (9) **Advanced**: its own tabs (`?view=`: Summary, Day by day, Best performing, Retention, Traffic, Audience, Videos; switched in the browser with `history.replaceState`); What works moved to Insights; imported normally (next/dynamic gave `useId` other values in the browser than on the server). (10) The Instagram logo's gradient is a CSS background (no `useId` ids: hydration mismatches inside `dynamic()` components on Objectives and Team → Connected accounts). (11) `StackedColumns`: a column too short for its parts is one bar; `showTotal`. (12) Video page back links for `from=overview|realtime|insights|search`; the dashboard's views widget opens the Overview. (13) Download for Claude passes custom / lifetime days. Mock: `analytics_top_content`, `analytics_live_buckets`, `global_search` (empty), daily impressions / CTR (Shorts and long apart); `shot.cjs` dark mode uses `vp-theme`; the sweep covers the new tabs and ranges.
- **1.19.0** (Oct 13 2026): **Every video, within minutes.** (0083) (1) **The live job** (`modules/analytics/lib/live.ts`): the timer's `{job: "live", team, platform}` every 10 minutes → `runLiveJob` → `liveTeamPlatform(team, platform, {full?, budgetMs = 45 s})` (needs the morning copy's `last_ok_at` and the same account; never throws; writes `live_at` / `live_error`, `live_full_at` + `live_cursor` for full passes, `live_totals`, the reach fields). Each call reads the hot videos (YouTube: the newest 50 + the ones gaining views in the last 24 h (`analytics_live_gains`) and the week's, ≤ 100 = 2 units; TikTok: the first page + hot by id via `/v2/video/query/`; Instagram: the first 25 posts; Facebook: the first 25 Reels), and a full pass of EVERY video (YouTube's uploads list `UU…` (= the channel id with UU for UC; `channels.list` only every 30 min and on full passes, and when the worked-out list 404s), every TikTok (20 a page), Instagram posts and Facebook Reels (100 a page)) every 6 h (YouTube, Instagram, Facebook) / 3 h (TikTok), going on from `live_cursor` when a pass runs out of time (a stale cursor starts over). `saveLive` → `snapshotsOf()` (pure, `lib/live-math.ts`): counts into `analytics_live` when they changed or at a first look, plus the counts as last seen at `live_at` (or the morning copy's `updated_at`) inserted with ignoreDuplicates, so a change is spread over the time it happened in. Rolling details, the oldest `details_at` first: YouTube 50 a call (lifetime stats + extras + per-video revenue with the monetary scope + 3 retention curves / traffic sources via `youtubeVideoDetails`), Instagram (views, reach, shares, saves) and Facebook Reels (plays, reactions, comments, shares) 15 a call + 10 hot ones (Meta's rate-limit errors stop the call; other refusals mark `details_at`). Facebook Reels the morning copy keeps as Page posts (their link has the Reel's id) aren't added twice. Account totals go to `analytics_syncs.live_totals`, NOT to the per-day rows (whose day-to-day changes are what a day got). `LIVE_MINUTES` (20, 30, 60; `liveDue()`) skips calls for a lighter load. YouTube quota ≈ 600 units a day for a 1,000-video channel. (2) **Impressions and CTR** (`lib/reach.ts`, `lib/reach-parse.ts` pure + tests/reach.test.ts): YouTube Reporting API job `channel_reach_basic_a1` (made or found once per channel, `reach_job`), new reports downloaded once an hour (12 a call, `createdAfter` = `reach_after`), CSV → `analytics_content_days` (source daily: impressions-only days don't count as views days: `video.ts` filters them) and `analytics_daily` (all / shorts / long, CTR weighted), then `analytics_reach_totals()`. A refusal becomes `reach_note` (API off → "turn on the YouTube Reporting API"). CTR read as a share of 1, or as % when any value in a report is above 1. (3) **Realtime card** on Audience (`components/realtime-card.tsx`, `lib/realtime.ts` `getRealtime()` + `loadRealtime()` action): 48 hourly bars per platform (`analytics_live_hours`), last 60 min / 48 h (`analytics_live_gains`), top videos now, each account's totals and "N of M videos counted"; refreshes on `analytics_syncs` UPDATEs (realtime), every 2 min and on return; follows the picked platforms. (4) **Content**: "Published in the last N days | Every video" (`?every=1`, `getContent(..., {every, limit})`: every row via `all()`, the dashboard widget asks for 60), Impressions + CTR columns when any video has them (`contentCols()` probes the columns once per request, so pages work before 0083), 100 rows then Show more / Show all, the table's hidden labels no longer widen the page. (5) **Video page**: Impressions and Impressions CTR keys (typical, rank), `live` (`liveFor()`: its `analytics_live` rows of the last 60 h → `gainedBetween` / `perHour`), a live line in the hero and a "The last 48 hours" chart. (6) **TikTok's morning copy** reads every video (35 s budget; `total_views` only from a complete read) and writes yesterday's `views` worked out per video (`tiktokDayViews`: videos in both copies, plus every view of the ones posted after yesterday's copy; a video found for the first time adds nothing); Analytics, objectives (`dailyValue`), projections and Advanced prefer that `views` and fall back to the change in `total_views`. `getViewsDay` pages the snapshots (Supabase hands out 1,000 rows at a time). (7) **Sign-in refreshes**: `ProviderError` carries the HTTP status; a timeout, network error, 429 or 5xx while refreshing no longer marks the account `needs_reconnect` (logged to the server only). (8) Sync now also runs a full live pass after answering (`after()`, with the time left of its 55 s). (9) `StackedColumns` takes `tips` (tooltip titles) and lets its last label overflow. (10) The dashboard's Calendar widget opens `/calendar?d=<day>&open=1`: the Calendar page (`openDay` prop of `CalendarView`) opens on that month with the day's window up, then drops `open` from the address. (11) Alert emails: one email to `alertsEmail()` (`ALERTS_EMAIL` → `NEXT_PUBLIC_CONTACT_EMAIL` → hello@cliptroop.com) for `reportError`, `reportFixed`, the developer page's test alert and reports; developer accounts keep the notifications; Developer → Status / Overview say where each goes; /setup lists `ALERTS_EMAIL` and `LIVE_MINUTES`. Mock: `live.cjs` (counts around the real now, the two RPCs), `MOCK_LIVE=0`, `MOCK_LIVE_FRESH=1`, `MOCK_NO_REACH=1`; YouTube rows get impressions and CTR.

Analytics: YouTube handles no longer get a second "@" (`getPlatformStatus`, `lib/social/tokens.ts`); the replaced-account note shows for 7 days after the switch and can be closed (`vp-account-notes-closed`).
- **1.18.0** (Oct 13 2026): **Win streak, Objectives as a table.** (no migration) (1) **Win streak** (`modules/objectives/lib/streak.ts`, pure, tests/streak.test.ts): a win = any `objective_periods` row with `reached_at`, on the day it was reached kept inside its period (`winDay()`); counted in the shortest period the active goals use (`streakUnit()`: day, else week…) from the first goal of that length; a finished period without a win breaks it, the current one adds once won, a period every goal of that length was switched off for (target 0 in `objective_targets`) neither breaks nor counts; `best`, `bestBefore` ("New best!"), `sinceLast` (days since the last win, any goal), `wins30`, `slots` (this week's days Monday to Sunday, or the last 7 weeks / 6 months / 4 quarters / 3 years). Loaded by `getWinStreak()` (board.ts; the page's board with `streak: true`), the `loadWinStreak()` action + `useLiveStreak()` (realtime on objective_periods / objectives / objective_targets). Pieces in `components/streak.tsx` (StreakFlame (its own orange in every theme, flickers), StreakStrip, SinceLast, StreakSummary). **Dashboard widget "Win streak"** (`streak` in `layout.ts`, `streak-widget.tsx`; 3×3, adapts from 2×2 to 6×5; "New" badge moved from Objectives to it). **The streak's card** in `ObjectiveCelebrations`: after a win's card, once per period and device (`vp-streak-cheers`), the flame bursts in, the count goes up by one, the strip, "Streak started!" / "New best streak!", orange confetti. (2) **Objectives page**: a simpler overview (reached now with the rings and each cadence's count as filter chips, "N behind"; the win streak; days since the last win, which goal, wins in 30 days, "Put the streak on my dashboard") and **Table / Cards** (`components/ui/table-bits.tsx`: `useStoredView` (`vp-objectives-layout`, default table), `LayoutSwitch`, `SortTh`, `nextSort`); `objective-table.tsx` (Objective, Progress with the pace mark, Status, Time left (lg), In a row and Reached (xl), Who helped (2xl); sortable; a row opens the goal; phones get a list). (3) **Projections**: Table / Cards (`vp-projections-layout`), `projection-table.tsx` (Projection, Now with % and the bar, Status, At this pace (`headingShort()`), Deadline, Compare). (4) **Loading screens**: the outer `loading.tsx` of shorts, shorts/[id], videos, videos/[id], analytics and meetings are client components that show the inner page's skeleton for deeper paths (a task → a script no longer flashes the shorts list). (5) A short's Activity shows ClipLogo for the app's own actions (`ClipLogo` takes an `id` for its clip paths). (6) The month calendar's day card (dashboard Calendar widget, date pickers): posted videos with a tick at the end, not crossed out. (7) `InstagramIcon` gets a `useId()` gradient (a shared id left logos colourless after a hidden phone-only list). Mock: `MOCK_LAYOUT=streak` (+ `MOCK_STREAK_SIZE=4x3`), `MOCK_NO_DAILY=1` (pauses the daily goal: a weekly streak), posted shorts have an app-made "posted" event.
- **1.17.0** (Oct 13 2026): **Every video's own analytics.** (0082) (1) **A video's page** `/analytics/video/<platform>/<id>` (`?tab=advanced`, `?from=content|advanced|audience|dashboard`): `modules/analytics/lib/video.ts` `getVideoAnalytics()` → keys (each number next to the median of the up to 30 of the same kind before it, its rank among them), daily (YouTube's per-day numbers, or the copies' running totals as day changes), growth (`cumulativeByAge` + `typicalCurve` for the first 60 days, drawn when it overlaps 3+ days; `versusTypical` for "ahead / behind since day N"), milestones (by day 1, 2, 7, 14, 30, 60), retention next to the average of its kind, traffic sources and search terms (`getVideoSplits`), the same short / long video on the other platforms, typical RPM; `historyToFill()` + `fillVideoHistory()` (action) + `fetchYouTubeVideoHistory()` (sync.ts) ask YouTube for a video's whole day-by-day history when its start is missing (≤ 9 videos, 3 at a time, 9 s budget; at most every 30 min per video and server). Screens `modules/analytics/components/video/video-analytics.tsx` (hero with the verdict: "3.4×", "Under your usual"; Overview: key numbers with "2.1× typical" / "+6 pts" chips, growth vs typical, views per day, retention vs average (dashed grey), splits, the same short everywhere; Advanced: every number with typical and rank, everything else the platform shares, money, full splits, a day-by-day table) + `loading.tsx`. (2) **× typical** (`lib/scores.ts`, pure, tests/scores.test.ts): `outlierScores`, `scoreText`, `scoreTone` (≥ 3 high, ≥ 1.4 up, > 0.7 usual, else down), `rankOf`, `median`, growth helpers; `AdvVideo` gains `day` (posted day in the team's time zone), `score`, `typical` (`withScores`), the mapper moved to `videoMapper()` (shared with the video page). (3) **Content tab** `components/content-list.tsx`: search across all time (`searchVideos` → `findVideos`: platform title, ClipTroop title, #number; `data-page-search` so "/" lands there), ↑ ↓ + Enter opens the analytics, Shorts / Long, sortable columns (Published, Views, × typical, Likes, Comments, Shares), table / grid (kept per device), Analytics + open-on-platform buttons; `ContentItem` gains `score`, `typical`. Loaded with `next/dynamic` from `analytics-view.tsx`; CSV export covers it. (2) **The sync**, split: `sync-core.ts` (fetch helpers, `ytFirst` (the first metric set YouTube accepts), `upsert` / `upsertSome`, `keepStoredMetrics`, `inBatches`), `sync-advanced.ts`: YouTube per-day splits (traffic source, device, OS, playback location, subscribed or not, YouTube product), window snapshots 7 / 28 / 90 / 365 fetched again after 20 h (age × gender, sharing services, traffic by content type, search terms, outside sites, suggesting videos and playlists with their titles), each video's retention + traffic sources + search terms (6 per run: new ones every 2 days for their first month, then every 2 weeks), lifetime per-video extras into `metrics`, per-video revenue / CPM, daily CPM / ad impressions / monetized playbacks, Shorts vs long averages; Instagram 7 / 28-day splits (viewer type, content type) and follower / engaged-audience demographics (age, gender, country, city); Facebook Reels total plays, replays and `post_video_retention_graph`. The extras stop at a 30 s budget (`deadline`) and carry on next run. `retention.ts` (pure, tests/retention.test.ts): YouTube and Facebook curves, averages, summaries (after 3 s for Shorts / 30 s for long videos, the half, the end, the biggest drop, a rewatch peak, vs similar videos). TikTok's Display API gives only views / likes / comments / shares per video; its Business API would add watch time, retention, traffic and audience (a separate app and review, not requested). (3) **What works** (`lib/patterns.ts`, pure, tests/patterns.test.ts): median views per group for length, posting hour, weekend, weekday, title (question, number, capitals, emoji, length), hook (question, number, length), short type, held past 3 s / 30 s, % watched, skip rate; momentum; best vs the rest (`topVsRest`); platforms side by side (`crossPlatform`). Groups of 3+, 6+ videos at least a week old; "strong" when both groups have 6+ and one is ×1.5. Hooks from the video's own script (`lib/hooks.ts`, tests/hooks.test.ts: the Hook part, else the first two sentences; the staging doc, then review, then write). (4) **Download for Claude** (`modules/analytics/export/`): `claude-export.tsx` (platforms, range, all / most viewed 25-50-100 / posted in the range, whole scripts, revenue (only for `can_view_revenue`), Text + PDF / Text / PDF, the request to paste into Claude), `build-text.ts` (plain text with a glossary), `build-pdf.ts` (pdf-lib + fontkit loaded from `/vendor`; Inter embedded whole and Fraunces subset from `public/fonts/pdf/` (OFL); thumbnails through `/api/analytics/thumb` (signed in; i.ytimg / ggpht / googleusercontent / cdninstagram / fbcdn / tiktokcdn / byteimg / muscdn hosts only; YouTube tries oardefault then maxresdefault; images ≤ 6 MB; private cache); images re-encoded to JPEG on a canvas; frame strips; retention curves; 95 pages at most (Claude reads PDFs under 100 pages)). Data: `gatherClaudeExport()` (`lib/claude-export.ts`) through `loadClaudeExport()` (team members; the range ends yesterday; frames as 1-hour signed URLs). (5) **Opening frames**: `modules/review/lib/frames.ts` (8 frames: 0, 1, 2, 3 s, 25 / 50 / 75 % and the end, one JPEG strip with the times) captured while a short's version uploads (`uploader.tsx`), stored in `video-frames`, recorded with `saveVersionFrames()` → `set_version_frames`; versions uploaded before 1.16.0 have none. (6) **Rounded squares**: every `rounded-full` in app / components / modules reviewed (96 kept: avatars, spinners, rings, big circles; the rest `rounded-md` / `rounded-lg` / `rounded-[2px]`–`[4px]`), the live dot's ping too. (7) **Scroll lock** (`lib/hooks/use-scroll-lock.ts`: counted `lockScroll` / `useScrollLock`, `useLatest`, `releaseStrayScrollLock` on every navigation) in Dialog, both lightboxes, the search palette, the tour, the sketch studio, the review workspace and the phone menu sheet: closing popups in any order gives the page its scrollbar back. (8) **Popup forms fill once per opening** (`lib/hooks/use-fill-on-open.ts`): the projection, objective, schedule and meeting editors; a `?p=` link opens its projection once. (9) **Objectives and Projections pages** redone (`components/ui/segmented.tsx` for their tabs and filters; an overview with one segment row per cadence; the ones needing a push first; an auto-fill card grid; phone headers with New and Manage), **New objective** on the Objectives page (`modules/objectives/components/new-objective.tsx`, shared with the dashboard widget), **Team → Projections** (`app/(dashboard)/team/projections-settings.tsx`: masters edit / archive / bring back / delete, live), archiving a projection asks first. (10) Analytics → Content links to Advanced; the Analytics loading screen shows 5 tabs. (11) `Dialog` focuses its first control with `preventScroll`, so a long window (What's new) opens at its top. "subscribers" when YouTube only), followers_net, followers_per_1k, total_views/likes, views, views_per_day, engaged_views, engaged_rate, reach, watch_hours, avg_view_duration, avg_view_pct, likes, comments, shares, saves, engagement/like/comment/dislike rates, videos_posted, avg/median views per video, top_video_views, hit_rate (share of videos past a views mark), per video likes / like rate / shares / watch / % viewed, skip_rate (direction down), subs_per_video, engaged_per_video, revenue, revenue_per_day, rpm (money: `can_view_revenue`), shorts_per_week, longs_per_month; WINDOWS 7/28/90; `cleanScope` (platforms, window, kind shorts|long, threshold), `describeProjection` ("Reach 200K subscribers on YouTube by Dec 9, 2026."), `formatValue`, `unitWords`, DEADLINE_PRESETS, COMPARE_SET / COMPARE_MONEY); `record.ts` (`recordProjections(team)`: today's point for every running projection (upsert), reached and ended recorded once each (`.is(null)` guards), notifications `projection_reached` (everyone; a money one to masters + revenue_access) and `projection_ended` (masters + its creator), `queueRecordIfDue` when the page opens before the morning job ran). (2) **Only the connected accounts' numbers** (0079): `modules/analytics/lib/account-rules.ts` (pure: `sameAccount`, `visibleRows`, `inAccountTime`; tests/connected-numbers.test.ts) + `accounts.ts` (NUMBER_TABLES, `purgeNumbers`, `adoptAccount` (OAuth callback and `chooseFacebookPage`), `hadOtherAccount`, `numbersVisibility` (React cache)); the sync adopts before copying (another account → purge + full backfill); every reader filters (Analytics tabs, dashboard widgets through `sharedLookups`, objectives' sources, projections' data); Content's "ours" rows only since account_since, platform links through `safeUrl`; `AccountNote` on Audience and Content for a switch in the last 180 days; the disconnect dialog says the numbers leave and come back with the same account. (3) **More numbers** (0079): YouTube daily engagedViews / averageViewPercentage / dislikes / videosAddedToPlaylists (a second, optional metric set), per-video lifetime stats (`ytVideoStats`), per-day per-video reports (`syncYouTubeVideoDays`, 4 at a time, stops after 3 failures) into analytics_content_days; Instagram Reels average watch time, skip rate, reposts; morning snapshots of running totals for Instagram / TikTok / Facebook. YouTube's API has no Shorts "swiped away" number: engaged views / engaged view rate is the closest. (4) **Views per day → that day's videos**: LineChart `onPick` (click, Enter or Space), `modules/analytics/components/day-videos.tsx` (platform cards with their share of the day, Shorts / long chips, sort by views or newest, ←/→ to the day before or after, a per-day cache, notes when a platform's split isn't known), `getViewsDay` + `loadViewsDay` (asks YouTube for a day the copies missed: at most every 10 minutes per team and day, up to 3 years back). (5) **Objectives**: periods before a goal was set aren't judged (`firstCounted`, `statsOf`; statuses `before` and `nodata`; muted history columns with a "Goal set" line; the detail table folds them), 24 colours, New objective button on the dashboard widget. (6) **Daily word**: one RPC per guess, the tiles start flipping at once (the word list is checked in the browser). (7) **Themes** Ink Orange / Ink Grape / Ink Orchid / Ink Cherry (`inkorange`, `inkgrape`, `inkorchid`, `inkcherry`: 24 themes). (8) **Developer**: Usage fills the space under Uploads per day (where storage is heading, busiest days, room left, "≈ N days at this pace"); Status shows the 3 latest incidents, the rest folded. (9) **Script comments** were always per document; the panel now names its document, and comments on words rewritten since sit in "On text that changed since" with Resolve all (`resolveComments`). (10) Fixed "Start your first team" on a first open (loading the teams is retried, a failure is never read as no teams). (11) **Performance and hardening**: dashboard widgets load with `next/dynamic` (+ `PRELOAD` while the page hydrates, everything on Customize) inside `WidgetBoundary` (one widget failing shows Try again / Reload in its own box): first load JS 270 → 130 kB; tus-js-client is imported on the first upload (short page 243 → 229 kB, Settings 241 → 227 kB); the Objectives / Projections views and the Team tabs load only their own code (`objectives/views.tsx`, `team/lazy-tabs.tsx`); `getPlatformStatus` and `numbersVisibility` cached per request; `MAP_MODES` and `CONTRIB_COLORS` moved out of the drawing code; `poweredByHeader: false`. each with label/hint/verb/noun/unit/which filters it takes/needs/icon/suggested targets; `cleanFilters` (platforms, only = planned for nowhere else, shortTypes, longTypes, member, content Shorts|long for YouTube views), `describe` ("Post 3 reels only on Instagram every week", "For maria: edit 6 shorts every week"), `suggestTitle`, TEMPLATES (8 quick starts), OBJECTIVE_COLORS (8, validated light/dark: --obj-* tokens in globals.css), formatAmount/unitFor); `sources.ts` (server-only: loads only what the goals need for one window, all pages of every query, 100-id chunks); `board.ts` (`getObjectivesBoard(db, team, {history: full|short|none, items})` → every objective's current period, history, stats, overrides, the wins of the last 90 days, people, audienceReady; - **1.12.0** (Oct 9 2026): **Facebook Page posting (Reels), separate from Instagram everywhere; video preview on the Posting card; phone calendar weekend tap.** Why: Instagram does NOT share posts made through its API to Facebook, so the old "Instagram published → Facebook marked posted" was wrong. (1) `lib/social/publishers/facebook.ts` (Reels Publishing API with the Page token = `social_accounts.access_token`, Page id = `external_id`): `start` (`POST /{page}/video_reels upload_phase=start` → video_id; refuses < 3 or > 90 s first) → `upload` (rupload `file_url` = 2 h signed link; if Facebook refuses it, or hasn't fetched it after 5 min, we stream the file ourselves: `offset` = Facebook's `bytes_transfered`, `file_size`, octet-stream, `duplex: "half"`, cut 12 s before the run's deadline and resumed next run) → `uploaded` (poll `fields=status` until `uploading_phase` complete; processing errors are final) → `finish` (`upload_phase=finish`, `video_state=PUBLISHED`, `description` = caption ≤ 5000) → `check` (poll until `publishing_phase` complete) → published with `permalink_url` (made absolute) or `https://www.facebook.com/reel/{id}`. Friendly errors: (#200)/permission → reconnect and allow managing posts; (#190) → sign-in stopped working; (#4/17/32/613) → 30-a-day limit, retried. Tested against a fake Graph/rupload + the dev mock (hosted, refused → streamed with a cut at 100 KB and resumed from the offset, request timeout, processing error, permission, limit, 120 s video, no time left). (2) Worker: `facebookStep` dispatch, NAME facebook; the Instagram → Facebook auto-mark in `markPosted` is GONE. (3) providers.ts: `FACEBOOK_POST_SCOPE = "pages_manage_posts"` added to FB_SCOPES (only used without FACEBOOK_LOGIN_CONFIG_ID: with Login for Business the configuration must have it ticked); NOT required to connect (a Page connected for Analytics keeps working): Team → Connected accounts shows "Reconnect once and allow managing posts" (`missingPermission`), the Posting card shows "Connected for Analytics only…" + Mark as posted, the Posting page says "connected for Analytics only", and `schedulePosts` / `checkBeforeScheduling` refuse without it. `POSTING_PLATFORMS` has facebook. (4) schedule-actions.ts: `ScheduleEntry` facebook `{caption}`; checks: scope, 3–90 s, ≤ 30 Facebook posts within ±24 h of the time on that account, live check `GET /{page}?fields=id,name` with the Page token ("Posting to <Page> as a public Reel"). (5) schedule-panel.tsx: Facebook is its own card (caption, time, a "Who can see this Reel" select with only Public, as Meta asks; status steps "Sent to Facebook at …" → "Facebook processes it" → Published; "Open Reel"); `hasFacebook` / `withFacebook` / `facebookPosted` removed; Post all at the same time covers four. (6) `post-video-preview.tsx`: the version that gets posted (approved_version_id, else the latest; read on the short page) as a still (`#t=0.1`) + Preview dialog player via `getPlaybackUrl` (TikTok's audit asks for a preview on the posting screen). (7) Copy: landing, tutorial, brand description, README, privacy (LEGAL_UPDATED Oct 9) now say Facebook too; notification bell + Posting page know Facebook. (8) Calendar on phones: a tapped weekend cell's `bg-surface-2/20` beat `bg-amber/10` → `max-sm:!bg-amber/10`. Dev mock: `MOCK_VIDEO=<file>` plays a real file for every review-videos link (use .webm in Playwright: its Chromium has no H.264), `MOCK_FB_ANALYTICS_ONLY=1`, sample shorts planned for Facebook, a Facebook post uploading on #234 with MOCK_STATUS_BAD. Migration 0071. **Meta setup (owner)**: add `pages_manage_posts` to the app (Use cases → Facebook Login for Business → Permissions) and tick it in the Login for Business configuration (`FACEBOOK_LOGIN_CONFIG_ID`), then reconnect Facebook in Team → Connected accounts; App Review video must show posting a Reel.
- **1.11.1** (Oct 9 2026): **Instagram's repeated sign-in.** After a successful connect the owner saw "Instagram sign-in expired or came back to a different copy" (Instagram can deliver the same callback twice; the repeat found its one-time `oauth_states` row already used). `app/api/social/[platform]/callback/route.ts`: when the state is gone, `justConnected()` checks whether this signed-in person connected that platform in the last 2 minutes (`social_accounts.connected_by` + `connected_at`, status active) and answers ok instead of "expired". `connected-accounts.tsx`: an "expired" result waits 2.5 s and is dropped when the same platform succeeded within 60 s (either order); other platforms' errors still show. Tested on the preview (repeat → ok in the popup and in the tab; an old connection → still "expired") and in the browser (success then expired, expired then success, expired alone, another platform). No migration. Also: the review-video scripts live in the owner's doc "ClipTroop review videos" (Instagram, Facebook Page, YouTube, TikTok); TikTok's posting screen still needs a video preview inside the Posting card before its audit video (done in 1.12.0).

- **1.9.5** (Oct 7 2026): **Instant feedback + Clip loading screen**, plus the robots fix. (1) `app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx` is now a sync layout that wraps an async `DashboardShell` (the old layout body) in `<Suspense fallback={<AppSplash />}>`: the splash (`components/ui/app-splash.tsx`, Clip clapping in a loop, rotating lines, sliding bar; plain HTML + CSS, styles "App splash" in globals.css, fades in after 180 ms so quick loads never flash it) streams in the first bytes while profile/teams/notifications load (first load, installed app start, coming from /login). Checked with curl chunk timing: splash in the first chunk, shell after. (2) `components/ui/nav-progress.tsx` (`NavProgress`, mounted in the shell): thin amber bar; starts on a capture-phase click on an internal link (`internalTarget()`), on `popstate`, and on the `cliptroop:nav-start` event from `lib/nav-events.ts` `startNavProgress(href?)` (skips same pathname+search), which `instrumentation-client.ts` fires for EVERY Next navigation via `onRouterTransitionStart`; finishes when pathname/search change (12 s safety). While loading it **nudges React every 300 ms** (tiny setState) until the URL changed and no `[aria-busy="true"]` is left (max 15 s): found with the mock (`MOCK_DELAY=1200`) that ~30–40 % of same-page step moves on /shorts/[id] and /videos/[id] (also in 1.9.4, also with `experimental.clientSegmentCache`) never committed although the RSC response had fully arrived (router queue fine, server-patch applied; a lost Suspense ping) until any other state update happened; React's markRootUpdated retries suspended transitions on any update, so the nudge makes every move finish (14/14 and 10/10 runs after). (3) `components/ui/pending-nav.tsx`: `<PendingNav>` (useTransition + router.push, `pending` key), `<PendingLink navKey>` (falls back to a plain Link outside a provider), `<PendingSwap fallback | fallbacks>` (the changing part shows the target's skeleton while pending). Used by: the short's StageTracker + "Back to current" (`?view=`, `shorts/[id]/step-skeleton.tsx` per stage), the long video's LongStepBar + "Go to …" (`?tab=`, `videos/[id]/step-skeleton.tsx` per step, master move buttons), Settings tabs (`TabNav` is now a client component; `settings/skeletons.tsx`: SETTINGS_TABS, settingsTab(), SettingsTabSkeleton per tab), Team tabs (`team/skeletons.tsx`: TEAM_TABS, teamTab(), TeamTabSkeleton per tab; TeamTabs client), and the script workspace (wrapped in PendingNav; DocRail / FlowStrip / phone docs menu light up at once and `ScriptEditor switching` shows `PaperLines` in the page while the editor stays mounted, hidden). (4) Settings and Team `loading.tsx` are client components that read `?tab=` (useSearchParams) and show that tab's skeleton with it underlined (checked: opening /settings?tab=preferences or /team?tab=accounts from elsewhere). "Notifications & app" was missing from the Settings skeleton tabs; `InstallApp` / `PushSettings` show `InstallAppSkeleton` / `PushSettingsSkeleton` (components/pwa.tsx) instead of a blank box while the device is checked. New loading screens: /setup, /status. `SkeletonTabs` takes `active`. (5) `startNavProgress(url)` also in calendar/analytics range moves, global search and the bell. (6) Service worker `2026-10-07.1`: navigation preload (the page request starts while the worker wakes up). (7) The robots fix: robots.txt let nothing in (`Disallow: /`), so platform checkers that respect it couldn't read the legal pages (Meta: "Data deletion instructions URL should represent a valid URL"). Now `app/robots.ts` allows `/$`, `/privacy`, `/terms`, `/data-deletion` and disallows the rest, with `sitemap` = siteUrl()/sitemap.xml; `app/sitemap.ts` lists those 4 (`/sitemap.xml` is a public route in middleware); shared `lib/public-pages.ts` (PUBLIC_PAGES, siteUrl = NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL || NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || app.APP_DOMAIN). Those 4 pages override the root layout's noindex with `robots: { index: true, follow: true }`. Tests `tests/robots.test.ts`, `tests/nav-feedback.test.ts`. dev-mock: `MOCK_DELAY=<ms>` makes every answer slow (to see loading screens). No migration.

## Roadmap (agreed order; details and questions in the planning reply of Oct 2 2026)
1. ✅ **1.2 Scripting** (done in 1.2.0): @mentions in script comments and editing ideas (notifications), draft safety (placeholder highlight while writing, confirm before discarding, local draft backup), Sketch Studio for editing ideas (Miro-like tools, confirm on exit, preview + full view, phone friendly), drawings in Word/PDF exports under their idea.
2. ✅ **1.3 Navigation + Meetings** (done in 1.3.0): grouped sidebar + phone bottom bar with a More sheet; /meetings (masters + schedulers schedule; everyone RSVPs; agenda, notes, action items; reminders 3 days / 1 day / 1 hour in-app + email via the pg_cron timer); Next meeting widget; meetings in the calendar in their own colour; .ics / Add to Google Calendar.
3. ✅ **1.4 Analytics** (done in 1.4.0; platform numbers need each account reconnected, and Instagram/TikTok stats need their permissions turned on in the developer apps first): A) production analytics from our own data (no approvals needed); B) platform analytics (YouTube Analytics incl. countries for the world map, Instagram insights, TikTok video stats) with daily snapshots, tabs, date ranges, comparisons, CSV. Analytics scopes must be final BEFORE app reviews.
4. ✅ **1.5 Fixes + analytics widgets + calmer UI** (done in 1.5.0; owner asked for it Oct 3 2026).
5. ✅ **1.6 Themes** (done in 1.6.0, together with Facebook analytics and the Audience filter). Still to decide the app name: "Clippy" is a Microsoft trademark (fine for the internal mascot, risky as a public product name); free domains found Oct 3 2026: clapdeck.app / clapdeck.studio / getclapdeck.com, clapperly.app / getclapperly.com, clapstack.app, clipnest.app, takeplan.app.
6. ✅ **1.7 Revenue streams + Studio tidy-up** (done in 1.7.0; owner asked for it Oct 3 2026), then **1.7.5** (script hand-offs, globe, currencies, themes). Hardening moves to 1.8, and the steps below shift by one.
7. ✅ **1.8 Hardening** (done in 1.8.0): Database Action (Supabase CLI) + CI on every push, error alerts without an outside service (Sentry wasn't installable here; the app records and alerts itself), /status + /api/health, error screens, performance pass, tasks + notifications for every step incl. meeting action items, one account = one team, analytics catch-up, globe zoom. Still open from the old 1.8 list: generated database types (`npx supabase gen types typescript --db-url … > types/database.ts`, then fix what it finds), RLS tests in CI (started in 1.14.0: `supabase/tests/*.test.sql`; add one per older feature), mobile patches.
8. **1.9 Approvals first**. 1.9.0 done (Oct 4 2026): installable app + push, public pages, brand constants. 1.9.1 done (Oct 4 2026): Staging done, invite onboarding, App setup page (/setup), log out confirmation, ready for the own domain (old addresses redirect once the env is set); the rename waits for the owner to buy a .com with cPanel hosting (see the backlog and "Domain move"). The owner's domain rules changed on Oct 4: .com + cPanel, no GoDaddy, so the .app plan below is superseded. Next: owner picks the name (Oct 3 shortlist with free .app domains: Clapdeck, Clapwise, Clapcrew, Takeplan, Slateflow, Clapstack; most .com are taken, so production = name.app, staging = staging.name.app, one domain to buy, ~$8 first year / ~$14–15 renewal at Cloudflare or Porkbun) → 1.9.1 rename + domain switch (brand.ts, NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL, OAuth return addresses in Google/Meta/TikTok, Supabase Auth site URL + redirect URLs, Resend sender, Vercel domains incl. staging branch domain) → demo-video scripts + form texts → submit. (Owner, Oct 3 2026: don't pay for Supabase Pro while waiting; launch after the approvals). Order: pick the name → buy the domain (cheap; Google's verification wants the home page + privacy policy on a domain you own, and changing name / logo / domain after approval means re-verifying) → privacy policy, terms, data deletion page on it → submit Google OAuth verification + YouTube API audit, Meta App Review (screencasts), TikTok Content Posting audit (demo video) → while they're reviewed: push notifications (web push / PWA) and the rest of 1.9. No Supabase Pro until the approvals are through.
9. **1.12 Production cut-over** (after approvals; was 1.10, then 1.11: 1.10.0 became the home page + Clip's tour and 1.11.0 notification history + storage clean-up, Oct 8 2026): Supabase Pro on production only, fresh production secrets, backups, production timers/secrets, private test post per platform, then public.
10. **2.0 Launch**, then the ongoing version backlog.
- Owner's answers (Oct 2 2026): meetings are held wherever (mostly Discord), about 2 a month, planned ahead; reminders 3 days / 1 day / 1 hour. The channel is monetized: revenue is visible to the master only by default, the master can grant it to specific people. /server-status is visible to everyone; alert emails go to the master only. Production domain: vivplanner.vercel.app or a bought app.vplanner.com (maybe a rebrand), always on Vercel. Meta app: personal (no company yet), so stay on Instagram Login with the team's accounts added as app testers instead of business verification for now. YouTube / TikTok approvals: to be decided together.
- Start the slow paperwork early, in parallel: Meta business verification, Google domain verification, TikTok app details.
